name: Build APK

on:
  push:
    branches: [main]
  pull_request:
    branches: [main]
  workflow_dispatch:
    inputs:
      validate_signing:
        description: "Build and verify a signed APK as a workflow artifact without publishing a release"
        type: boolean
        required: true
        default: false
      publish_release:
        description: "Build, sign, and publish a production GitHub Release"
        type: boolean
        required: true
        default: false

permissions:
  contents: read

jobs:
  build:
    permissions:
      contents: read
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:

      - run: python3 -m pip install pillow

          node --check sw.js
          node --test tests/*.test.js

      # Do not produce a downloadable APK unless the same commit passes the full
      # browser, media, offline-runtime, and real-export QA suite.

        run: |
          sudo apt-get update
          sudo apt-get install -y ffmpeg
          python -m pip install playwright
          python -m playwright install --with-deps chromium

      - name: Fetch and verify the bundled speech runtime

      - name: Run browser smoke tests

      - name: Run device-encoder FFmpeg integration

      - name: Run FFmpeg worker integration
        run: python test_ffmpeg_worker.py

      - name: Verify speech runtime works offline
        run: python tests/offline_speech_runtime.py

      - name: Run real FFmpeg.wasm export integration
        run: python tests/export_integration.py

      - run: python3 make_icons.py before

      - run: |
          mkdir -p www
          cp index.html style.css mediay-icons.js mediay-core.js mediay-fast.js app.js setup.js setup.css manifest.json sw.js legal.html favicon.svg icon-*.png www/
          mkdir -p www/vendor/ffmpeg
          cp vendor/ffmpeg/ffmpeg.js vendor/ffmpeg/814.ffmpeg.js vendor/ffmpeg/LICENSE www/vendor/ffmpeg/

      - run: |

          cat > "$APP_SRC/MediaYExportService.java" <<'JAVA'
          package com.projectmediay.app;

          import android.app.Notification;
          import android.app.NotificationChannel;
          import android.app.NotificationManager;
          import android.app.PendingIntent;
          import android.app.Service;
          import android.content.Context;
          import android.content.Intent;
          import android.content.pm.ServiceInfo;
          import android.os.Build;
          import android.os.Handler;
          import android.os.IBinder;
          import android.os.Looper;
          import android.os.PowerManager;

          public final class MediaYExportService extends Service {
              public static final String ACTION_START = "com.projectmediay.app.EXPORT_START";
              private static final String CHANNEL_ID = "mediay-export";
              private static final int NOTIFICATION_ID = 26024;
              // Safety net: if the web app never reports that the job ended, stop by itself instead of
              // holding the CPU awake and showing a stale notification forever.
              private static final long MAX_RUN_MS = 3L * 60L * 60L * 1000L;

              private PowerManager.WakeLock wakeLock;
              private final Handler handler = new Handler(Looper.getMainLooper());
              private final Runnable stopWhenExpired = new Runnable() {
                  @Override
                  public void run() {
                      stopSelf();
                  }
              };

              @Override
              public void onCreate() {
                  super.onCreate();
                  if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
                      NotificationChannel channel = new NotificationChannel(
                          CHANNEL_ID, "MediaY export", NotificationManager.IMPORTANCE_LOW);
                      channel.setDescription("Shows when MediaY is rendering a video.");
                      NotificationManager manager = getSystemService(NotificationManager.class);
                      if (manager != null) manager.createNotificationChannel(channel);
                  }
              }

              @Override
              public int onStartCommand(Intent intent, int flags, int startId) {
                  // A foreground service must call startForeground promptly after startForegroundService,
                  // so the notification is always built first.
                  Notification.Builder builder = Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O
                      ? new Notification.Builder(this, CHANNEL_ID)
                      : new Notification.Builder(this);
                  builder.setSmallIcon(getApplicationInfo().icon)
                      .setContentTitle("MediaY is working")
                      .setContentText("Rendering your video. Tap to return to MediaY.")
                      .setCategory(Notification.CATEGORY_PROGRESS)
                      .setPriority(Notification.PRIORITY_LOW)
                      .setOngoing(true)
                      .setOnlyAlertOnce(true)
                      .setProgress(0, 0, true);

                  Intent launchIntent = getPackageManager().getLaunchIntentForPackage(getPackageName());
                  if (launchIntent != null) {
                      int pendingFlags = PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT;
                      if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.M) {
                          pendingFlags |= PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE;
                      }
                      builder.setContentIntent(PendingIntent.getActivity(this, 0, launchIntent, pendingFlags));
                  }

                  Notification notification = builder.build();
                  if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.Q) {
                      startForeground(NOTIFICATION_ID, notification, ServiceInfo.FOREGROUND_SERVICE_TYPE_DATA_SYNC);
                  } else {
                      startForeground(NOTIFICATION_ID, notification);
                  }

                  // Without a wake lock Android may suspend the CPU as soon as the screen turns off,
                  // even while a foreground service is running.
                  if (wakeLock == null) {
                      PowerManager power = (PowerManager) getSystemService(Context.POWER_SERVICE);
                      if (power != null) {
                          wakeLock = power.newWakeLock(PowerManager.PARTIAL_WAKE_LOCK, "MediaY:export");
                          wakeLock.setReferenceCounted(false);
                          wakeLock.acquire(MAX_RUN_MS);
                      }
                  }
                  handler.removeCallbacks(stopWhenExpired);
                  handler.postDelayed(stopWhenExpired, MAX_RUN_MS);

                  // NOT_STICKY: if Android kills the process, do not resurrect an "export in progress"
                  // notification for an export that no longer exists.
                  return START_NOT_STICKY;
              }

              @Override
              public void onDestroy() {
                  handler.removeCallbacks(stopWhenExpired);
                  if (wakeLock != null && wakeLock.isHeld()) {
                      wakeLock.release();
                  }
                  wakeLock = null;
                  stopForeground(true);
                  super.onDestroy();
              }

              @Override
              public IBinder onBind(Intent intent) {
                  return null;
              }
          }
          JAVA

          cat > "$APP_SRC/MediaYExportServicePlugin.java" <<'JAVA'
          package com.projectmediay.app;

          import android.Manifest;
          import android.content.Intent;
          import android.content.pm.PackageManager;
          import android.os.Build;

          import com.getcapacitor.Plugin;
          import com.getcapacitor.PluginCall;
          import com.getcapacitor.PluginMethod;
          import com.getcapacitor.annotation.CapacitorPlugin;

          @CapacitorPlugin(name = "MediaYExportService")
          public class MediaYExportServicePlugin extends Plugin {
              @PluginMethod
              public void start(PluginCall call) {
                  try {
                      if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 33
                          && getContext().checkSelfPermission(Manifest.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS) != PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED
                          && getActivity() != null) {
                          getActivity().requestPermissions(new String[] { Manifest.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS }, 26024);
                      }
                      Intent intent = new Intent(getContext(), MediaYExportService.class);
                      intent.setAction(MediaYExportService.ACTION_START);
                      if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
                          getContext().startForegroundService(intent);
                      } else {
                          getContext().startService(intent);
                      }
                      call.resolve();
                  } catch (Exception error) {
                      call.reject("Could not start the Android export service.", error);
                  }
              }

              @PluginMethod
              public void stop(PluginCall call) {
                  try {
                      getContext().stopService(new Intent(getContext(), MediaYExportService.class));
                      call.resolve();
                  } catch (Exception error) {
                      call.reject("Could not stop the Android export service.", error);
                  }
              }
          }
          JAVA

          cat > "$APP_SRC/MediaYMediaStorePlugin.java" <<'JAVA'
          package com.projectmediay.app;

          import android.Manifest;
          import android.content.ContentResolver;
          import android.content.ContentValues;
          import android.media.MediaScannerConnection;
          import android.net.Uri;
          import android.os.Build;
          import android.os.Environment;
          import android.provider.MediaStore;

          import com.getcapacitor.JSObject;
          import com.getcapacitor.PermissionState;
          import com.getcapacitor.Plugin;
          import com.getcapacitor.PluginCall;
          import com.getcapacitor.PluginMethod;
          import com.getcapacitor.annotation.CapacitorPlugin;
          import com.getcapacitor.annotation.Permission;
          import com.getcapacitor.annotation.PermissionCallback;

          import java.io.BufferedInputStream;
          import java.io.BufferedOutputStream;
          import java.io.File;
          import java.io.FileInputStream;
          import java.io.FileOutputStream;
          import java.io.IOException;
          import java.io.InputStream;
          import java.io.OutputStream;

          @CapacitorPlugin(
              name = "MediaYMediaStore",
              permissions = {
                  @Permission(alias = "legacyStorage", strings = { Manifest.permission.WRITE_EXTERNAL_STORAGE })
              }
          )
          public final class MediaYMediaStorePlugin extends Plugin {
              private Uri streamingUri;
              private OutputStream streamingOutput;
              private String streamingFileName;
              private long streamingBytes;

              @PluginMethod
              public synchronized void beginStreamingSave(PluginCall call) {
                  if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT < Build.VERSION_CODES.Q) {
                      call.reject("Streaming MediaStore export requires Android 10 or newer.");
                      return;
                  }
                  String fileName = call.getString("fileName");
                  if (fileName == null || !fileName.matches("ProjectMedia-Y-[0-9]+\\.mp4")) {
                      call.reject("Invalid exported video filename.");
                      return;
                  }
                  abortStreamingSaveInternal();
                  ContentResolver resolver = getContext().getContentResolver();
                  try {
                      ContentValues values = new ContentValues();
                      values.put(MediaStore.Video.Media.DISPLAY_NAME, fileName);
                      values.put(MediaStore.Video.Media.MIME_TYPE, "video/mp4");
                      values.put(MediaStore.Video.Media.RELATIVE_PATH, Environment.DIRECTORY_MOVIES + "/MediaY");
                      values.put(MediaStore.Video.Media.IS_PENDING, 1);
                      Uri uri = resolver.insert(MediaStore.Video.Media.EXTERNAL_CONTENT_URI, values);
                      if (uri == null) throw new IOException("Android did not create a Movies/MediaY media entry.");
                      // Track the pending row before opening its stream so every failure path can delete it.
                      streamingUri = uri;
                      streamingFileName = fileName;
                      OutputStream output = resolver.openOutputStream(uri, "w");
                      if (output == null) {
                          throw new IOException("Android did not open the destination file.");
                      }
                      streamingOutput = new BufferedOutputStream(output, 256 * 1024);
                      streamingBytes = 0L;
                      JSObject result = new JSObject();
                      result.put("fileName", fileName);
                      result.put("location", "Movies/MediaY");
                      call.resolve(result);
                  } catch (Exception error) {
                      abortStreamingSaveInternal();
                      call.reject("Could not start streaming export: " + error.getMessage(), error);
                  }
              }

              @PluginMethod
              public synchronized void appendStreamingChunk(PluginCall call) {
                  String encoded = call.getString("data");
                  if (streamingOutput == null || encoded == null || encoded.isEmpty()) {
                      call.reject("No active streaming export or empty chunk.");
                      return;
                  }
                  try {
                      byte[] chunk = android.util.Base64.decode(encoded, android.util.Base64.DEFAULT);
                      if (chunk.length == 0 || chunk.length > 4 * 1024 * 1024) {
                          throw new IOException("Export chunk is outside the allowed size.");
                      }
                      streamingOutput.write(chunk);
                      streamingBytes += chunk.length;
                      JSObject result = new JSObject();
                      result.put("bytesWritten", chunk.length);
                      result.put("totalBytes", streamingBytes);
                      call.resolve(result);
                  } catch (Exception error) {
                      abortStreamingSaveInternal();
                      call.reject("Could not write export chunk: " + error.getMessage(), error);
                  }
              }

              @PluginMethod
              public synchronized void finishStreamingSave(PluginCall call) {
                  if (streamingOutput == null || streamingUri == null || streamingBytes <= 0L) {
                      abortStreamingSaveInternal();
                      call.reject("The streamed MP4 is empty or no export is active.");
                      return;
                  }
                  Uri uri = streamingUri;
                  String fileName = streamingFileName;
                  long bytes = streamingBytes;
                  try {
                      streamingOutput.flush();
                      streamingOutput.close();
                      streamingOutput = null;
                      ContentValues published = new ContentValues();
                      published.put(MediaStore.Video.Media.IS_PENDING, 0);
                      if (getContext().getContentResolver().update(uri, published, null, null) <= 0) {
                          throw new IOException("Android could not publish the exported video to Gallery.");
                      }
                      streamingUri = null;
                      streamingFileName = null;
                      streamingBytes = 0L;
                      JSObject result = new JSObject();
                      result.put("fileName", fileName);
                      result.put("location", "Movies/MediaY");
                      result.put("uri", uri.toString());
                      result.put("bytes", bytes);
                      call.resolve(result);
                  } catch (Exception error) {
                      abortStreamingSaveInternal();
                      call.reject("Could not finish streaming export: " + error.getMessage(), error);
                  }
              }

              @PluginMethod
              public synchronized void abortStreamingSave(PluginCall call) {
                  abortStreamingSaveInternal();
                  call.resolve();
              }

              private void abortStreamingSaveInternal() {
                  if (streamingOutput != null) {
                      try { streamingOutput.close(); } catch (Exception ignored) {}
                  }
                  streamingOutput = null;
                  if (streamingUri != null) {
                      try { getContext().getContentResolver().delete(streamingUri, null, null); } catch (Exception ignored) {}
                  }
                  streamingUri = null;
                  streamingFileName = null;
                  streamingBytes = 0L;
              }

              @PluginMethod
              public void saveVideo(PluginCall call) {
                  if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT < Build.VERSION_CODES.Q
                      && getPermissionState("legacyStorage") != PermissionState.GRANTED) {
                      requestPermissionForAlias("legacyStorage", call, "legacyStoragePermissionCallback");
                      return;
                  }
                  saveVideoInternal(call);
              }

              @PermissionCallback
              private void legacyStoragePermissionCallback(PluginCall call) {
                  if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT < Build.VERSION_CODES.Q
                      && getPermissionState("legacyStorage") != PermissionState.GRANTED) {
                      call.reject("Storage permission was denied. MediaY could not save the MP4.");
                      return;
                  }
                  saveVideoInternal(call);
              }

              private void saveVideoInternal(PluginCall call) {
                  String fileName = call.getString("fileName");
                  if (fileName == null || !fileName.matches("ProjectMedia-Y-[0-9]+\\.mp4")) {
                      call.reject("Invalid exported video filename.");
                      return;
                  }

                  File source = new File(getContext().getCacheDir(), fileName);
                  if (!source.isFile() || source.length() <= 0L) {
                      call.reject("The temporary exported MP4 is missing or empty.");
                      return;
                  }

                  ContentResolver resolver = getContext().getContentResolver();
                  Uri insertedUri = null;
                  File legacyFile = null;
                  try {
                      if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.Q) {
                          ContentValues values = new ContentValues();
                          values.put(MediaStore.Video.Media.DISPLAY_NAME, fileName);
                          values.put(MediaStore.Video.Media.MIME_TYPE, "video/mp4");
                          values.put(
                              MediaStore.Video.Media.RELATIVE_PATH,
                              Environment.DIRECTORY_MOVIES + "/MediaY"
                          );
                          values.put(MediaStore.Video.Media.IS_PENDING, 1);
                          insertedUri = resolver.insert(MediaStore.Video.Media.EXTERNAL_CONTENT_URI, values);
                          if (insertedUri == null) {
                              throw new IOException("Android did not create a Movies/MediaY media entry.");
                          }

                          try (InputStream input = new BufferedInputStream(new FileInputStream(source));
                               OutputStream output = resolver.openOutputStream(insertedUri, "w")) {
                              if (output == null) throw new IOException("Android did not open the destination file.");
                              copy(input, output);
                          }

                          ContentValues published = new ContentValues();
                          published.put(MediaStore.Video.Media.IS_PENDING, 0);
                          if (resolver.update(insertedUri, published, null, null) <= 0) {
                              throw new IOException("Android could not publish the exported video to Gallery.");
                          }
                      } else {
                          File movies = Environment.getExternalStoragePublicDirectory(Environment.DIRECTORY_MOVIES);
                          File folder = new File(movies, "MediaY");
                          if (!folder.isDirectory() && !folder.mkdirs() && !folder.isDirectory()) {
                              throw new IOException("Could not create the Movies/MediaY folder.");
                          }
                          legacyFile = new File(folder, fileName);
                          if (legacyFile.exists()) throw new IOException("A video with this name already exists.");
                          try (InputStream input = new BufferedInputStream(new FileInputStream(source));
                               OutputStream output = new BufferedOutputStream(new FileOutputStream(legacyFile))) {
                              copy(input, output);
                          }
                          MediaScannerConnection.scanFile(
                              getContext(),
                              new String[] { legacyFile.getAbsolutePath() },
                              new String[] { "video/mp4" },
                              null
                          );
                      }

                      JSObject result = new JSObject();
                      result.put("fileName", fileName);
                      result.put("location", "Movies/MediaY");
                      result.put("uri", insertedUri != null ? insertedUri.toString() : legacyFile.getAbsolutePath());
                      result.put("bytes", source.length());
                      call.resolve(result);
                  } catch (Exception error) {
                      if (insertedUri != null) {
                          try { resolver.delete(insertedUri, null, null); } catch (Exception ignored) {}
                      }
                      if (legacyFile != null && legacyFile.exists()) {
                          try { legacyFile.delete(); } catch (Exception ignored) {}
                      }
                      call.reject("Could not save the MP4 to Movies/MediaY: " + error.getMessage(), error);
                  }
              }

              private static void copy(InputStream input, OutputStream output) throws IOException {
                  byte[] buffer = new byte[256 * 1024];
                  int count;
                  while ((count = input.read(buffer)) != -1) {
                      output.write(buffer, 0, count);
                  }
                  output.flush();
              }
          }
          JAVA

          cat > "$APP_SRC/MainActivity.java" <<'JAVA'
          package com.projectmediay.app;

          import android.os.Bundle;
          import com.getcapacitor.BridgeActivity;

          public class MainActivity extends BridgeActivity {
              @Override
              public void onCreate(Bundle savedInstanceState) {
                  registerPlugin(MediaYExportServicePlugin.class);
                  registerPlugin(MediaYMediaStorePlugin.class);
                  super.onCreate(savedInstanceState);
              }
          }
          JAVA

          M="android/app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml"
          grep -q 'android.permission.FOREGROUND_SERVICE"' "$M" || sed -i 's#<application#<uses-permission android:name="android.permission.FOREGROUND_SERVICE" />\n    <uses-permission android:name="android.permission.FOREGROUND_SERVICE_DATA_SYNC" />\n    <uses-permission android:name="android.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS" />\n    <application#' "$M"
          grep -q 'android.permission.WAKE_LOCK"' "$M" || sed -i 's#<application#<uses-permission android:name="android.permission.WAKE_LOCK" />\n    <application#' "$M"
          grep -q 'android.permission.WRITE_EXTERNAL_STORAGE' "$M" || sed -i 's#<application#<uses-permission android:name="android.permission.WRITE_EXTERNAL_STORAGE" android:maxSdkVersion="28" />\n    <application#' "$M"
          grep -q 'MediaYExportService' "$M" || sed -i 's#</application>#        <service android:name=".MediaYExportService" android:exported="false" android:foregroundServiceType="dataSync" />\n    </application>#' "$M"

      - run: python3 make_icons.py after

      - name: Set unique Android version
        env:
          BUILD_NUMBER: ${{ github.run_number }}
        run: |
          python3 - <<'PY'
          import os
          import re
          from pathlib import Path

          gradle_file = Path("android/app/build.gradle")
          source = gradle_file.read_text()
          number = int(os.environ["BUILD_NUMBER"])
          if number < 1:
              raise SystemExit("GitHub run number must be positive")

          version_code, count_code = re.subn(
              r"(?m)^(\s*versionCode\s+)\d+\s*$",
              lambda match: match.group(1) + str(number),
              source,
              count=1,
          )
          version_name, count_name = re.subn(
              r"(?m)^(\s*versionName\s+)[\"'][^\"']*[\"']\s*$",
              lambda match: match.group(1) + f'"1.0.{number}"',
              version_code,
              count=1,
          )
          if count_code != 1 or count_name != 1:
              raise SystemExit("Could not set versionCode/versionName in generated android/app/build.gradle")
          gradle_file.write_text(version_name)
          print(f"Set Android versionName=1.0.{number}, versionCode={number}")
          PY

      - run: |
          SM="$ANDROID_HOME/cmdline-tools/latest/bin/sdkmanager"
          yes | "$SM" --licenses > /dev/null || true
          "$SM" "platforms;android-34" "build-tools;34.0.0" || true

      - name: Build debug APK
        run: |
          cd android
          chmod +x gradlew
          ./gradlew assembleDebug

      - name: Stage debug APK
        run: cp android/app/build/outputs/apk/debug/app-debug.apk MediaY-debug.apk

      - uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        with:
          name: MediaY-debug-apk
          path: MediaY-debug.apk
          if-no-files-found: error

      - name: Build and verify production-signed APK
        if: github.event_name == 'workflow_dispatch' && (inputs.validate_signing || inputs.publish_release)
        env:
          ANDROID_KEYSTORE_BASE64: ${{ secrets.ANDROID_KEYSTORE_BASE64 }}
          ANDROID_KEYSTORE_PASSWORD: ${{ secrets.ANDROID_KEYSTORE_PASSWORD }}
          ANDROID_KEY_ALIAS: ${{ secrets.ANDROID_KEY_ALIAS }}
          ANDROID_KEY_PASSWORD: ${{ secrets.ANDROID_KEY_PASSWORD }}
          ANDROID_KEYSTORE_PATH: ${{ runner.temp }}/mediay-release.jks
          BUILD_NUMBER: ${{ github.run_number }}
        run: |
          set -euo pipefail

          for secret_name in ANDROID_KEYSTORE_BASE64 ANDROID_KEYSTORE_PASSWORD ANDROID_KEY_ALIAS ANDROID_KEY_PASSWORD; do
            if [ -z "${!secret_name:-}" ]; then
              echo "::error::Missing required GitHub Actions secret: $secret_name"
              echo "Add all four Android signing secrets in Settings > Secrets and variables > Actions, then rerun the workflow."
              exit 1
            fi
          done

          printf '%s' "$ANDROID_KEYSTORE_BASE64" | base64 --decode > "$ANDROID_KEYSTORE_PATH"
          chmod 600 "$ANDROID_KEYSTORE_PATH"
          test -s "$ANDROID_KEYSTORE_PATH"

          cat > android/app/mediay-signing.gradle <<'GRADLE'
          android {
              signingConfigs {
                  release {
                      storeFile file(System.getenv("ANDROID_KEYSTORE_PATH"))
                      storePassword System.getenv("ANDROID_KEYSTORE_PASSWORD")
                      keyAlias System.getenv("ANDROID_KEY_ALIAS")
                      keyPassword System.getenv("ANDROID_KEY_PASSWORD")
                  }
              }
              buildTypes {
                  release {
                      signingConfig signingConfigs.release
                  }
              }
          }
          GRADLE
          printf "\napply from: 'mediay-signing.gradle'\n" >> android/app/build.gradle

          (
            cd android
            ./gradlew assembleRelease
          )

          RELEASE_APK="android/app/build/outputs/apk/release/app-release.apk"
          test -s "$RELEASE_APK"

          APKSIGNER="$ANDROID_HOME/build-tools/34.0.0/apksigner"
          test -x "$APKSIGNER"
          "$APKSIGNER" verify --verbose --print-certs "$RELEASE_APK"

          RELEASE_NAME="MediaY-v1.0.$BUILD_NUMBER.apk"
          cp "$RELEASE_APK" "$RELEASE_NAME"
          sha256sum "$RELEASE_NAME" > "$RELEASE_NAME.sha256"
          rm -f "$ANDROID_KEYSTORE_PATH"

          echo "Verified signed release APK: $RELEASE_NAME"
          echo "Android versionCode: $BUILD_NUMBER"

      - name: Upload signed APK validation artifact
        if: github.event_name == 'workflow_dispatch' && (inputs.validate_signing || inputs.publish_release)
        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        with:
          name: MediaY-signed-release-${{ github.run_number }}
          path: |
            MediaY-v1.0.*.apk
            MediaY-v1.0.*.apk.sha256
          if-no-files-found: error
          retention-days: 7

  publish-release:
    if: github.event_name == 'workflow_dispatch' && inputs.publish_release
    needs: build
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    permissions:
      contents: write
      actions: read
    steps:
      - name: Download verified signed APK
        uses: actions/download-artifact@v4
        with:
          name: MediaY-signed-release-${{ github.run_number }}
          path: release-assets

      - name: Publish GitHub Release
        uses: softprops/action-gh-release@v2
        with:
          tag_name: v1.0.${{ github.run_number }}
          name: MediaY v1.0.${{ github.run_number }}
          body: |
            Production-signed Android APK for MediaY.

            - Version: 1.0.${{ github.run_number }}
            - Android versionCode: ${{ github.run_number }}
            - APK signature verification ran in GitHub Actions before publishing.
            - SHA-256 checksum is included alongside the APK.
          files: |
            release-assets/MediaY-v1.0.${{ github.run_number }}.apk
            release-assets/MediaY-v1.0.${{ github.run_number }}.apk.sha256

# Instructions for AI coding agents

## Read first

- `docs/timeline-trim-architecture.md` — timeline timing model, trim invariants, and the intended transactional edit architecture.
- `docs/performance-triage.md` — what has been optimized, known latency limits, release gates, and the prioritized next-agent task list.

## Project constraints

MediaY is a browser-first video editor with a Capacitor Android build. Project media is stored locally in IndexedDB; do not introduce an upload/server dependency for editing or captions without explicitly designing the privacy model and asking for user consent.

The main video lane is a serialized list. Audio and overlays use independent absolute timeline positions. Do not implement global ripple by shifting every `start` field: items crossing a ripple range need source-correct splitting, and video overlays must preserve `sourceIn`.

Treat timeline edits as transactions:
1. Capture stable target IDs and source/timeline times.
2. Validate all affected ranges before mutation.
3. Commit one complete valid state as one Undo operation, or leave the project unchanged.
4. Keep preview timing, audio timing, export timing, autosave, and Undo/Redo consistent.

The FFmpeg export runs in WebAssembly (unless the device-encoder path in `docs/fast-export.md` handles the video). Memory usage is a first-class constraint on Android. Keep source/overlay asset caches bounded and clean generated FFmpeg virtual-filesystem files on both success and failure.

Auto captions use Transformers.js Whisper. A first run downloads the model; browser model caching is enabled by the library. Caption inference is chunked, but source audio decoding still uses whole-buffer `decodeAudioData`, which is a known long-file latency bottleneck. Do not claim performance improvements without recording measurements on real media.

## Required validation

For JavaScript changes:
```sh
node --check app.js
node --check setup.js
node --check sw.js
```

For browser and media-worker regressions, install Playwright plus Chromium and run:
```sh
python test_smoke.py
python test_ffmpeg_worker.py
```

The GitHub Actions **MediaY smoke tests** workflow runs those checks for pull requests. **Build APK** compiles the Android debug APK and uploads it as `MediaY-debug-apk`. The build also stamps a monotonically increasing Android version code from the workflow run number. A worker smoke test is not a substitute for an app-level export test; add real end-to-end export coverage as the next agent task.

Do not merge if the same PR head has a failing/pending required check. After code changes, verify the Android APK build as well as browser tests.

## Releases

A push/merge to `main` builds and uploads only a **debug APK**. It does not publish a GitHub Release automatically.

Production release publication is explicit: use **Actions → Build APK → Run workflow** and opt in to **Build, sign, and publish a production GitHub Release**. Before doing so, the repository owner must configure these Actions secrets:

- `ANDROID_KEYSTORE_BASE64`: Base64-encoded contents of the private release keystore.
- `ANDROID_KEYSTORE_PASSWORD`: keystore password.
- `ANDROID_KEY_ALIAS`: signing key alias.
- `ANDROID_KEY_PASSWORD`: key password.

Never commit the keystore, print the secrets, or replace an established app signing key without a deliberate migration plan. The workflow decodes the keystore into the ephemeral runner, builds `assembleRelease`, verifies the resulting APK with Android `apksigner`, and publishes the signed APK plus a SHA-256 checksum. Release tags are `v1.0.<workflow-run-number>`; Android `versionCode` uses the same positive run number. If a signing secret is absent, the release job should fail rather than publish a debug or unsigned APK. Debug APKs and production-signed APKs are different signing identities and may not update each other in place.

## Known scope gaps

- Physical-device export performance has not yet been benchmarked.
- The device-encoder (WebCodecs) export path in `mediay-fast.js` is new and has not run on a physical Android device. See `docs/fast-export.md`; it falls back to the FFmpeg-only path on any problem. CI exercises H.264 WebCodecs output when its Chromium build supports it, but Android WebView hardware support and speed still require a phone test.
- No full app-level automated test validates export duration, frame/audio sync, and repeat export after Undo/Redo.
- Export cancellation is not implemented. Export/caption stage timing diagnostics are available in the Export pane, but physical-device benchmark data is still missing.
- Whole-media caption audio decoding is still slow for long videos.
- Multi-thread FFmpeg has not been enabled; it requires cross-origin isolation / `SharedArrayBuffer` verification and may increase memory use.
- Global ripple-across-tracks, slip, and roll edits are not yet implemented.

ProjectMedia Y — mobile editor repair package

This patch is based on the source files read from https://github.com/kxzyte01-cloud/MediaY and the project files supplied in the conversation.

REPLACEMENT FILES
- index.html
- app.js
- style.css
- setup.js
- setup.css
- sw.js

WHAT THIS PATCH FIXES / ADDS
1. Home dashboard is shown on every app launch, after the saved editor state finishes restoring.
2. A Home button stays in the editor toolbar so the dashboard is reachable while editing.
3. Fixes the opposite empty-state visibility condition in updateChrome(). When a video, overlay, or audio track exists, the “What are you making?” panel no longer reappears over the editor/preview.
4. Enables pointer/touch interaction on Fabric's overlay canvas and recalculates canvas offset after responsive layout changes.
5. Adds a local project library: save named projects, continue the recent project, open other saved projects, and delete saved project records. Starting a new project first preserves the existing edit instead of silently replacing it.
6. Makes home-screen loading wait for IndexedDB project restoration, preventing the dashboard from racing startup.
7. Keeps transition duration changes in one undo step after the slider is released.
8. Updates the service worker cache version and makes its shell caching tolerant of optional/missing routes so a single missing file does not prevent installation.

INSTALL
1. Download and extract this ZIP.
2. In the root of the GitHub repository, replace the six files listed above.
3. Commit the changes and wait for your host / GitHub Pages deployment to finish.
4. On the phone, reload the website. If it still serves the old app, clear the site's stored cache/service worker or open it in a private tab to verify the new version.
5. Test: open Home, create a project, add a video, add an overlay, move/resize it, save the project, go Home, create another project, and reopen the saved project. Also test template save/use and export with a short video.

TESTING
- JavaScript syntax checks passed for app.js, setup.js, and sw.js.
- Automated Chromium smoke test passed for: home on startup, new-project setup, entering editor, returning Home, empty-state stays hidden when an overlay exists, named project save, and preservation of a previous project when starting another.
- The smoke test uses stubs for browser libraries and does not validate real media decoding, Fabric touch behavior on a physical Android device, FFmpeg export, or native APK packaging.

IMPORTANT
- This is a web-source patch, not a rebuilt APK. If your Android app embeds the web project, rebuild/repackage the APK after the source is deployed.
- Projects and media are stored locally in the browser's IndexedDB. They are not synced to GitHub or another device. Deleting browser storage can remove local projects/media.
- The GitHub integration allowed reading the repository files, but GitHub rejected write/branch operations with HTTP 403. Therefore this ZIP is prepared for upload; the repository itself has NOT been changed by this assistant.

ProjectMedia Y — mobile editor patch

Included replacement files:
- index.html   Adds an adjustable transition duration control to the Transition sheet.
- app.js       Fixes template edge cases and supports adjustable transition duration during export.
- style.css    Existing editor styling from the supplied project.
- setup.js     Adds a mobile-first Home screen with Continue Editing, New Project, My Templates, and Export Last Project.
- setup.css    Home screen, setup flow, overlay touch handling, and transition styling.
- sw.js        Bumps the service-worker cache and precaches the setup files. The precache list only uses files supplied in this patch.

How to use
1. Download and extract the ZIP.
2. In your GitHub repository root, replace the six files above with the files from this ZIP.
3. Commit and push the changes, then wait for GitHub Pages/your host to deploy.
4. On the phone, fully reload the site. If it still shows the old UI, clear the site's cached data/service worker or open it once in a private tab to test.
5. Test: Home > New project > Start editing > Media; add a video, tap Overlay to add a picture/video, select the overlay and drag/resize it in the preview; open Templates to save and reuse a template; select the second main clip > Transition to choose an effect and duration; export.

Important limitations
- This is a source-code patch, not a rebuilt APK. If you install a native APK, rebuild/repackage it from the updated web source.
- The supplied app currently stores one active project as “current”; the Home screen resumes that last project. It does not yet provide a multi-project library/history.
- Transition effects and their chosen duration are rendered during export; this source does not implement real-time transition preview playback in the editor.
- Export uses FFmpeg.wasm and may be slow or memory-limited on phones, especially at 2K/4K.

# Bundled speech engine (CPU)

The four third-party files below are NOT committed to Git (they are listed in `.gitignore`). `bash scripts/fetch_speech_engine.sh`
downloads `@xenova/transformers@2.17.2` from npm and refuses to continue unless every file matches `SHA256SUMS`. CI and the APK
build run it automatically. Only `SHA256SUMS` and this README are committed.

- `transformers.min.js` — @xenova/transformers 2.17.2 `dist/transformers.min.js`, unmodified (Apache-2.0, see LICENSE).
- `ort-wasm-simd.wasm`, `ort-wasm.wasm` — the ONNX Runtime WebAssembly binaries shipped in the same npm package
  (`dist/`). Without them the library downloads its runtime from cdn.jsdelivr.net, so the library alone is NOT offline-capable.
  Only the single-thread variants are bundled because the Android WebView is not cross-origin isolated (no threads).
- `SHA256SUMS` — checked in CI (`sha256sum -c SHA256SUMS`). To update: `npm pack @xenova/transformers@<version>`, copy the
  four files above from the package, regenerate `SHA256SUMS`, and update `SPEECH_ENGINE` in `app.js`.

The speech MODEL (Xenova/whisper-tiny, ~40 MB) is not bundled: it downloads from huggingface.co on first use and is kept in
the browser Cache Storage (`transformers-cache`).

# MediaY Security Baseline

Last reviewed: 2026-11-05

## Architecture and scope
MediaY is a browser-first, local-first editor. Project media is stored in browser IndexedDB and the Android package uses Capacitor. The repository currently has no application backend, user accounts, database service, Stripe integration, admin API, or server-side webhook endpoint.

The reel's Supabase RLS, IDOR, admin-route, Stripe webhook, and account-signup examples are therefore not current MediaY attack surfaces. Do not add pretend RLS policies or fake webhook verification to this client-only application. If a backend, accounts, cloud sync, payments, or AI proxy is introduced, those controls become mandatory release gates.

## Current security rules
1. No frontend secrets. Anything in HTML, JavaScript, WASM, an APK, source maps, or browser storage is inspectable. Never put provider API keys, service-role credentials, signing keys, or private tokens in the client.
2. Keep local media local by default. Do not upload source media, project files, captions, or diagnostics without a clear design, user consent, and privacy disclosure.
3. Treat imported files as untrusted. Validate media metadata and file sizes; do not build shell commands from filenames or user-controlled strings; keep FFmpeg arguments structured; clean temporary virtual filesystem files on success and failure.
4. Avoid unsafe DOM sinks. Do not pass untrusted media metadata, captions, filenames, or model output to innerHTML. Prefer textContent, DOM construction, and allowlisted markup.
5. Keep dependencies controlled. Pin dependency versions. Prefer local runtime assets for offline-critical paths. CDN scripts are executable third-party code and must be reviewed; do not silently add remote scripts or fonts.
6. Keep service-worker caching scoped to known application assets. Bump the cache key when shipped assets change; never cache secrets or user media in the application shell.
7. Request only Android permissions required for the selected action. Do not log media contents, credentials, or private file URIs.
8. Keep keystores and passwords in protected GitHub Actions secrets. Never commit signing material or private credentials. Disable public source maps unless there is a deliberate policy.
9. Treat AI model output and prompt content as untrusted data. Do not give a client-side model privileged shell, repository, payment, or account-management tools. Enforce spend limits on any future server-side AI proxy.

## Backend-only controls — required if that architecture is added
- Enable RLS on every user-data table; policies must be ownership-scoped and default-deny.
- Enforce object ownership on every server-side read/write; test with two distinct users.
- Check admin roles on the server using trusted claims or a protected role table, never client-editable profile metadata.
- Verify payment webhooks with official signature verification, event IDs, and replay/idempotency controls.
- Rate-limit signup, login, password reset, and billable AI endpoints; add abuse controls and per-account spend limits.
- Apply HSTS, nosniff, frame restrictions, and a CSP at the actual hosting/server layer. A static HTML meta tag is not a substitute for response headers.

## Pre-release checks
- Review tracked files for .env, private keys, tokens, and accidental credentials.
- Scan built web bundles and APK contents for secrets; all client-bundled values are public.
- Run dependency vulnerability checks against actual lockfiles and vendored dependencies.
- Test import, export, caption, cancellation, offline, and project restore paths with malformed and unusually large media.
- Re-check hosting headers and service-worker cache behavior on the deployed origin.
- If backend features are introduced, add two-user authorization, webhook-signature, rate-limit, and backup-restore tests before enabling signups.

/* ProjectMedia Y — app.js
   Libraries: Fabric.js (layers), Anime.js (motion), Dexie (storage), FFmpeg.wasm (export, loaded on first Export) */
'use strict';
const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);
const RATIOS = { '9:16': [540, 960], '16:9': [960, 540], '1:1': [720, 720] };
const FX = [
  { n: 'Original', c: '', f: '' },
  { n: 'Vivid', c: 'saturate(1.5) contrast(1.1)', f: 'eq=saturation=1.5:contrast=1.1' },
  { n: 'Warm', c: 'sepia(.25) saturate(1.3)', f: 'colorbalance=rs=.15:bs=-.15,eq=saturation=1.2' },
  { n: 'Cool', c: 'hue-rotate(-12deg) saturate(1.1)', f: 'colorbalance=rs=-.12:bs=.15' },
  { n: 'Mono', c: 'grayscale(1)', f: 'hue=s=0' },
  { n: 'Noir', c: 'grayscale(1) contrast(1.5) brightness(.9)', f: 'hue=s=0,eq=contrast=1.5:brightness=-.05' },
  { n: 'Fade', c: 'contrast(.85) brightness(1.1) saturate(.8)', f: 'eq=contrast=.85:brightness=.06:saturation=.8' },
  { n: 'Retro', c: 'sepia(.7) contrast(1.1)', f: 'colorchannelmixer=.393:.769:.189:0:.349:.686:.168:0:.272:.534:.131' },
  { n: 'Cinema', c: 'contrast(1.2) saturate(.9) brightness(.95)', f: 'eq=contrast=1.2:saturation=.9:brightness=-.03,vignette=PI/5' }];

const DEFAULT_ADJUST = Object.freeze({ brightness: 0, contrast: 100, saturation: 100, sharpen: 0, highlights: 0, shadows: 0, grain: 0 });
const BLEND_OPTIONS = [
  ['Normal', 'normal', 'source-over'], ['Add', 'addition', 'lighter'], ['Average', 'average', 'source-over'],
  ['Color burn', 'burn', 'color-burn'], ['Color dodge', 'dodge', 'color-dodge'], ['Darken', 'darken', 'darken'],
  ['Lighten', 'lighten', 'lighten'], ['Multiply', 'multiply', 'multiply'], ['Screen', 'screen', 'screen'],
  ['Overlay', 'overlay', 'overlay'], ['Hard light', 'hardlight', 'hard-light'], ['Soft light', 'softlight', 'soft-light']
];
const adjustOf = clip => ({ ...DEFAULT_ADJUST, ...(clip && clip.adjust || {}) });
const bounded = (value, min, max) => Math.max(min, Math.min(max, Number(value) || 0));
function adjustmentFilter(clip) {
  const a = adjustOf(clip), filters = [];
  if (a.brightness !== 0 || a.contrast !== 100 || a.saturation !== 100) {
    filters.push('eq=brightness=' + (bounded(a.brightness, -100, 100) / 200).toFixed(3) +
      ':contrast=' + Math.max(0.1, bounded(a.contrast, 0, 200) / 100).toFixed(3) +
      ':saturation=' + (bounded(a.saturation, 0, 200) / 100).toFixed(3));
  }
  if (a.highlights !== 0 || a.shadows !== 0) {
    const low = bounded(0.25 + bounded(a.shadows, -100, 100) / 100 * 0.12, 0.13, 0.37);
    const high = bounded(0.75 + bounded(a.highlights, -100, 100) / 100 * 0.12, 0.63, 0.87);
    filters.push("curves=all='0/0 0.25/" + low.toFixed(3) + " 0.5/0.5 0.75/" + high.toFixed(3) + " 1/1'");
  }
  if (a.sharpen > 0) filters.push('unsharp=5:5:' + (bounded(a.sharpen, 0, 100) / 50).toFixed(2) + ':5:5:0.00');
  if (a.grain > 0) filters.push('noise=alls=' + (bounded(a.grain, 0, 100) * 0.18).toFixed(1) + ':allf=t+u');
  return filters.join(',');
}
function previewFilter(clip) {
  if (!clip) return '';
  const a = adjustOf(clip), filters = [];
  if (FX[clip.fx || 0] && FX[clip.fx || 0].c) filters.push(FX[clip.fx || 0].c);
  if (a.brightness !== 0) filters.push('brightness(' + (1 + bounded(a.brightness, -100, 100) / 200).toFixed(2) + ')');
  if (a.contrast !== 100) filters.push('contrast(' + Math.max(0, bounded(a.contrast, 0, 200) / 100).toFixed(2) + ')');
  if (a.saturation !== 100) filters.push('saturate(' + (bounded(a.saturation, 0, 200) / 100).toFixed(2) + ')');
  return filters.join(' ');
}
function overlaySourceAt(overlay, timelineTime) {
  // Keep preview, rendered picture-in-picture, and its audio on the same source-time mapping.
  return Math.max(0, Number(overlay && overlay.sourceIn) || 0) +
    Math.max(0, Number(timelineTime) - (Number(overlay && overlay.start) || 0));
}
function canvasBlendMode(mode) {
  const found = BLEND_OPTIONS.find(item => item[1] === mode);
  return found ? found[2] : 'source-over';
}

const TS = [ // text styles
  () => ({ fill: '#fff', stroke: '#000', strokeWidth: 2 }),
  () => ({ fill: '#fff', strokeWidth: 0, shadow: new fabric.Shadow({ color: '#ff2d95', blur: 26 }) }),
  () => ({ fill: '#111', strokeWidth: 0, backgroundColor: '#ffe14a' }),
  () => ({ fill: '#ffe14a', stroke: '#000', strokeWidth: 5, fontStyle: 'italic', paintFirst: 'stroke' }),
  () => ({ fill: '#fff', strokeWidth: 0, backgroundColor: '#c8101e', fontSize: 44 }),
  () => ({ fill: '#fff', strokeWidth: 0, backgroundColor: '#0b2a5b', fontSize: 40 })];
const BASE_PX = 30;
let PX = BASE_PX; // timeline pixels per second; editable with timeline zoom controls
const uid = () => Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10);
const fmt = s => `${String(Math.floor(s / 60)).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(Math.floor(s % 60)).padStart(2, '0')}`;
const TIMELINE_FPS = 30; // One authoritative project/export timebase.
const MIN_TIMELINE_FRAME = 1 / TIMELINE_FPS;
const say = m => ($('status').textContent = m);

// Shared blocking progress UI for long-running media tasks.
function updateBusy(title, detail, progress = null, stage = 'Working') {
  const overlay = $('busyOverlay');
  if (!overlay) return;
  overlay.hidden = false;
  overlay.dataset.state = 'busy';
  document.body.setAttribute('aria-busy', 'true');
  $('busyDismiss').hidden = true;
  if (title != null) $('busyTitle').textContent = title;
  if (detail != null) $('busyDetail').textContent = detail;
  $('busyStage').textContent = stage;
  const bar = $('busyProgress'), label = $('busyPercent');
  if (typeof progress === 'number' && Number.isFinite(progress)) {
    const value = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, progress));
    bar.value = value;
    label.textContent = Math.round(value) + '%';
  } else {
    bar.removeAttribute('value');
    label.textContent = '';
  }
}
function showBusyError(title, detail) {
  updateBusy(title, detail, null, 'Needs attention');
  const overlay = $('busyOverlay');
  if (overlay) overlay.dataset.state = 'error';
  const dismiss = $('busyDismiss');
  dismiss.hidden = false;
  dismiss.disabled = false;
  dismiss.textContent = 'Close';
  dismiss.onclick = hideBusy;
}
function hideBusy() {
  const overlay = $('busyOverlay');
  if (overlay) { overlay.hidden = true; delete overlay.dataset.state; }
  const dismiss = $('busyDismiss');
  if (dismiss) { dismiss.textContent = 'Close'; dismiss.disabled = false; dismiss.onclick = hideBusy; }
  document.body.removeAttribute('aria-busy');
}
$('busyDismiss').onclick = hideBusy;

/* ---------- Database (Dexie): media blobs + project state ---------- */
const db = new Dexie('projectmedia-y');
db.version(1).stores({ projects: 'id', media: 'id' });
const urls = {};      // mediaId -> object URL
const files = {};     // mediaId -> Blob
async function storeMedia(file) {
  const id = uid();
  await db.media.put({ id, name: file.name, type: file.type, blob: file }); // copied into IndexedDB
  files[id] = file; urls[id] = URL.createObjectURL(file);
  return id;
}
async function loadMedia(id) {
  if (urls[id]) return urls[id];
  const row = await db.media.get(id);
  if (!row || !row.blob) return null;
  // Preserve the original name/type after IndexedDB restores a Blob, so FFmpeg can
  // choose the appropriate input suffix for saved projects and reusable templates.
  const blob = row.blob;
  files[id] = (typeof File !== 'undefined' && blob instanceof File)
    ? blob
    : new File([blob], row.name || `${id}.bin`, { type: row.type || blob.type || 'application/octet-stream' });
  return (urls[id] = URL.createObjectURL(files[id]));
}

// Decode template image media before changing the active project. Some mobile pickers
// supply images in a browser-supported-but-not-HTMLImageElement-friendly format, or with
// a MIME type/extension that does not match the actual encoding. Fall back to bitmap decode
// and normalize it to PNG so the template can reuse the same durable media ID.
const decodeImageElement = (src, label) => new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
  const image = new Image();
  image.onload = () => resolve(image);
  image.onerror = () => reject(new Error(`Could not decode overlay image “${label}”.`));
  image.src = src;
});
async function loadOverlayImage(url, overlay) {
  const label = overlay.name || 'unnamed image';
  try {
    return await decodeImageElement(url, label);
  } catch (firstError) {
    const row = await db.media.get(overlay.mediaId).catch(() => null);
    const source = files[overlay.mediaId] || (row && row.blob);
    if (!source || typeof createImageBitmap !== 'function') {
      throw new Error(`Could not read overlay image “${label}”. Re-import it as a standard JPG or PNG and save the template again.`);
    }
    let bitmap;
    try {
      bitmap = await createImageBitmap(source);
    } catch (_) {
      throw new Error(`Overlay image “${label}” is missing, damaged, or in a format this browser cannot decode. Re-import a standard JPG or PNG and save the template again.`);
    }
    try {
      const normalized = document.createElement('canvas');
      normalized.width = bitmap.width;
      normalized.height = bitmap.height;
      const ctx = normalized.getContext('2d');
      if (!ctx) throw new Error('Could not create an image conversion canvas.');
      ctx.drawImage(bitmap, 0, 0);
      const png = await new Promise((resolve, reject) => normalized.toBlob(blob => blob ? resolve(blob) : reject(new Error('Could not convert the overlay image to PNG.')), 'image/png'));
      const name = `${label.replace(/\.[^.]+$/, '') || 'overlay'}.png`;
      const file = new File([png], name, { type: 'image/png', lastModified: Date.now() });
      await db.media.put({ id: overlay.mediaId, name, type: 'image/png', blob: file });
      files[overlay.mediaId] = file;
      if (urls[overlay.mediaId]) URL.revokeObjectURL(urls[overlay.mediaId]);
      urls[overlay.mediaId] = URL.createObjectURL(file);
      return await decodeImageElement(urls[overlay.mediaId], name);
    } finally {
      if (bitmap && typeof bitmap.close === 'function') bitmap.close();
    }
  }
}
async function validateTemplateAssets(template, options = {}) {
  // A template can contain original intro/outro clips, a replaceable slot, and audio
  // as well as visual overlays. Check every stored reference before changing the edit.
  const mainClips = [
    ...(template.pre || []),
    ...(template.slotClip && !options.skipSlotClip ? [template.slotClip] : []),
    ...(template.post || [])
  ];
  for (const clip of mainClips) {
    const url = clip.mediaId && await loadMedia(clip.mediaId);
    if (!url) throw new Error(`Template video “${clip.name || 'unnamed clip'}” is missing from this browser's local storage. Re-import it, save the template again, and retry.`);
    const length = await probe(url, 'video');
    if (!Number.isFinite(length) || length <= 0) throw new Error(`Template video “${clip.name || 'unnamed clip'}” cannot be decoded by this browser. Convert it to a standard MP4 or MOV and save the template again.`);
  }
  for (const track of template.audio || []) {
    const url = track.mediaId && await loadMedia(track.mediaId);
    if (!url) throw new Error(`Template audio “${track.name || 'unnamed track'}” is missing from local storage. Re-import it and save the template again.`);
    const length = await probe(url, 'audio');
    if (!Number.isFinite(length) || length <= 0) throw new Error(`Template audio “${track.name || 'unnamed track'}” cannot be decoded by this browser. Re-import it as a supported audio file and save the template again.`);
  }
  for (const overlay of template.overlays || []) {
    if (overlay.type === 'text' || !overlay.mediaId) continue;
    const url = await loadMedia(overlay.mediaId);
    if (!url) throw new Error(`Template media for “${overlay.name || overlay.type}” is missing from local storage. Re-import the original image/video and save the template again.`);
    if (overlay.type === 'image') {
      await loadOverlayImage(url, overlay);
    } else if (overlay.type === 'video') {
      const length = await probe(url, 'video');
      if (!Number.isFinite(length) || length <= 0) {
        throw new Error(`Template overlay video “${overlay.name || 'unnamed video'}” cannot be decoded in this browser. Convert it to MP4 and save the template again.`);
      }
    }
  }
}

/* ---------- State (single source of truth) ---------- */
let S = { ratio: '16:9', main: [], audio: [], overlays: [], nextLayer: 1 };
let sel = null, t = 0, playing = false, undoStack = [], redoStack = [], saveTimer;
// The current row is the crash-safe resume checkpoint. Named project rows live beside it.
let activeProjectId = 'current', activeProjectName = 'Untitled project';
const dur = c => (c.out - c.in) / (c.speed || 1);
const total = () => S.main.reduce((a, c) => a + dur(c), 0);
const video = $('sourceVideo');

/* ---------- Fabric canvas ---------- */
const canvas = new fabric.Canvas('overlayCanvas', { enableRetinaScaling: false, preserveObjectStacking: true, selection: false, perPixelTargetFind: false, targetFindTolerance: 12, stopContextMenu: true });
// Keep transform handles physically large on phones even though the editor uses a
// high-resolution logical canvas scaled down to the responsive preview.
const OVERLAY_HANDLE_CSS_PX = 44;
function syncOverlayControls() {
  // Caption persistence and other non-browser tests may provide a canvas-compatible
  // stub that does not implement Fabric's sizing/selection APIs. Overlay handles are
  // optional in those contexts; never let UI sizing break the underlying edit action.
  if (!canvas || typeof canvas.getWidth !== 'function' ||
      typeof canvas.getObjects !== 'function' || typeof canvas.requestRenderAll !== 'function') return;
  const displayWidth = canvas.upperCanvasEl && canvas.upperCanvasEl.getBoundingClientRect().width;
  const logicalWidth = canvas.getWidth();
  if (!displayWidth || !logicalWidth) return;
  const handleSize = Math.max(22, Math.min(180, OVERLAY_HANDLE_CSS_PX * logicalWidth / displayWidth));
  for (const obj of canvas.getObjects()) {
    if (!obj || typeof obj.set !== 'function') continue;
    obj.set({ cornerSize: handleSize, touchCornerSize: handleSize, padding: Math.max(8, handleSize * 0.12),
      transparentCorners: false, cornerColor: '#ff4b55', cornerStrokeColor: '#ffffff', borderColor: '#ff4b55',
      borderScaleFactor: Math.max(1.5, handleSize / 22) });
  }
  canvas.requestRenderAll();
}
// Recalculate handle hit areas when the preview changes size (rotation, split-screen,
// responsive layout), without rebuilding or moving any overlay objects.
if (typeof ResizeObserver !== 'undefined') {
  const overlayResizeObserver = new ResizeObserver(() => requestAnimationFrame(syncOverlayControls));
  overlayResizeObserver.observe($('previewFrame'));
} else {
  window.addEventListener('resize', () => requestAnimationFrame(syncOverlayControls), { passive: true });
}
Object.assign(canvas.wrapperEl.style, { position: 'absolute', inset: '0', width: '100%', height: '100%', maxWidth: '100%', maxHeight: '100%' });
// Fabric creates a fixed-pixel wrapper by default. Force it to follow the responsive preview frame.
canvas.wrapperEl.style.zIndex = '2';
function sizeCanvas() {
  const [w, h] = RATIOS[S.ratio];
  canvas.setDimensions({ width: w, height: h });
  canvas.setDimensions({ width: '100%', height: '100%' }, { cssOnly: true });
  $('previewFrame').style.aspectRatio = `${w} / ${h}`;
  $('previewFrame').style.width = `min(100%, calc(100cqh * ${w / h}))`;
  $('previewFrame').style.maxWidth = 'none';
  $('aspectRatio').value = S.ratio; markRatio();
  requestAnimationFrame(syncOverlayControls);
}
// Locked layering: every overlay keeps its own layer number. Stack order always comes from it.
function restack() {
  canvas._objects.sort((a, b) => a.layer - b.layer);
  canvas.requestRenderAll();
}
canvas.on('object:modified', e => {
  const o = S.overlays.find(x => x.id === e.target.pmId); if (!o) return;
  Object.assign(o, { left: e.target.left, top: e.target.top, scaleX: e.target.scaleX, scaleY: e.target.scaleY, angle: e.target.angle });
  commit();
});
canvas.on('selection:created', e => select(e.selected[0].pmId));
canvas.on('selection:updated', e => select(e.selected[0].pmId));
canvas.on('selection:cleared', () => { if (sel && sel.lane === 'overlay') select(null); });

async function buildObject(o, { restackAfter = true } = {}) {
  const [w, h] = RATIOS[S.ratio];
  let obj;
  if (o.type === 'text') {
    await ensureFontsReady();
    obj = new fabric.Textbox(o.text, { width: w * 0.8, fontFamily: 'Avenir Next, Avenir, Segoe UI, system-ui, sans-serif', fontWeight: 700, fontSize: 56, textAlign: 'center', ...TS[o.style || 0]() });
  } else {
    const url = await loadMedia(o.mediaId); if (!url) return;
    let el;
    if (o.type === 'video') {
      el = document.createElement('video'); el.src = url; el.muted = true; el.playsInline = true; o.el = el;
      await new Promise((resolve, reject) => { el.onloadeddata = resolve; el.onerror = () => reject(new Error('Could not read this overlay video. Try converting it to MP4.')); });
      el.width = el.videoWidth; el.height = el.videoHeight;
      const decodedDuration = Number.isFinite(el.duration) && el.duration > 0 ? el.duration :
        (Number.isFinite(o.srcDur) && o.srcDur > 0 ? o.srcDur : o.dur);
      o.srcDur = decodedDuration;
      o.sourceIn = Math.max(0, Math.min(Number(o.sourceIn) || 0, Math.max(0, decodedDuration - 0.001)));
      obj = new fabric.Image(el);
    } else {
      el = await loadOverlayImage(url, o);
      obj = new fabric.Image(el);
    }
    if (o.scaleX == null) { o.scaleX = o.scaleY = (w * 0.4) / obj.width; }
  }
  Object.assign(obj, { pmId: o.id, layer: o.layer });
  obj.set({ left: o.left ?? w * 0.1, top: o.top ?? h * 0.1, scaleX: o.scaleX ?? 1, scaleY: o.scaleY ?? 1, angle: o.angle || 0, opacity: bounded(o.opacity ?? 1, 0, 1), globalCompositeOperation: canvasBlendMode(o.blend || 'normal'), cornerSize: 22, transparentCorners: false, cornerColor: '#ff4b55', cornerStrokeColor: '#ffffff', borderColor: '#ff4b55', lockUniScaling: o.type !== 'text', lockScalingFlip: true, centeredScaling: false, perPixelTargetFind: false });
  Object.assign(o, { left: obj.left, top: obj.top, scaleX: obj.scaleX, scaleY: obj.scaleY });
  if (o.key && obj.applyFilters) { obj.filters = [keyFilter(o.key)]; obj.applyFilters(); }
  canvas.add(obj); syncOverlayControls(); if (restackAfter) restack(); return obj;
}
function releaseOverlayMediaElement(element) {
  if (!element || String(element.tagName || '').toUpperCase() !== 'VIDEO') return;
  try { element.pause(); } catch (_) {}
  try { element.removeAttribute('src'); element.load(); } catch (_) {}
}
function disposeOverlayObject(object, overlay) {
  if (object) {
    // After restore(), S already points at snapshot objects without .el. The live
    // video element must therefore be released through the Fabric object itself.
    try { if (typeof object.getElement === 'function') releaseOverlayMediaElement(object.getElement()); } catch (_) {}
    try { canvas.remove(object); } catch (_) { /* already detached */ }
    try { if (typeof object.dispose === 'function') object.dispose(); } catch (_) { /* release best-effort */ }
  }
  const element = overlay && overlay.el;
  if (element) {
    releaseOverlayMediaElement(element);
    delete overlay.el;
  }
}
let rebuildGeneration = 0;
async function rebuildCanvas() {
  // Prevent overlapping Undo/Redo rebuilds from leaving stale Fabric objects.
  const generation = ++rebuildGeneration;
  for (const object of canvas.getObjects().slice()) {
    const overlay = S.overlays.find(item => item.id === object.pmId);
    disposeOverlayObject(object, overlay);
  }
  canvas.clear(); sizeCanvas();
  for (const o of [...S.overlays].sort((a, b) => a.layer - b.layer)) {
    if (generation !== rebuildGeneration) return;
    const built = await buildObject(o);
    if (generation !== rebuildGeneration) {
      if (built) disposeOverlayObject(built, o);
      return;
    }
  }
  if (generation === rebuildGeneration) refresh(t);
}
const objOf = id => canvas.getObjects().find(x => x.pmId === id);

/* ---------- Undo / save ---------- */
const snap = () => JSON.stringify(S, (k, v) => (k === 'el' ? undefined : v));
function scheduleProjectSave() {
  clearTimeout(saveTimer);
  saveTimer = setTimeout(() => saveProject().catch(err => {
    console.warn('Auto-save failed:', err);
    say('Auto-save failed. Check available storage on this device.');
  }), 800);
}
function commit() {
  undoStack.push(snap()); if (undoStack.length > 50) undoStack.shift(); redoStack = [];
  drawTimeline();
  scheduleProjectSave();
}
function normalizeState(value) {
  const raw = typeof value === 'string' ? JSON.parse(value) : value;
  return {
    ...raw,
    ratio: RATIOS[raw && raw.ratio] ? raw.ratio : '16:9',
    main: Array.isArray(raw && raw.main) ? raw.main : [],
    audio: Array.isArray(raw && raw.audio) ? raw.audio : [],
    overlays: Array.isArray(raw && raw.overlays) ? raw.overlays : [],
    nextLayer: Number.isFinite(raw && raw.nextLayer) ? raw.nextLayer : 1
  };
}
async function restore(json) { S = normalizeState(json); sel = null; await rebuildCanvas(); drawTimeline(); }
$('undoBtn').onclick = async () => {
  if (undoStack.length < 2) return;
  redoStack.push(undoStack.pop());
  await restore(undoStack[undoStack.length - 1]);
  scheduleProjectSave();
};
$('redoBtn').onclick = async () => {
  if (!redoStack.length) return;
  const s = redoStack.pop();
  undoStack.push(s);
  await restore(s);
  scheduleProjectSave();
};
async function saveProject() {
  const state = snap(), updated = Date.now();
  // Keep one always-up-to-date resume checkpoint, even when editing a named project.
  await db.projects.put({ id: 'current', state, updated, activeProjectId, activeProjectName });
  if (activeProjectId !== 'current') {
    await db.projects.put({ id: activeProjectId, name: activeProjectName || 'Untitled project', state, updated, kind: 'project' });
  }
  say(activeProjectId === 'current' ? 'Saved on this device.' : `Saved “${activeProjectName}”.`);
}
$('saveBtn').onclick = async () => {
  const suggested = activeProjectName && activeProjectName !== 'Untitled project' ? activeProjectName :
    ((S.main[0] && S.main[0].name) || (S.overlays[0] && S.overlays[0].name) || 'My video project').replace(/\.[^.]+$/, '');
  const entered = window.prompt(activeProjectId === 'current' ? 'Name this project:' : 'Save project as:', suggested);
  if (entered == null) return;
  const name = entered.trim() || suggested;
  if (activeProjectId === 'current') activeProjectId = `project-${uid()}`;
  activeProjectName = name.slice(0, 80);
  try { await saveProject(); } catch (err) { console.error(err); say('Could not save this project: ' + (err.message || err)); }
};

/* ---------- Runtime guard: heavy-job safety, background keep-alive, in-place project refresh ---------- */
let exportInProgress = 0, exportCancelRequested = false;
let fontsReadyPromise = null;
// Canvas text is measured the moment it is created. Use the same local system-font stack as the editor,
// and wait only for fonts already used by the document; never depend on an unbundled web font.
function ensureFontsReady() {
  if (fontsReadyPromise) return fontsReadyPromise;
  fontsReadyPromise = (async () => {
    if (!document.fonts || !document.fonts.ready) return;
    try {
      await Promise.race([
        document.fonts.ready,
        new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 800))
      ]);
    } catch (_) { /* system-font fallback remains available */ }
  })();
  return fontsReadyPromise;
}

// Reference-counted wrapper around the native Android foreground service (MediaYExportService), so that
// export and caption jobs share one service, start/stop calls never overlap, and the last job stops it.
const Background = (() => {
  let holders = 0, running = false;
  let chain = Promise.resolve();
  const queue = job => (chain = chain.then(job, job));
  return {
    begin() {
      holders++;
      return queue(async () => {
        if (running) return true;
        if (holders <= 0) return false; // the job already ended while an earlier call was in flight
        running = await beginNativeExportService();
        return running;
      });
    },
    end() {
      holders = Math.max(0, holders - 1);
      return queue(async () => {
        if (holders > 0 || !running) return;
        running = false;
        await endNativeExportService();
      });
    },
    get active() { return running; }
  };
})();

// Frees the two big memory users once a job is finished. Both reload automatically on next use.
async function releaseHeavyResources({ includeSpeech = true } = {}) {
  const released = [];
  if (asr && includeSpeech) {
    const model = asr; asr = null;
    try { if (typeof model.dispose === 'function') await model.dispose(); } catch (_) { /* already gone */ }
    released.push('speech model');
  }
  if (ff && !ffLoading && !exportInProgress && !(pendingExportSave && pendingExportSave.engine === ff)) { disposeFFmpeg(); released.push('video engine'); }
  return released;
}
function dropMediaCaches() {
  Object.values(audios).forEach(el => { try { el.pause(); el.removeAttribute('src'); el.load(); } catch (_) {} });
  Object.keys(audios).forEach(k => delete audios[k]);
  Object.keys(audioLoads).forEach(k => delete audioLoads[k]);
  try { video.pause(); } catch (_) {}
  delete video.dataset.mid; video.removeAttribute('src'); try { video.load(); } catch (_) {}
  for (const id of Object.keys(urls)) { try { URL.revokeObjectURL(urls[id]); } catch (_) {} delete urls[id]; }
  for (const id of Object.keys(files)) delete files[id];
  for (const id of Object.keys(decoded)) delete decoded[id];
  for (const id of Object.keys(thumbs)) delete thumbs[id];
}
// Does by itself what closing and reopening the app and the project used to do: saves the project, frees the
// heavy engines, forgets cached media handles, then rebuilds preview and timeline from the saved copy.
let reopenInFlight = null;
function reopenProjectInPlace(reason = 'generation') {
  if (reopenInFlight) return reopenInFlight;
  reopenInFlight = (async () => {
    try {
      if (playing) stop();
      const keepTime = Math.min(t, total());
      clearTimeout(saveTimer);
      await saveProject();
      const row = await db.projects.get('current');
      if (!row || typeof row.state !== 'string') throw new Error('The saved copy could not be read back.');
      const next = normalizeState(row.state); // parsed before anything live is touched
      if (next.main.length !== S.main.length || next.audio.length !== S.audio.length || next.overlays.length !== S.overlays.length) {
        throw new Error('The saved copy does not match the project on screen.');
      }
      await releaseHeavyResources();
      dropMediaCaches();
      S = next; sel = null;
      await rebuildCanvas();
      drawTimeline();
      if (S.main.length) await seek(keepTime);
      refresh(keepTime);
      if (S.main.length) setTimeout(preloadEngine, 4000);
      return { ok: true, reason };
    } catch (error) {
      console.warn('In-place project refresh skipped:', error);
      return { ok: false, reason, error };
    } finally {
      reopenInFlight = null;
    }
  })();
  return reopenInFlight;
}

/* ---------- Import ---------- */
$('importBtn').onclick = () => { $('mediaInput').accept = 'video/*,image/*,audio/*'; $('mediaInput').click(); };
$('audioBtn').onclick = () => { $('mediaInput').accept = 'audio/*'; $('mediaInput').click(); };
$('mediaInput').onchange = async e => {
  const selected = [...(e.target.files || [])]; e.target.value = '';
  if (!selected.length) return;
  say('Importing…'); let added = 0;
  updateBusy('Importing media…', 'Preparing the selected files.', 0, 'Import');
  try {
    for (let fileIndex = 0; fileIndex < selected.length; fileIndex++) {
      const f = selected[fileIndex];
      updateBusy('Importing media…', `Reading ${f.name} (${fileIndex + 1} of ${selected.length}).`, (fileIndex / Math.max(1, selected.length)) * 100, 'Import');
      const mediaId = await storeMedia(f);
      const isImage = f.type.startsWith('image'), isAudio = f.type.startsWith('audio'), isVideo = f.type.startsWith('video');
      if (isImage) { await addOverlay({ type: 'image', mediaId, name: f.name, start: t, dur: 5 }); added++; continue; }
      if (!isAudio && !isVideo) throw new Error(`Unsupported file type: ${f.name}`);
      const length = await probe(urls[mediaId], isAudio ? 'audio' : 'video');
      if (!Number.isFinite(length) || length <= 0) throw new Error(`Could not read ${f.name}. Try a standard MP4/MOV video or MP3/M4A audio file.`);
      if (isVideo) S.main.push({ id: uid(), mediaId, name: f.name, in: 0, out: length, src: length });
      else S.audio.push({ id: uid(), mediaId, name: f.name, start: t, in: 0, out: length, src: length });
      added++;
    }
    commit(); if (S.main.length) await seek(Math.min(t, total())); refresh(Math.min(t, total())); say(`${added} media file${added === 1 ? '' : 's'} added.`);
  } catch (err) {
    commit(); refresh(t); console.error(err); say('Import stopped: ' + (err.message || err));
  }
  hideBusy();
  if (S.main.length) setTimeout(preloadEngine, 1500); // warm the export engine in the background
};
const probe = (url, kind = 'video') => new Promise(resolve => {
  const m = document.createElement(kind === 'audio' ? 'audio' : 'video'); m.preload = 'metadata';
  const done = value => { m.onloadedmetadata = null; m.onerror = null; resolve(Number.isFinite(value) ? value : 0); };
  m.onloadedmetadata = () => done(m.duration); m.onerror = () => done(0); m.src = url;
});

/* ---------- Overlays (PiP, stickers, text) ---------- */
async function addOverlay(o) {
  o = { id: uid(), layer: S.nextLayer++, dur: 3, start: t, ...o };
  if (o.type === 'video') {
    o.sourceIn = Math.max(0, Number(o.sourceIn) || 0);
    o.srcDur = Number.isFinite(o.srcDur) && o.srcDur > 0 ? o.srcDur : o.dur;
  }
  S.overlays.push(o);
  const obj = await buildObject(o);
  if (obj && o.type === 'text') anime({ targets: obj, opacity: [0, 1], top: [obj.top + 50, obj.top], duration: 700, easing: 'easeOutElastic(1, .6)', update: () => canvas.requestRenderAll() });
  commit();
}
$('textBtn').onclick = () => openSheet('text');
const pipInput = Object.assign(document.createElement('input'), { type: 'file', accept: 'video/*,image/*', hidden: true });
document.body.appendChild(pipInput);
$('pipBtn').onclick = () => { pipInput.dataset.replace = sel && sel.lane === 'overlay' ? sel.id : ''; pipInput.click(); };
pipInput.onchange = async () => {
  const f = pipInput.files && pipInput.files[0]; if (!f) return;
  pipInput.value = '';
  try {
    const mediaId = await storeMedia(f), type = f.type.startsWith('video') ? 'video' : f.type.startsWith('image') ? 'image' : '';
    if (!type) throw new Error('Choose a picture or video for an overlay.');
    const rid = pipInput.dataset.replace;
    if (rid) { // Keep the layer's position and timing when replacing an overlay.
      const o = S.overlays.find(x => x.id === rid); if (!o) throw new Error('The selected overlay no longer exists.');
      const replacementDuration = type === 'video' ? await probe(urls[mediaId], 'video') : 0;
      if (type === 'video' && (!Number.isFinite(replacementDuration) || replacementDuration <= 0)) {
        throw new Error('Could not read this replacement overlay video. Try converting it to MP4.');
      }
      const old = objOf(rid); disposeOverlayObject(old, o);
      Object.assign(o, { mediaId, type, name: f.name }); delete o.el; delete o.scaleX; delete o.scaleY;
      if (type === 'video') {
        o.sourceIn = 0;
        o.srcDur = replacementDuration;
        o.dur = Math.min(Number.isFinite(o.dur) && o.dur > 0 ? o.dur : replacementDuration, replacementDuration);
      } else {
        delete o.sourceIn;
        delete o.srcDur;
      }
      await buildObject(o); restack(); commit(); refresh(t); say('Overlay replaced.');
    } else {
      const length = type === 'video' ? await probe(urls[mediaId], 'video') : 5;
      if (type === 'video' && (!Number.isFinite(length) || length <= 0)) throw new Error('Could not read this video. Try an MP4 or MOV file.');
      await addOverlay({ type, mediaId, name: f.name, dur: length, ...(type === 'video' ? { sourceIn: 0, srcDur: length } : {}) });
      refresh(t); say('Overlay added. Drag it in the preview to position it.');
    }
  } catch (err) { console.error(err); say('Could not add overlay: ' + (err.message || err)); }
};

/* ---------- Timeline ---------- */
function select(id, lane) {
  const L = lane || (S.main.some(c => c.id === id) ? 'main' : S.audio.some(c => c.id === id) ? 'audio' : S.overlays.some(c => c.id === id) ? 'overlay' : null);
  sel = id ? { id, lane: L } : null;
  if (L === 'overlay') { const ob = objOf(id); if (ob && canvas.getActiveObject() !== ob) canvas.setActiveObject(ob); } else canvas.discardActiveObject();
  drawTimeline();
}
const tl = $('tl'), inner = $('tlInner');
let drag = null, lastScroll = 0;
// Programmatic scrolling keeps the playhead visible. Ignore only the matching scroll event;
// otherwise the scroll handler can mistake refresh()/zoom movement for a user scrub.
let programmaticTimelineScroll = null;
function scrollTimelineToTime(time) {
  const maxLeft = Math.max(0, tl.scrollWidth - tl.clientWidth);
  const target = Math.max(0, Math.min(maxLeft, Math.max(0, Number(time) || 0) * PX));
  const current = tl.scrollLeft;
  const moving = Math.abs(current - target) > 0.5;
  // Only arm a guard when this call changes scroll position or supersedes a still-pending write.
  // refresh() runs repeatedly during playback, so no-op writes must not suppress real user scrolls.
