/* MediaY fast export engine (WebCodecs).
   Decodes the source video with the device's own video decoder, composites trim/speed/fade/overlays on a canvas,
   and encodes with the device's own H.264 encoder (hardware where the phone has it). Audio and the final MP4 are
   still produced by FFmpeg (cheap work). Every public function either returns a result or throws; the caller
   falls back to the FFmpeg-only export on ANY problem, so this module can never be the reason an export fails.

   Loaded as a browser global (window.MediaYFast) and as a Node module (parsing/planning helpers are unit-tested in Node;
   the decode/encode parts need a browser with WebCodecs). */
(function (root, factory) {
  if (typeof module === 'object' && module.exports) module.exports = factory();
  else root.MediaYFast = factory();
}(typeof self !== 'undefined' ? self : this, function () {
  'use strict';

  class Unsupported extends Error {
    constructor(message) { super(message); this.name = 'Unsupported'; }
  }
  const TS_BIAS_US = 5e6; // chunk timestamps must not be negative; edit lists can make early frames negative
  const even = n => Math.max(2, Math.round(n / 2) * 2);

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------
     1. MP4 / MOV parsing (moov box only; sample data is read later, in ranges)
     ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  const fourcc = (v, o) => String.fromCharCode(v.getUint8(o), v.getUint8(o + 1), v.getUint8(o + 2), v.getUint8(o + 3));
  const u64 = (v, o) => Number(v.getBigUint64(o));

  function listBoxes(v, start, end) {
    const out = [];
    let p = start;
    while (p + 8 <= end) {
      let size = v.getUint32(p);
      const type = fourcc(v, p + 4);
      let header = 8;
      if (size === 1) { size = u64(v, p + 8); header = 16; } else if (size === 0) size = end - p;
      if (size < header || p + size > end) break;
      out.push({ type, start: p + header, end: p + size });
      p += size;
    }
    return out;
  }
  const child = (v, box, type) => listBoxes(v, box.start, box.end).find(b => b.type === type) || null;
  const childrenOf = (v, box, type) => listBoxes(v, box.start, box.end).filter(b => b.type === type);

  async function readMoov(file) {
    let pos = 0;
    const size = file.size;
    while (pos + 8 <= size) {
      const head = new DataView(await file.slice(pos, Math.min(size, pos + 16)).arrayBuffer());
      if (head.byteLength < 8) break;
      let boxSize = head.getUint32(0);
      const type = fourcc(head, 4);
      let header = 8;
      if (boxSize === 1 && head.byteLength >= 16) { boxSize = u64(head, 8); header = 16; } else if (boxSize === 0) boxSize = size - pos;
      if (boxSize < header) throw new Unsupported('damaged MP4 box');
      if (type === 'moof') throw new Unsupported('fragmented MP4');
      if (type === 'moov') {
        if (boxSize > 512 * 1024 * 1024) throw new Unsupported('moov too large');
        return await file.slice(pos + header, pos + boxSize).arrayBuffer();
      }
      pos += boxSize;
    }
    throw new Unsupported('not an MP4/MOV file (no moov box)');
  }

  function hex2(n) { return (n & 255).toString(16).padStart(2, '0'); }

  function codecFromConfig(fourccName, cfg) {
    if (fourccName === 'avc1' || fourccName === 'avc3') return 'avc1.' + hex2(cfg[1]) + hex2(cfg[2]) + hex2(cfg[3]);
    if (fourccName === 'hvc1' || fourccName === 'hev1') {
      const profileSpace = cfg[1] >> 6, tier = (cfg[1] >> 5) & 1, profile = cfg[1] & 31;
      let compat = ((cfg[2] << 24) | (cfg[3] << 16) | (cfg[4] << 8) | cfg[5]) >>> 0, rev = 0;
      for (let i = 0; i < 32; i++) { rev = ((rev << 1) | (compat & 1)) >>> 0; compat >>>= 1; }
      const constraints = Array.from(cfg.subarray(6, 12));
      while (constraints.length && constraints[constraints.length - 1] === 0) constraints.pop();
      return fourccName + '.' + ['', 'A', 'B', 'C'][profileSpace] + profile + '.' + rev.toString(16).toUpperCase() +
        '.' + (tier ? 'H' : 'L') + cfg[12] + (constraints.length ? '.' + constraints.map(b => b.toString(16).toUpperCase()).join('.') : '');
    }
    if (fourccName === 'vp09') { // vpcC: 4 bytes version/flags, profile, level, bitDepth(4) chroma(3) range(1)
      return 'vp09.' + String(cfg[4]).padStart(2, '0') + '.' + String(cfg[5]).padStart(2, '0') + '.' + String(cfg[6] >> 4).padStart(2, '0');
    }
    return null;
  }

  function parseTrack(v, trak) {
    const tkhd = child(v, trak, 'tkhd'), mdia = child(v, trak, 'mdia');
    if (!tkhd || !mdia) return null;
    const mdhd = child(v, mdia, 'mdhd'), hdlr = child(v, mdia, 'hdlr'), minf = child(v, mdia, 'minf');
    if (!mdhd || !hdlr || !minf) return null;
    const handler = fourcc(v, hdlr.start + 8);
    const mdhdVersion = v.getUint8(mdhd.start);
    const timescale = mdhdVersion === 1 ? v.getUint32(mdhd.start + 20) : v.getUint32(mdhd.start + 12);
    const mediaDuration = mdhdVersion === 1 ? u64(v, mdhd.start + 24) : v.getUint32(mdhd.start + 16);
    const track = { handler, timescale, mediaDuration };
    // display matrix -> rotation (clockwise degrees as shown on screen)
    const m = v.getUint8(tkhd.start) === 1 ? tkhd.start + 52 : tkhd.start + 40;
    const a = v.getInt32(m) / 65536, b = v.getInt32(m + 4) / 65536;
    const angle = Math.round(Math.atan2(b, a) * 180 / Math.PI);
    track.rotation = ((angle % 360) + 360) % 360;
    if (![0, 90, 180, 270].includes(track.rotation)) track.rotation = 0;
    const stbl = child(v, minf, 'stbl');
    if (!stbl) return null;
    const stsd = child(v, stbl, 'stsd');
    const entry = stsd && listBoxes(v, stsd.start + 8, stsd.end)[0];
    track.sampleEntry = entry ? entry.type : null;
    if (handler === 'soun') return track;
    if (handler !== 'vide' || !entry) return null;

    const entryChildren = listBoxes(v, entry.start + 78, entry.end);
    track.width = v.getUint16(entry.start + 24);
    track.height = v.getUint16(entry.start + 26);
    const cfgBox = entryChildren.find(b => ['avcC', 'hvcC', 'vpcC'].includes(b.type));
    if (!cfgBox) throw new Unsupported('video codec ' + track.sampleEntry + ' is not supported by the fast export');
    const cfg = new Uint8Array(v.buffer, v.byteOffset + cfgBox.start, cfgBox.end - cfgBox.start);
    track.codec = codecFromConfig(track.sampleEntry, cfg);
    if (!track.codec) throw new Unsupported('video codec ' + track.sampleEntry + ' is not supported by the fast export');
    track.description = cfgBox.type === 'vpcC' ? null : new Uint8Array(cfg);
    track.configType = cfgBox.type;
    // 10-bit / HDR sources look wrong when drawn to an 8-bit canvas, so leave those to the FFmpeg path.
    if (cfgBox.type === 'avcC' && cfg[1] >= 100 && cfg[1] !== 100) track.highBitDepth = true; // High 10 / 4:2:2 / 4:4:4
    if (cfgBox.type === 'hvcC' && (cfg[1] & 31) !== 1) track.highBitDepth = true; // anything but Main
    if (cfgBox.type === 'vpcC' && (cfg[6] >> 4) > 8) track.highBitDepth = true;
    const colr = entryChildren.find(b => b.type === 'colr');
    if (colr && fourcc(v, colr.start) === 'nclx') {
      const transfer = v.getUint16(colr.start + 6);
      if (transfer === 16 || transfer === 18) track.hdr = true; // PQ / HLG
    }

    // ---- sample tables ----
    const stts = child(v, stbl, 'stts'), stsz = child(v, stbl, 'stsz'), stsc = child(v, stbl, 'stsc');
    const stco = child(v, stbl, 'stco') || child(v, stbl, 'co64'), ctts = child(v, stbl, 'ctts'), stss = child(v, stbl, 'stss');
    if (!stts || !stsz || !stsc || !stco) throw new Unsupported('incomplete MP4 sample tables');
    const count = v.getUint32(stsz.start + 8);
    const fixed = v.getUint32(stsz.start + 4);
    const sizes = new Uint32Array(count);
    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) sizes[i] = fixed || v.getUint32(stsz.start + 12 + i * 4);

    const dts = new Float64Array(count), cts = new Float64Array(count);
    let n = 0, t = 0;
    const sttsCount = v.getUint32(stts.start + 4);
    for (let e = 0; e < sttsCount && n < count; e++) {
      const c = v.getUint32(stts.start + 8 + e * 8), d = v.getUint32(stts.start + 12 + e * 8);
      for (let k = 0; k < c && n < count; k++) { dts[n++] = t; t += d; }
    }
    if (n !== count) throw new Unsupported('inconsistent MP4 timing table');
    cts.set(dts);
    if (ctts) {
      const signed = v.getUint8(ctts.start) === 1, cc = v.getUint32(ctts.start + 4);
      let idx = 0;
      for (let e = 0; e < cc && idx < count; e++) {
        const c = v.getUint32(ctts.start + 8 + e * 8);
        const off = signed ? v.getInt32(ctts.start + 12 + e * 8) : v.getUint32(ctts.start + 12 + e * 8);
        for (let k = 0; k < c && idx < count; k++, idx++) cts[idx] += off;
      }
    }

    const key = new Uint8Array(count);
    if (stss) {
      const kc = v.getUint32(stss.start + 4);
      for (let i = 0; i < kc; i++) { const s = v.getUint32(stss.start + 8 + i * 4) - 1; if (s >= 0 && s < count) key[s] = 1; }
    } else key.fill(1);

    const is64 = stco.type === 'co64', chunkCount = v.getUint32(stco.start + 4);
    const stscCount = v.getUint32(stsc.start + 4);
    const offsets = new Float64Array(count);
    let s = 0;
    for (let e = 0; e < stscCount; e++) {
      const first = v.getUint32(stsc.start + 8 + e * 12), per = v.getUint32(stsc.start + 12 + e * 12);
      const last = e + 1 < stscCount ? v.getUint32(stsc.start + 8 + (e + 1) * 12) - 1 : chunkCount;
      for (let ch = first; ch <= last; ch++) {
        let off = is64 ? u64(v, stco.start + 8 + (ch - 1) * 8) : v.getUint32(stco.start + 8 + (ch - 1) * 4);
        for (let k = 0; k < per && s < count; k++) { offsets[s] = off; off += sizes[s]; s++; }
      }
    }
    if (s !== count) throw new Unsupported('inconsistent MP4 chunk table');

    // edit list: the first real edit's media_time is where presentation starts
    let editStart = 0;
    const edts = child(v, trak, 'edts'), elst = edts && child(v, edts, 'elst');
    if (elst) {
      const ver = v.getUint8(elst.start), ec = v.getUint32(elst.start + 4);
      for (let e = 0; e < ec; e++) {
        const base = elst.start + 8 + e * (ver === 1 ? 20 : 12);
        const mediaTime = ver === 1 ? Number(v.getBigInt64(base + 8)) : v.getInt32(base + 4);
        if (mediaTime >= 0) { editStart = mediaTime; break; }
      }
    }
    const pts = new Float64Array(count);
    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) { pts[i] = (cts[i] - editStart) / timescale; dts[i] = dts[i] / timescale; }
    let maxEnd = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) if (pts[i] > maxEnd) maxEnd = pts[i];
    Object.assign(track, { count, sizes, offsets, key, pts, dts, duration: maxEnd });
    return track;
  }

  /** Reads an MP4/MOV and returns { video, hasAudio }. Throws Unsupported for anything the fast path should not touch. */
  async function demux(file) {
    const buf = await readMoov(file);
    const v = new DataView(buf);
    const moov = { start: 0, end: buf.byteLength };
    let video = null, hasAudio = false;
    for (const trak of childrenOf(v, moov, 'trak')) {
      const t = parseTrack(v, trak);
      if (!t) continue;
      if (t.handler === 'vide' && !video) video = t;
      else if (t.handler === 'soun') hasAudio = true;
    }
    if (!video) throw new Unsupported('no video track');
    if (video.highBitDepth || video.hdr) throw new Unsupported('10-bit or HDR video');
    if (video.count < 1) throw new Unsupported('video track has no frames');
    return { video, hasAudio };
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------
     2. H.264 helpers: avcC parsing and AVCC -> Annex B conversion
     ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  function parseAvcC(d) {
    const u8 = d instanceof Uint8Array ? d : new Uint8Array(d);
    const nalLength = (u8[4] & 3) + 1;
    let p = 6;
    const sps = [], pps = [];
    for (let i = 0, n = u8[5] & 31; i < n; i++) { const len = (u8[p] << 8) | u8[p + 1]; sps.push(u8.subarray(p + 2, p + 2 + len)); p += 2 + len; }
    const ppsCount = u8[p++];
    for (let i = 0; i < ppsCount; i++) { const len = (u8[p] << 8) | u8[p + 1]; pps.push(u8.subarray(p + 2, p + 2 + len)); p += 2 + len; }
    return { nalLength, sps, pps };
  }

  /** Converts one length-prefixed H.264 sample to Annex B (00 00 00 01 start codes). When `params` is given
      (key frames), SPS and PPS are written first so every key frame is a valid stream start. */
  function avccToAnnexB(sample, nalLength, params) {
    const data = sample instanceof Uint8Array ? sample : new Uint8Array(sample);
    let total = 0, p = 0;
    const lens = [];
    while (p + nalLength <= data.length) {
      let len = 0;
      for (let i = 0; i < nalLength; i++) len = len * 256 + data[p + i];
      p += nalLength;
      if (len <= 0 || p + len > data.length) break;
      lens.push([p, len]); total += 4 + len; p += len;
    }
    let extra = 0;
    if (params) for (const x of [...params.sps, ...params.pps]) extra += 4 + x.length;
    const out = new Uint8Array(extra + total);
    let o = 0;
    const startCode = () => { out[o++] = 0; out[o++] = 0; out[o++] = 0; out[o++] = 1; };
    if (params) for (const x of [...params.sps, ...params.pps]) { startCode(); out.set(x, o); o += x.length; }
    for (const [start, len] of lens) { startCode(); out.set(data.subarray(start, start + len), o); o += len; }
    return out;
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------
     3. Planning helpers (pure, unit-tested)
     ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  /** Output frame count for a clip that lasts `seconds` on the timeline. Matches `-t` + the fps filter. */
  const frameCount = (seconds, fps) => Math.max(1, Math.round(seconds * fps));
  // Quantize cumulative timeline boundaries, not each clip independently. This prevents
  // fractional-frame rounding from adding one extra frame per cut in long projects.
  function planFrameCounts(durations, fps) {
    const rate = Number(fps);
    if (!Array.isArray(durations) || !Number.isFinite(rate) || rate <= 0) return [];
    let timelineSeconds = 0, previousFrame = 0;
    return durations.map(value => {
      const duration = Number(value);
      timelineSeconds += Number.isFinite(duration) && duration > 0 ? duration : 0;
      const boundaryFrame = Math.round(timelineSeconds * rate);
      const frames = Math.max(1, boundaryFrame - previousFrame);
      previousFrame += frames;
      return frames;
    });
  }

  /** Source time (seconds) below which the source frame for output slot k is chosen. Same rule as FFmpeg's `fps` filter (round to nearest). */
  const sourceLimit = (clip, k, fps) => clip.in + ((k + 0.5) / fps) * (clip.speed || 1);

  /** Level_idc for H.264 at this size and rate (Annex A limits for MaxFS and MaxMBPS). */
  function avcLevelIdc(W, H, fps) {
    const mbs = Math.ceil(W / 16) * Math.ceil(H / 16), mbps = mbs * fps;
    const levels = [[30, 1620, 40500], [31, 3600, 108000], [40, 8192, 245760], [42, 8704, 522240], [50, 22080, 589824], [51, 36864, 983040], [52, 36864, 2073600]];
    for (const [idc, maxFs, maxMbps] of levels) if (mbs <= maxFs && mbps <= maxMbps) return idc;
    return 52;
  }

  /** Bits per second: about what libx264 -crf 28 produces, never absurd. */
  function targetBitrate(W, H, fps) {
    return Math.round(Math.min(40e6, Math.max(1.5e6, W * H * fps * 0.09)));
  }

  /** Rectangle (output pixels) a video of displayW x displayH occupies when scaled to fit and centered, like scale(decrease)+pad. */
  function fitRect(displayW, displayH, W, H) {
    const sc = Math.min(W / displayW, H / displayH);
    const w = Math.min(W, even(displayW * sc)), h = Math.min(H, even(displayH * sc));
    return { x: Math.floor((W - w) / 2 / 2) * 2, y: Math.floor((H - h) / 2 / 2) * 2, w, h };
  }

  /** Decode range for one clip: first sample to feed (a key frame at or before `in`) and last sample needed. */
  function clipSampleRange(track, clip, frames, fps) {
    const limit = sourceLimit(clip, frames - 1, fps);
    let start = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < track.count; i++) if (track.key[i] && track.pts[i] <= clip.in + 1e-4) start = i;
    let end = start;
    for (let i = start; i < track.count; i++) if (track.pts[i] < limit + 1e-4) end = i; // feeding one extra frame is harmless
    return { start, end };
  }

  /** Why the fast path cannot be used for this project, or null when it can. `project` carries plain booleans. */
  function ineligibleReason(project) {
    if (!project || !project.clips || !project.clips.length) return 'no clips';
    for (const [i, c] of project.clips.entries()) {
      if (c.reverse) return `clip ${i + 1} is reversed`;
      if (c.effect) return `clip ${i + 1} has a filter`;
      if (c.adjust) return `clip ${i + 1} has color adjustments`;
      if (c.transition) return `clip ${i + 1} has a transition`;
    }
    for (const o of project.overlays || []) {
      if (o.type === 'video') return 'a video overlay is present';
      if (o.blend && o.blend !== 'normal') return 'an overlay uses a blend mode';
    }
    return null;
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------
     4. Encoder configuration and self-test (browser only)
     ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  function requireWebCodecs() {
    const g = typeof self !== 'undefined' ? self : globalThis;
    if (typeof g.VideoEncoder !== 'function' || typeof g.VideoDecoder !== 'function' || typeof g.EncodedVideoChunk !== 'function' || typeof g.VideoFrame !== 'function') {
      throw new Unsupported('this browser has no WebCodecs video support');
    }
    if (typeof g.OffscreenCanvas !== 'function' && typeof g.document === 'undefined') throw new Unsupported('no canvas available');
    if (typeof g.createImageBitmap !== 'function') throw new Unsupported('no createImageBitmap');
  }

  function h264Candidates(W, H, fps) {
    const level = hex2(avcLevelIdc(W, H, fps));
    return ['64', '4d', '42'].map(profile => 'avc1.' + profile + '00' + level); // High, Main, Baseline
  }

  /** First encoder configuration the device accepts, preferring hardware. Returns { config, codec, acceleration } or throws Unsupported. */
  async function chooseEncoderConfig(opts) {
    requireWebCodecs();
    const { W, H, fps } = opts;
    const bitrate = opts.bitrate || targetBitrate(W, H, fps);
    const codecs = opts.codecs || h264Candidates(W, H, fps);
    // Some phone encoders reject optional settings (variable bitrate, quality mode); retry with fewer of them before giving up on a codec.
    const variants = [{ bitrateMode: 'variable', latencyMode: 'quality' }, { bitrateMode: 'variable' }, {}];
    for (const acceleration of opts.acceleration || ['prefer-hardware', 'no-preference']) {
      for (const codec of codecs) {
        for (const extra of variants) {
          const cfg = Object.assign({ codec, width: W, height: H, bitrate, framerate: fps, hardwareAcceleration: acceleration }, extra);
          try {
            const r = await VideoEncoder.isConfigSupported(cfg);
            if (r && r.supported) return { config: cfg, codec, acceleration };
          } catch (_) { /* try the next one */ }
        }
      }
    }
    throw new Unsupported('this device has no usable video encoder for ' + W + 'x' + H + ' at ' + fps + ' fps');
  }

  /** Encodes a few synthetic frames and reports real speed, so a device whose "fast" encoder is slow is detected up front. */
  async function benchmarkEncoder(choice, frames = 12, timeoutMs = 15000) {
    const { config } = choice, W = config.width, H = config.height;
    const canvas = typeof OffscreenCanvas === 'function' ? new OffscreenCanvas(W, H) : Object.assign(document.createElement('canvas'), { width: W, height: H });
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    let err = null, outputs = 0;
    const enc = new VideoEncoder({ output: () => { outputs++; }, error: e => { err = e; } });
    const t0 = performance.now();
    const deadline = t0 + timeoutMs;
    const remaining = label => {
      const ms = deadline - performance.now();
      if (ms <= 0) throw new Error('Timed out: encoder self-test ' + label);
      return ms;
    };
    try {
      enc.configure(config);
      for (let i = 0; i < frames; i++) {
        if (err) throw new Unsupported('encoder self-test failed: ' + (err.message || err));
        ctx.fillStyle = `hsl(${(i * 37) % 360},70%,50%)`; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
        ctx.fillStyle = '#fff'; ctx.fillRect((i * 17) % W, H / 3, W / 4, H / 4);
        const f = new VideoFrame(canvas, { timestamp: Math.round(i * 1e6 / config.framerate) });
        try { enc.encode(f, { keyFrame: i === 0 }); } finally { f.close(); }
        while (enc.encodeQueueSize > 4) {
          await withTimeout(new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 2)), remaining('waiting for encoder queue'));
        }
      }
      await withTimeout(enc.flush(), remaining('flush'));
      if (err) throw new Unsupported('encoder self-test failed: ' + (err.message || err));
      if (outputs < 1) throw new Unsupported('encoder self-test produced no output');
      const ms = performance.now() - t0;
      return { fps: frames / (ms / 1000), ms };
    } finally {
      // A stuck encoder must not keep the export pipeline alive or retain device resources.
      try { enc.close(); } catch (_) { /* already closed or failed to configure */ }
    }
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------
     5. Sources: ranged file reads and a decoder that yields frames in display order
     ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  class RangeReader {
    constructor(file, windowBytes = 8 * 1024 * 1024) { this.file = file; this.win = windowBytes; this.buf = null; this.start = 0; this.end = 0; }
    async read(offset, size) {
      if (this.buf && offset >= this.start && offset + size <= this.end) return this.buf.subarray(offset - this.start, offset - this.start + size);
      const end = Math.min(this.file.size, offset + Math.max(size, this.win));
      this.buf = new Uint8Array(await this.file.slice(offset, end).arrayBuffer());
      this.start = offset; this.end = end;
      return this.buf.subarray(0, size);
    }
  }

  function withTimeout(promise, ms, label) {
    let timer;
    const timeout = new Promise((_, reject) => { timer = setTimeout(() => reject(new Error('Timed out: ' + label)), ms); });
    return Promise.race([promise, timeout]).finally(() => clearTimeout(timer));
  }

  /** True when this device can decode the track (checked up front so an unsupported phone never starts a long render). */
  async function checkDecoder(track) {
    requireWebCodecs();
    const cfg = { codec: track.codec, codedWidth: track.width, codedHeight: track.height };
    if (track.description) cfg.description = track.description;
    try { const r = await VideoDecoder.isConfigSupported(cfg); return !!(r && r.supported); } catch (_) { return false; }
  }

  class ClipDecoder {
    constructor(track, reader, range, opts = {}) {
      this.track = track; this.reader = reader; this.range = range;
      this.q = []; this.waiters = []; this.gates = [];
      this.err = null; this.done = false; this.closed = false;
      this.maxQueued = opts.maxQueued || 3;
      this.timeoutMs = opts.timeoutMs || 30000;
      this.decodeAcceleration = opts.acceleration || 'no-preference';
      this.dec = new VideoDecoder({
        output: frame => { if (this.closed) frame.close(); else { this.q.push(frame); this._wake(); } },
        error: e => { this.err = e; this._wake(); this._openGate(); }
      });
      this.dec.ondequeue = () => this._openGate();
    }
    async start() {
      const t = this.track;
      const cfg = { codec: t.codec, codedWidth: t.width, codedHeight: t.height, hardwareAcceleration: this.decodeAcceleration, optimizeForLatency: true };
      if (t.description) cfg.description = t.description;
      const support = await VideoDecoder.isConfigSupported(cfg);
      if (!support || !support.supported) throw new Unsupported('this device cannot decode ' + t.codec);
      this.dec.configure(cfg);
      this._feed();
    }
    _wake() { const w = this.waiters; this.waiters = []; for (const r of w) r(); }
    _openGate() { const g = this.gates; this.gates = []; for (const r of g) r(); }
    async _feed() {
      const t = this.track;
      try {
        for (let i = this.range.start; i <= this.range.end && !this.closed; i++) {
          while (!this.closed && !this.err && (this.dec.decodeQueueSize > 6 || this.q.length >= this.maxQueued)) {
            await withTimeout(new Promise(r => this.gates.push(r)), this.timeoutMs, 'decoder did not accept more input');
          }
          if (this.closed || this.err) return;
          const data = await this.reader.read(t.offsets[i], t.sizes[i]);
          this.dec.decode(new EncodedVideoChunk({
            type: t.key[i] ? 'key' : 'delta',
            timestamp: Math.round(t.pts[i] * 1e6) + TS_BIAS_US,
            data
          }));
        }
        if (!this.closed && !this.err) await withTimeout(this.dec.flush(), this.timeoutMs, 'decoder flush');
        this.done = true;
      } catch (e) {
        if (!this.closed) this.err = e;
      } finally { this._wake(); }
    }
    /** Next decoded frame in display order as { frame, pts } (pts in source seconds), or null at the end. */
    async next() {
      for (;;) {
        if (this.q.length) { const frame = this.q.shift(); this._openGate(); return { frame, pts: (frame.timestamp - TS_BIAS_US) / 1e6 }; }
        if (this.err) throw this.err;
        if (this.done) return null;
        await withTimeout(new Promise(r => this.waiters.push(r)), this.timeoutMs, 'decoder produced no frame');
      }
    }
    close() {
      this.closed = true;
      try { if (this.dec.state !== 'closed') this.dec.close(); } catch (_) { /* already closed */ }
      for (const f of this.q) { try { f.close(); } catch (_) { /* ignore */ } }
      this.q = []; this._openGate(); this._wake();
    }
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------
     6. Output sinks
     ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  /** Collects H.264 as an Annex B elementary stream (what `ffmpeg -f h264` reads). */
  class AnnexBSink {
    constructor(maxBytes = Infinity) {
      this.parts = []; this.bytes = 0; this.frameCount = 0; this.params = null; this.nalLength = 4;
      this.maxBytes = Number.isFinite(maxBytes) && maxBytes > 0 ? maxBytes : Infinity;
    }
    onChunk(chunk, meta) {
      // A rendered stream is temporarily held as individual JS chunks, then as one
      // contiguous Uint8Array, and then copied into FFmpeg.wasm. Refuse to grow
      // beyond the caller's memory budget rather than risking a WebView OOM.
      if (this.bytes + chunk.byteLength > this.maxBytes) {
        const error = new Unsupported('device video output exceeded its safe in-memory buffer budget'); error.code = 'PIECE_BUDGET_EXCEEDED'; throw error;
      }
      if (meta && meta.decoderConfig && meta.decoderConfig.description) {
        const parsed = parseAvcC(meta.decoderConfig.description);
        this.params = parsed; this.nalLength = parsed.nalLength;
      }
      const raw = new Uint8Array(chunk.byteLength);
      chunk.copyTo(raw);
      let out;
      if (this.params) out = avccToAnnexB(raw, this.nalLength, chunk.type === 'key' ? this.params : null);
      else out = raw; // encoder already produced Annex B
      if (this.bytes + out.length > this.maxBytes) {
        const error = new Unsupported('device video output exceeded its safe in-memory buffer budget'); error.code = 'PIECE_BUDGET_EXCEEDED'; throw error;
      }
      this.parts.push(out); this.bytes += out.length; this.frameCount++;
    }
    finish() {
      const all = new Uint8Array(this.bytes);
      let o = 0;
      for (const p of this.parts) { all.set(p, o); o += p.length; }
      this.parts = [];
      return all;
    }
  }

  /** IVF container, used by tests to inspect VP8/VP9 output with ffmpeg. Not used in production. */
  class IvfSink {
    constructor(fourccName, W, H, fps) { this.fourcc = fourccName; this.W = W; this.H = H; this.fps = fps; this.frames = []; }
    onChunk(chunk) { const d = new Uint8Array(chunk.byteLength); chunk.copyTo(d); this.frames.push({ d, ts: chunk.timestamp }); }
    finish() {
      const size = 32 + this.frames.reduce((s, f) => s + 12 + f.d.length, 0);
      const out = new Uint8Array(size), v = new DataView(out.buffer);
      out.set([0x44, 0x4b, 0x49, 0x46]); v.setUint16(4, 0, true); v.setUint16(6, 32, true);
      for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) out[8 + i] = this.fourcc.charCodeAt(i);
      v.setUint16(12, this.W, true); v.setUint16(14, this.H, true); v.setUint32(16, this.fps, true); v.setUint32(20, 1, true); v.setUint32(24, this.frames.length, true);
      let o = 32;
      this.frames.forEach((f, i) => { v.setUint32(o, f.d.length, true); v.setBigUint64(o + 4, BigInt(i), true); out.set(f.d, o + 12); o += 12 + f.d.length; });
      return out;
    }
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------
     7. The render loop
     ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  /**
   * Renders the main timeline to an encoded elementary stream.
   * opts.clips: [{ file, track, in, out, speed, fade }]  (track from demux().video)
   * opts.overlays: [{ bitmap, x, y, w, h, start, end }]  start/end in timeline seconds, x/y/w/h in output pixels
   * opts.choice: result of chooseEncoderConfig; opts.sink: AnnexBSink (or IvfSink in tests)
   * Resolves { frames, clipFrames:[n...], bytes, ms }.
   */
  async function renderTimeline(opts) {
    requireWebCodecs();
    const { clips, W, H, fps, choice, sink } = opts;
    const overlays = opts.overlays || [];
    const onProgress = opts.onProgress || (() => {});
    const isCancelled = opts.isCancelled || (() => false);
    const timeoutMs = opts.timeoutMs || 30000;
    const canvas = typeof OffscreenCanvas === 'function' ? new OffscreenCanvas(W, H) : Object.assign(document.createElement('canvas'), { width: W, height: H });
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d', { alpha: false });
    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = true; ctx.imageSmoothingQuality = 'high';

    let encErr = null, encoderWaiters = [], encoder = null;

    const plan = clips.map(c => ({ c, td: (c.out - c.in) / (c.speed || 1) }));
    const plannedFrames = planFrameCounts(plan.map(p => p.td), fps);
    plan.forEach((p, i) => { p.frames = plannedFrames[i]; });
    const totalFrames = plan.reduce((s, p) => s + p.frames, 0);
    const gop = Math.max(1, Math.round(fps * 2));
    const frameUs = Math.round(1e6 / fps);
    let globalFrame = 0, timelineOffset = 0;
    const t0 = performance.now();
    const cleanup = [];
    try {
      // Configure inside the guarded region so even a rejected configuration closes the encoder.
      encoder = new VideoEncoder({
        output: (chunk, meta) => { try { sink.onChunk(chunk, meta); } catch (e) { encErr = e; } },
        error: e => { encErr = e; }
      });
      encoder.ondequeue = () => { const w = encoderWaiters; encoderWaiters = []; for (const r of w) r(); };
      encoder.configure(choice.config);
      for (const p of plan) {
        const { c } = p;
        const track = c.track, reader = new RangeReader(c.file);
        const range = clipSampleRange(track, c, p.frames, fps);
        const decoder = new ClipDecoder(track, reader, range, { timeoutMs });
        cleanup.push(() => decoder.close());
        await decoder.start();
        const rot = track.rotation, swap = rot === 90 || rot === 270;
        const fd = Math.min(0.4, Math.max(0.05, p.td / 2));
        let cur = null, nxt = null;
        try {
          nxt = await decoder.next();
          for (let k = 0; k < p.frames; k++) {
          if (isCancelled()) throw new Error('Export cancelled');
          if (encErr) throw encErr;
          const limit = sourceLimit(c, k, fps);
          // A frame exactly on the half-slot boundary belongs to the NEXT slot (FFmpeg rounds .5 up). Timestamps are whole
          // microseconds, so allow for rounding: without this, 60 fps footage exported at 30 fps picks the wrong neighbour.
          while (nxt && nxt.pts < limit - 2e-6) { if (cur) cur.frame.close(); cur = nxt; nxt = await decoder.next(); }
          if (!cur && nxt) { cur = nxt; nxt = await decoder.next(); }
          if (!cur) throw new Error('The video has no decodable frames in the selected range.');

          const tClip = k / fps;
          let alpha = 1;
          const fadeIn = c.fadeIn == null ? !!c.fade : !!c.fadeIn;
          const fadeOut = c.fadeOut == null ? !!c.fade : !!c.fadeOut;
          if (fadeIn || fadeOut) alpha = Math.max(0, Math.min(1,
            fadeIn ? tClip / fd : 1, fadeOut ? (p.td - tClip) / fd : 1));
          ctx.globalAlpha = 1; ctx.fillStyle = '#000'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
          const f = cur.frame;
          const dw = swap ? f.displayHeight : f.displayWidth, dh = swap ? f.displayWidth : f.displayHeight;
          const r = fitRect(dw, dh, W, H);
          ctx.save();
          ctx.globalAlpha = alpha;
          ctx.translate(r.x + r.w / 2, r.y + r.h / 2);
          ctx.rotate(rot * Math.PI / 180);
          const pw = swap ? r.h : r.w, ph = swap ? r.w : r.h;
          ctx.drawImage(f, -pw / 2, -ph / 2, pw, ph);
          ctx.restore();
          ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
          const tl = timelineOffset + tClip;
          for (const o of overlays) {
            if (tl >= o.start - 1e-6 && tl <= o.end + 1e-6) ctx.drawImage(o.bitmap, o.x, o.y, o.w, o.h);
          }

          const vf = new VideoFrame(canvas, { timestamp: globalFrame * frameUs, duration: frameUs });
          try { encoder.encode(vf, { keyFrame: globalFrame % gop === 0 }); } finally { vf.close(); }
          globalFrame++;
          while (!encErr && encoder.encodeQueueSize > 6) {
            await withTimeout(new Promise(res => encoderWaiters.push(res)), timeoutMs, 'video encoder stopped accepting frames');
          }
          if (globalFrame % 8 === 0 || globalFrame === totalFrames) onProgress(globalFrame / totalFrames);
          }
        } finally {
          // Frames already handed out by decoder.next() are no longer in its internal queue.
          // Close both even when rendering is cancelled or a later decode/encode operation throws.
          if (cur) { try { cur.frame.close(); } catch (_) { /* already closed */ } }
          if (nxt) { try { nxt.frame.close(); } catch (_) { /* already closed */ } }
          decoder.close();
        }
        timelineOffset += p.td;
      }
      await withTimeout(encoder.flush(), timeoutMs * 2, 'video encoder flush');
      if (encErr) throw encErr;
      if (Number.isFinite(sink.frameCount) && sink.frameCount !== globalFrame) {
        throw new Unsupported(`device encoder emitted ${sink.frameCount} output frames for ${globalFrame} submitted frames`);
      }
    } finally {
      for (const fn of cleanup) { try { fn(); } catch (_) { /* ignore */ } }
      try { if (encoder && encoder.state !== 'closed') encoder.close(); } catch (_) { /* ignore */ }
    }
    return { frames: globalFrame, clipFrames: plan.map(p => p.frames), ms: performance.now() - t0, totalFrames };
  }

  /** Crops a full-canvas transparent overlay (the PNG of the whole editor canvas) to the pixels that matter, so 100 captions
      do not cost 100 full frames of memory. outW x outH is the exported video size; the PNG is stretched over the whole
      frame exactly like FFmpeg's scale=W:H did. Returns null for a fully transparent overlay. */
  async function overlayFromPng(pngBytes, outW, outH) {
    const full = await createImageBitmap(new Blob([pngBytes], { type: 'image/png' }));
    const width = full.width, height = full.height;
    const probe = typeof OffscreenCanvas === 'function' ? new OffscreenCanvas(width, height) : Object.assign(document.createElement('canvas'), { width, height });
    const pctx = probe.getContext('2d', { willReadFrequently: true });
    pctx.drawImage(full, 0, 0);
    const data = pctx.getImageData(0, 0, width, height).data;
    let x0 = width, y0 = height, x1 = -1, y1 = -1;
    for (let y = 0; y < height; y++) {
      for (let x = 0, i = y * width * 4 + 3; x < width; x++, i += 4) {
        if (data[i] !== 0) { if (x < x0) x0 = x; if (x > x1) x1 = x; if (y < y0) y0 = y; if (y > y1) y1 = y; }
      }
    }
    if (x1 < 0) { full.close(); return null; }
    const cw = x1 - x0 + 1, ch = y1 - y0 + 1;
    const bitmap = await createImageBitmap(full, x0, y0, cw, ch);
    full.close();
    const sx = outW / width, sy = outH / height;
    const x = Math.floor(x0 * sx), y = Math.floor(y0 * sy);
    return { bitmap, x, y, w: Math.max(1, Math.min(outW - x, Math.ceil((x0 + cw) * sx) - x)), h: Math.max(1, Math.min(outH - y, Math.ceil((y0 + ch) * sy) - y)) };
  }

  return {
    Unsupported, demux, parseAvcC, avccToAnnexB, codecFromConfig,
    frameCount, planFrameCounts, sourceLimit, avcLevelIdc, targetBitrate, fitRect, clipSampleRange, ineligibleReason,
    chooseEncoderConfig, benchmarkEncoder, checkDecoder, renderTimeline, overlayFromPng,
    AnnexBSink, IvfSink, RangeReader, ClipDecoder
  };
}));

FILE: scripts/fetch_speech_engine.sh
BLOB SHA: 64fba5dbb8a879300f5d56df9ab066e1450eb7ed
================================================================================
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Downloads the pinned speech engine files (library + ONNX Runtime WASM) from the npm registry and verifies
# their SHA-256 checksums against vendor/transformers/SHA256SUMS. Nothing is trusted unless the checksums match.
# Used by the CI workflows before the tests and before the APK web bundle is assembled.
# Local use:  bash scripts/fetch_speech_engine.sh
set -euo pipefail
cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."
VERSION="2.17.2"
DEST="vendor/transformers"
mkdir -p "$DEST"
if (cd "$DEST" && sha256sum -c SHA256SUMS >/dev/null 2>&1); then
  echo "Speech engine files already present and verified."
  exit 0
fi
TMP="$(mktemp -d)"
trap 'rm -rf "$TMP"' EXIT
(cd "$TMP" && npm pack "@xenova/transformers@${VERSION}" --silent >/dev/null && tar -xzf ./*.tgz)
for f in transformers.min.js ort-wasm-simd.wasm ort-wasm.wasm; do cp "$TMP/package/dist/$f" "$DEST/$f"; done
cp "$TMP/package/LICENSE" "$DEST/LICENSE"
(cd "$DEST" && sha256sum -c SHA256SUMS)
echo "Speech engine ${VERSION} downloaded and verified."

      </section>
      <section class="hm-library" aria-labelledby="hmLibraryHeading">

      <footer class="hm-footer"><span>Stored locally on this device.</span><a href="./legal.html">Terms, privacy &amp; licenses</a></footer>
    </div>`;
  document.body.appendChild(home);
  if (window.MediaYIcons) MediaYIcons.render(document);

  const setup = document.createElement('section'); setup.id = 'setup'; setup.setAttribute('aria-label', 'New project'); setup.hidden = true;
  setup.innerHTML = `
    <div class="su-wrap">
      <button id="suBack" class="su-back">← Home</button>
      <div class="su-head"><div class="empty-logo"><img src="./favicon.svg" alt="" aria-hidden="true"></div><h1>Set up your project</h1><p>Choose a format and export settings.</p></div>
      <div><p class="su-label">Video shape</p><div id="suShape" class="su-shapes"></div></div>
      <div><p class="su-label">Export quality</p><div id="suRes" class="su-row q5"></div><p id="suHint" class="su-hint"></p></div>
      <div><p class="su-label">Frames per second</p><div id="suFps" class="su-row"></div></div>
      <div class="su-foot"><p id="suSum"></p><button id="suStart" class="primary wide">Start editing <span aria-hidden="true">→</span></button></div>
    </div>`;
  document.body.appendChild(setup);

  const q = id => setup.querySelector('#' + id);
  const hasContent = (st = S) => !!(st && ((st.main || []).length || (st.overlays || []).length || (st.audio || []).length));
  const hasVideo = (st = S) => !!(st && (st.main || []).length);
  const visibleName = st => {
    const clip = (st.main || []).find(c => c && c.name) || (st.overlays || []).find(c => c && c.name) || (st.audio || []).find(c => c && c.name);
    return clip && clip.name ? clip.name.replace(/\.[^.]+$/, '').slice(0, 80) : 'Untitled project';
  };
  const niceName = v => String(v || 'Untitled project').replace(/[\\/:*?"<>|]/g, '-').trim().slice(0, 80) || 'Untitled project';

  function renderSetup() {
    q('suShape').innerHTML = SHAPES.map(s => `<button type="button" data-k="r" data-v="${s.v}" class="${s.v === pick.r ? 'on' : ''}"><i style="width:${s.w}px;height:${s.h}px"></i><b>${s.name}</b><small>${s.sub}</small></button>`).join('');
    q('suRes').innerHTML = QUALITY.map(o => `<button type="button" data-k="res" data-v="${o.v}" class="${o.v === pick.res ? 'on' : ''}"><b>${o.name}</b><small>${o.sub}</small></button>`).join('');
    q('suFps').innerHTML = FPS.map(o => `<button type="button" data-k="fps" data-v="${o.v}" class="${o.v === pick.fps ? 'on' : ''}"><b>${o.name}</b><small>${o.sub}</small></button>`).join('');
    q('suHint').textContent = pick.res >= 1440 ? '2K/4K exports can be slow or run out of memory on phones.' : pick.res === 1080 ? 'Recommended for a clear HD export.' : 'A smaller file and quicker export.';
    q('suSum').textContent = `${pick.r}  ·  ${QUALITY.find(o => o.v === pick.res).name}  ·  ${pick.fps} fps`;
  }
  setup.addEventListener('click', e => {
    const b = e.target.closest('button[data-k]'); if (!b) return;
    pick[b.dataset.k] = b.dataset.k === 'r' ? b.dataset.v : Number(b.dataset.v); renderSetup();
  });

  function showEditor() {
    home.hidden = true; setup.hidden = true;
    const brand = $('brandHome'); if (brand) brand.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
  }
  function showSetup() {
    pick.r = SHAPES.some(s => s.v === (S.ratio || $('aspectRatio').value)) ? (S.ratio || $('aspectRatio').value) : pick.r;
    home.hidden = true; setup.hidden = false; renderSetup();
  }
  async function applySetup() {
    try { localStorage.setItem(PREFKEY, JSON.stringify(pick)); } catch (_) {}
    EXP.res = pick.res; EXP.fps = pick.fps;
    [['resList', pick.res], ['fpsList', pick.fps]].forEach(([id, v]) => {
      const root = $(id), b = root && root.querySelector(`[data-v="${v}"]`); if (b) b.click();
    });
    const ratio = $('aspectRatio');
    if (ratio && ratio.value !== pick.r) { ratio.value = pick.r; ratio.dispatchEvent(new Event('change')); }
    else if (S.ratio !== pick.r) { S.ratio = pick.r; await rebuildCanvas(); commit(); }
    await saveProject();
    showEditor();
    try { say(`${pick.r} · ${pick.res}p · ${pick.fps} fps. Add media to begin.`); } catch (_) {}
  }
  q('suStart').onclick = applySetup;
  q('suBack').onclick = async () => { await openHome(); };

  // If the user switches away from an unnamed session, give it a real project record first.
  async function preserveCurrentProject() {
    if (activeProjectId === 'current' && hasContent(S)) {
      const id = `project-${uid()}`, name = niceName(visibleName(S)), state = snap(), updated = Date.now();
      await db.projects.put({ id, name, state, updated, kind: 'project' });
      activeProjectId = id; activeProjectName = name;
    }
    await saveProject();
  }

  async function openSavedProject(id) {
    try {
      await preserveCurrentProject();
      const row = await db.projects.get(id);
      if (!row || !row.state) return say('That saved project could not be found.');
      activeProjectId = row.id; activeProjectName = niceName(row.name || visibleName(JSON.parse(row.state)));
      clearTimeout(saveTimer);
      await restore(row.state); t = 0; undoStack = [snap()]; redoStack = [];
      if (S.main.length) await seek(0); refresh(0); await saveProject();
      closeSheet(); showEditor(); say(`Opened “${activeProjectName}”.`);
    } catch (err) { console.error(err); say('Could not open this project: ' + (err.message || err)); }
  }

  async function collectUnreferencedMedia() {
    // Flush the current editor state before calculating references, or a pending edit could lose its media.
    await saveProject();
    const projects = await db.projects.toArray();
    const referenced = new Set();
    for (const row of projects) {
      if (!row || !row.state) continue;
      let state;
      try { state = typeof row.state === 'string' ? JSON.parse(row.state) : row.state; }
      catch (_) {
        console.warn('Media cleanup skipped because a saved project state could not be read.');
        return 0; // Unknown references must never cause user media to be deleted.
      }
      if (!state || typeof state !== 'object' ||
          !Array.isArray(state.main) || !Array.isArray(state.audio) || !Array.isArray(state.overlays)) {
        console.warn('Media cleanup skipped because a saved project has an unsupported state shape.');
        return 0; // Older or partial project records are treated as unknown references.
      }
      for (const lane of [state.main, state.audio, state.overlays]) {
        if (!Array.isArray(lane)) continue;
        for (const item of lane) if (item && item.mediaId) referenced.add(item.mediaId);
      }
    }
    // Saved templates live in localStorage rather than the projects table. Treat an
    // unreadable or unknown template schema as a reason to skip collection: deleting
    // media while references are uncertain would permanently break a user's template.
    const templateRaw = localStorage.getItem('pmy-templates');
    if (templateRaw !== null) {
      let templates;
      try { templates = JSON.parse(templateRaw); }
      catch (_) {
        console.warn('Media cleanup skipped because saved templates could not be read.');
        return 0;
      }
      if (!Array.isArray(templates)) {
        console.warn('Media cleanup skipped because the saved template list has an unsupported shape.');
        return 0;
      }
      for (const template of templates) {
        if (!template || typeof template !== 'object' ||
            !Array.isArray(template.pre) || !Array.isArray(template.post) ||
            !Array.isArray(template.overlays) || !Array.isArray(template.audio)) {
          console.warn('Media cleanup skipped because a saved template has an unsupported shape.');
          return 0;
        }
        for (const lane of [template.pre, template.post, template.overlays, template.audio]) {
          for (const item of lane) if (item && item.mediaId) referenced.add(item.mediaId);
        }
        if (template.slotClip && template.slotClip.mediaId) referenced.add(template.slotClip.mediaId);
      }
    }
    const mediaIds = await db.media.toCollection().primaryKeys();
    const orphanIds = mediaIds.filter(id => !referenced.has(id));
    if (!orphanIds.length) return 0;
    await db.media.bulkDelete(orphanIds);
    for (const id of orphanIds) {
      if (urls[id]) { URL.revokeObjectURL(urls[id]); delete urls[id]; }
      if (audios[id]) {
        try { audios[id].pause(); audios[id].removeAttribute('src'); audios[id].load(); } catch (_) {}
        delete audios[id];
      }
      delete audioLoads[id];
      delete decoded[id];
      delete thumbs[id];
      delete files[id];
    }
    return orphanIds.length;
  }

  async function deleteSavedProject(id) {
    if (!window.confirm('Delete this saved project? Media used by other projects will be kept; unused imported media may be cleaned up.')) return;
    try {
      await db.projects.delete(id);
      if (activeProjectId === id) {
        activeProjectId = 'current'; activeProjectName = 'Untitled project';
        await saveProject();
      }
      let removedMedia = 0;
      try { removedMedia = await collectUnreferencedMedia(); }
      catch (cleanupError) { console.warn('Unused media cleanup was skipped:', cleanupError); }
      await loadHome();
      say(removedMedia
        ? `Saved project deleted. Cleaned up ${removedMedia} unused media file${removedMedia === 1 ? '' : 's'}.`
        : 'Saved project deleted. Media still used by a project was kept.');
    } catch (err) { say('Could not delete the project: ' + (err.message || err)); }
  }

  async function renderProjectLibrary() {
    const root = $('hmProjectList'), count = $('hmProjectCount');
    root.textContent = '';
    let rows = [];
    try { rows = await db.projects.toArray(); } catch (err) { console.warn('Could not list projects:', err); }
    rows = rows.filter(r => r && r.id !== 'current' && r.state && r.name && r.id !== activeProjectId)
      .sort((a, b) => (b.updated || 0) - (a.updated || 0));
    count.textContent = `${rows.length} saved`;
    if (!rows.length) {
      const p = document.createElement('p'); p.className = 'hm-library-empty';
      p.textContent = 'Named projects you save will appear here. Your current edit is kept above.'; root.appendChild(p); return;
    }
    rows.forEach(row => {
      const item = document.createElement('article'); item.className = 'hm-project-row';
      const info = document.createElement('div'); info.className = 'hm-project-info';
      const name = document.createElement('strong'); name.textContent = row.name || 'Untitled project';
      const meta = document.createElement('small');
      try {
        const st = JSON.parse(row.state);
        meta.textContent = `${(st.main || []).length} video · ${(st.overlays || []).length} overlay · ${new Date(row.updated || Date.now()).toLocaleDateString()}`;
      } catch (_) { meta.textContent = 'Saved on this device'; }
      info.append(name, meta);
      const controls = document.createElement('div'); controls.className = 'hm-project-controls';
      const open = document.createElement('button'); open.className = 'hm-open-project'; open.textContent = 'Open'; open.onclick = () => openSavedProject(row.id);
      const del = document.createElement('button'); del.className = 'hm-delete-project'; del.textContent = 'Delete'; del.onclick = () => deleteSavedProject(row.id);
      controls.append(open, del); item.append(info, controls); root.appendChild(item);
    });
  }

  async function loadHome() {
    if (loadingHome) return;
    loadingHome = true;
    try {
      // Don't race the editor's IndexedDB restore. Still show home even if restore fails.
      if (window.projectMediaReady) await window.projectMediaReady.catch(err => console.warn(err));
      const row = await db.projects.get('current').catch(() => null);
      let st = null;
      if (row && row.state) {
        try { st = JSON.parse(row.state); } catch (_) {}
      }
      const hasRecent = hasContent(st);
      $('hmRecent').classList.toggle('is-empty', !hasRecent);
      $('hmContinue').hidden = !hasRecent;
      $('hmNoProject').hidden = hasRecent;
      $('hmExport').hidden = !hasVideo(st);
      if (hasRecent) {
        const name = row.activeProjectName && row.activeProjectName !== 'Untitled project' ? row.activeProjectName : visibleName(st);
        $('hmProjectTitle').textContent = name;
        const d = new Date(row.updated || Date.now()).toLocaleDateString();
        $('hmProjectMeta').textContent = `${(st.main || []).length} video clip${(st.main || []).length === 1 ? '' : 's'} · ${(st.overlays || []).length} overlay${(st.overlays || []).length === 1 ? '' : 's'} · ${(st.audio || []).length} audio track${(st.audio || []).length === 1 ? '' : 's'} · Saved ${d}`;
        if (SHAPES.some(s => s.v === st.ratio)) pick.r = st.ratio;
      } else {
        $('hmProjectTitle').textContent = 'Your last edit';
        $('hmProjectMeta').textContent = 'Start a project and it will appear here.';
      }
      home.hidden = false; setup.hidden = true; renderSetup(); await renderProjectLibrary();
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Could not load home screen:', err);
      home.hidden = false; setup.hidden = true;
      try { say('Home is open, but the saved-project list could not be loaded.'); } catch (_) {}
    } finally { loadingHome = false; }
  }

  async function openHome() {
    try { if (typeof stop === 'function') stop(); } catch (_) {}
    try { clearTimeout(saveTimer); await saveProject(); } catch (err) { console.warn('Could not save before opening home:', err); }
    try { if (typeof select === 'function') select(null); if (typeof closeSheet === 'function') closeSheet(); } catch (_) {}
    await loadHome();
  }

  async function startNewProject() {
    try {
      await preserveCurrentProject();
      activeProjectId = 'current'; activeProjectName = 'Untitled project';
      const blank = { ratio: pick.r, main: [], audio: [], overlays: [], nextLayer: 1 };
      clearTimeout(saveTimer); t = 0; sel = null;
      await restore(JSON.stringify(blank));
      undoStack = [snap()]; redoStack = []; drawTimeline(); refresh(0); await saveProject();
      showSetup();
    } catch (err) { console.error(err); say('Could not start a new project: ' + (err.message || err)); }
  }

  $('hmContinue').onclick = showEditor;
  $('hmNew').onclick = startNewProject;
  $('hmTemplates').onclick = () => { showEditor(); openSheet('tpl'); };
  $('hmExport').onclick = () => { showEditor(); $('exportBtn').click(); };
  $('homeBtn').onclick = openHome;
  const brand = $('brandHome');
  if (brand) {
    brand.style.cursor = 'pointer';
    brand.onclick = openHome;
    brand.onkeydown = e => { if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') { e.preventDefault(); openHome(); } };
  }
  q('suStart').addEventListener('click', () => {}); // keep the primary action explicitly keyboard-focusable

  // First load always opens the dashboard, not just on the very first installation.
  loadHome();
})();

// ProjectMedia Y offline shell. Only cache app files that are part of this repository.
const CACHE = 'pmy-v26';
const SHELL = ['./', './index.html', './style.css', './setup.css', './mediay-icons.js', './mediay-core.js', './mediay-fast.js', './app.js', './setup.js', './vendor/ffmpeg/ffmpeg.js', './vendor/ffmpeg/814.ffmpeg.js', './vendor/media-libraries/fabric.min.js', './vendor/media-libraries/anime.min.js', './vendor/media-libraries/dexie.min.js'];
self.addEventListener('install', event => {
  event.waitUntil((async () => {
    const cache = await caches.open(CACHE);
    await Promise.all(SHELL.map(async path => {
      try {
        const response = await fetch(path, { cache: 'reload' });
        if (response && response.ok) await cache.put(path, response);
      } catch (_) { /* keep install alive if the host omits an optional route */ }
    }));
    await self.skipWaiting();
  })());
});
self.addEventListener('activate', event => {
  event.waitUntil((async () => {
    const keys = await caches.keys();
    await Promise.all(keys.filter(key => key.startsWith('pmy-') && key !== CACHE).map(key => caches.delete(key)));
    await self.clients.claim();
  })());
});
self.addEventListener('fetch', event => {
  if (event.request.method !== 'GET') return;
  event.respondWith((async () => {
    try {
      const response = await fetch(event.request);
      if (response && response.ok) {
        const copy = response.clone();
        caches.open(CACHE).then(cache => cache.put(event.request, copy)).catch(() => {});
      }
      return response;
    } catch (_) {
      return (await caches.match(event.request)) || Response.error();
    }
  })());
});

from http.server import SimpleHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer
from functools import partial
from pathlib import Path
from threading import Thread
from playwright.sync_api import sync_playwright
import json

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
handler = partial(SimpleHTTPRequestHandler, directory=str(ROOT))
server = ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", 0), handler)
Thread(target=server.serve_forever, daemon=True).start()
base_url = f"http://127.0.0.1:{server.server_port}"

try:
    with sync_playwright() as p:
        browser = p.chromium.launch(headless=True, args=["--no-sandbox", "--disable-dev-shm-usage"])
        page = browser.new_page()
        page.set_default_timeout(240_000)
        page.on("pageerror", lambda error: print("PAGE ERROR:", error))
        page.goto(base_url + "/ffmpeg-worker-smoke.html", wait_until="domcontentloaded")
        page.wait_for_function("window.testResult !== undefined", timeout=240_000)
        result = page.evaluate("window.testResult")
        print(json.dumps(result))
        browser.close()
        if not result.get("ok"):
            raise SystemExit("FFmpeg worker integration test failed: " + str(result.get("error")))
finally:
    server.shutdown()
    server.server_close()

from pathlib import Path
from playwright.sync_api import sync_playwright

def assert_computed_contrast(page, selectors, label):
    report = page.evaluate("""selectors => {
      const parse = value => {
        const m = String(value).match(/rgba?\\(\\s*([\\d.]+)[,\\s]+([\\d.]+)[,\\s]+([\\d.]+)(?:\\s*[,/]\\s*([\\d.]+))?\\s*\\)/i);
        return m ? [Number(m[1]), Number(m[2]), Number(m[3]), m[4] === undefined ? 1 : Number(m[4])] : null;
      };
      const luminance = rgb => {
        const c = rgb.map(v => { v /= 255; return v <= .04045 ? v / 12.92 : ((v + .055) / 1.055) ** 2.4; });
        return .2126 * c[0] + .7152 * c[1] + .0722 * c[2];
      };
      const results = [];
      for (const selector of selectors) for (const el of document.querySelectorAll(selector)) {
        const cs = getComputedStyle(el);
        if (!el.textContent.trim() || el.matches(':disabled') || cs.display === 'none' || cs.visibility === 'hidden' || !el.getClientRects().length) continue;
        let chain = [], node = el, gradient = false, bg = [8, 13, 24], opacity = 1;
        while (node) { chain.unshift(node); node = node.parentElement; }
        for (const item of chain) {
          const st = getComputedStyle(item);
          if (item === el && st.backgroundImage !== 'none') { gradient = true; break; }
          const color = parse(st.backgroundColor);
          if (color && color[3] > 0) bg = bg.map((v, i) => color[i] * color[3] + v * (1 - color[3]));
          const nodeOpacity = parseFloat(st.opacity); if (Number.isFinite(nodeOpacity)) opacity *= nodeOpacity;
        }
        if (gradient) continue;
        const fg = parse(cs.color);
        if (!fg) continue;
        const alpha = fg[3] * opacity;
        const visibleFg = fg.slice(0, 3).map((v, i) => v * alpha + bg[i] * (1 - alpha));
        const ratio = (Math.max(luminance(visibleFg), luminance(bg)) + .05) / (Math.min(luminance(visibleFg), luminance(bg)) + .05);
        const size = parseFloat(cs.fontSize) || 16, weight = parseInt(cs.fontWeight, 10) || 400;
        results.push({selector, text:el.textContent.trim().slice(0,32), ratio:Number(ratio.toFixed(2)), threshold:(size >= 24 || (size >= 18.66 && weight >= 700)) ? 3 : 4.5, foreground:cs.color, background:bg.map(v=>Math.round(v))});
      }
      return results;
    }""", selectors)
    assert report, f"No visible text controls found for {label}"
    failures = [item for item in report if item["ratio"] + 0.01 < item["threshold"]]
    assert not failures, f"Computed WCAG contrast failed for {label}: {failures}"
    print(f"PASS: computed WCAG contrast for {label} ({len(report)} rendered text controls)")

html=html.replace('<script src="./app.js"></script>', '<script>'+app+'</script>')
html=html.replace('<script src="./setup.js"></script>', '<script>'+setup+'</script>')
html=html.replace('<script>'+app+'</script>', '<script>try{localStorage.setItem("pmy-consent-v1","yes")}catch(e){}</script><script>'+app+'</script>')

with sync_playwright() as p:
    browser=p.chromium.launch(headless=True,args=['--no-sandbox','--disable-dev-shm-usage'])
    context=browser.new_context(viewport={'width':390,'height':844}, is_mobile=True, has_touch=True)
    page=context.new_page(); errors=[]; page.on('pageerror', lambda e: errors.append(str(e)))
    page.set_content(html, wait_until='domcontentloaded')
    page.wait_for_selector('#homeScreen:not([hidden])',timeout=10000)
    if page.locator('#consentDialog[open]').count(): page.locator('#consentBtn').click()
    assert "async function beginNativeExportService()" in app, 'native export-service start hook is missing'
    assert "async function endNativeExportService()" in app, 'native export-service cleanup hook is missing'
    assert "await endNativeExportService()" in app, 'native export service is not stopped during export cleanup'
    assert "foreground-service notification" in html, 'background export guidance is missing'
    print('PASS: Android export service hooks and foreground-export guidance are present')
    assert page.locator('.dock svg[data-mediay-svg]').count() >= 10, 'custom MediaY toolbar SVG icons did not render'
    assert page.locator('#homeScreen svg[data-mediay-svg]').count() >= 4, 'custom MediaY home SVG icons did not render'
    assert 'lucide.min.js' not in html and 'fonts.googleapis.com' not in html, 'remote icon/font dependencies remain'
    print('PASS: home screen appears on startup')
    print('PASS: original local SVG icons render in home and editor toolbar')
    page.locator('#hmNew').click(); page.wait_for_selector('#setup:not([hidden])')
    print('PASS: New project opens setup screen')
    page.locator('#suStart').click(); page.wait_for_function("document.querySelector('#homeScreen').hidden === true")
    print('PASS: Start editing closes home/setup and opens editor')
    page.evaluate("""() => {
      S.main.push({id:'contrast-clip', mediaId:'missing-contrast', name:'Contrast clip.mp4', in:0, out:5, src:5});
      select('contrast-clip','main'); t=1; drawTimeline(); refresh(t);
    }""")
    assert_computed_contrast(page, ['#status', '#timeDisplay', '.timeline-tools-title', '#tlZoomLabel', '#copyTillBtn', '.dock button', '.track-name', '.timeline-ruler'], 'editor and timeline controls')
    page.evaluate("S.main=[]; select(null); drawTimeline(); refresh(0);")
    assert page.locator('#tlZoom').count() == 1, 'timeline zoom slider is missing'
    assert page.locator('#copyTillBtn').count() == 1, 'Copy till here control is missing'
    assert page.locator('#adjustBtn').count() == 1, 'Adjust control is missing'
    assert page.locator('#blendBtn').count() == 1, 'Blending control is missing'
    transition_count = page.locator('#trList button').count()
    assert transition_count == 59, f'expected None plus 58 FFmpeg transitions, found {transition_count}'
    assert page.locator('#trPreviewPlay').count() == 1, 'transition preview control is missing'
    assert page.locator('#trPreviewStage').count() == 1, 'transition preview stage is missing'
    assert page.evaluate("typeof previewSelectedTransition === 'function'"), 'transition preview handler is missing'
    assert "transitionPreviewAbortController" in app, 'transition preview cancellation controller is missing'
    assert "Timed out loading a clip for the transition preview" in app, 'media metadata load has no bounded timeout'
    assert "Timed out seeking media for the transition preview" in app, 'media seek has no bounded timeout'
    assert "setTransitionPreviewVideo(before, previous, Math.max(previous.in, previous.out - seconds * (previous.speed || 1)), token, previewSignal)" in app, 'outgoing preview does not receive cancellation state'
    assert "setTransitionPreviewVideo(after, c, c.in, token, previewSignal)" in app, 'incoming preview does not receive cancellation state'
    transition_failure = page.evaluate("""async () => {
      resetTransitionPreview();
      const token = transitionPreviewToken;
      const controller = new AbortController();
      try {
        await setTransitionPreviewVideo(document.createElement('video'),
          {mediaId:'missing-transition-preview-media', in:0, out:1}, 0, token, controller.signal);
        return '';
      } catch (error) { return error.message; }
    }""")
    assert 'missing' in transition_failure.lower(), transition_failure
    print('PASS: transition preview has cancellation, bounded media waits, and missing-media failure handling')
    old_zoom = page.evaluate('PX')
    page.locator('#tlZoomIn').click()
    assert page.evaluate('PX') == min(90, old_zoom + 6), 'timeline zoom-in control did not change scale'
    page.locator('#tlZoomFit').click()
    print('PASS: timeline zoom controls work and all 58 built-in transitions are offered')
    page.evaluate("""() => {
      S.main.push({id:'adjust-test-a', mediaId:'missing-adjust-a', name:'adjust-a.mp4', in:0, out:5, src:5});
      S.main.push({id:'adjust-test-b', mediaId:'missing-adjust-b', name:'adjust-b.mp4', in:0, out:5, src:5});
      select('adjust-test-a','main');
    }""")
    page.locator('#adjustBtn').click()

    page.locator('#adjustApplyAll').click()

    page.evaluate("S.overlays.push({id:'test-overlay', type:'text', text:'TEST', style:0, name:'TEST', layer:1, start:0, dur:3, left:10, top:10, scaleX:1, scaleY:1}); drawTimeline();")

    assert hidden, 'empty-state overlay is still visible when an overlay exists'

    page.evaluate("""() => {
      S.overlays.push({id:'blend-test-a', type:'image', mediaId:'missing-blend-a', name:'blend-a.png', start:0, dur:3, layer:2, left:10, top:10, scaleX:1, scaleY:1});
      S.overlays.push({id:'blend-test-b', type:'image', mediaId:'missing-blend-b', name:'blend-b.png', start:0, dur:3, layer:3, left:20, top:20, scaleX:1, scaleY:1});
      select('blend-test-a','overlay');
    }""")
    page.locator('#blendBtn').click()

    page.locator('#blendApplyAll').click()
    blend_result = page.evaluate("({a:S.overlays.find(o=>o.id==='blend-test-a'),b:S.overlays.find(o=>o.id==='blend-test-b')})")
    assert blend_result['a']['blend'] == 'multiply' and blend_result['b']['blend'] == 'multiply', blend_result
    assert abs(blend_result['a']['opacity'] - .55) < .001 and abs(blend_result['b']['opacity'] - .55) < .001, blend_result
    page.locator('#sheetClose').click()
    page.evaluate("select(null); drawTimeline();")
    print('PASS: blending mode and opacity apply to all overlay layers')
    image_result = page.evaluate("""async () => {
      const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
      canvas.width = 2; canvas.height = 2;
      const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
      ctx.fillStyle = '#f01925'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 2, 2);
      const png = await new Promise(resolve => canvas.toBlob(resolve, 'image/png'));
      const id = 'overlay-image-recovery-test';
      const file = new File([png], 'overlay-test.png', {type:'image/png'});
      await db.media.put({id, name:file.name, type:file.type, blob:file});
      files[id] = file;
      // Simulate a stale/broken object URL while the original bytes remain in IndexedDB.
      const recovered = await loadOverlayImage('data:image/png;base64,not-a-valid-image', {id:'overlay', mediaId:id, name:file.name});
      const stored = await db.media.get(id);
      const ok = recovered.width === 2 && recovered.height === 2 && stored.type === 'image/png';
      if (urls[id] && urls[id].startsWith('blob:')) URL.revokeObjectURL(urls[id]);
      return {ok, width:recovered.width, height:recovered.height, storedType:stored.type};
    }""")
    assert image_result['ok'], image_result
    print('PASS: template overlay image can recover from a broken preview URL')
    page.evaluate("S.main.push({id:'test-video', mediaId:'missing-media', name:'Smoke test video.mp4', in:0, out:5, src:5}); t=7; select('test-video','main');")
    copy_result = page.evaluate("""async () => {
      await copyTillPlayhead();
      return {count:S.main.length, duration:total(), selected:sel && sel.id, enabled:!$('copyTillBtn').disabled};
    }""")
    assert copy_result['count'] == 2, copy_result
    assert abs(copy_result['duration'] - 7) < 0.01, copy_result
    assert copy_result['selected'] != 'test-video' and not copy_result['enabled'], copy_result
    print('PASS: Copy till here duplicates a clip up to the playhead')
    # Regression coverage for the playhead trim actions across the main, audio, and overlay lanes.
    page.evaluate("""() => {
      S.main = [
        {id:'trim-main-start', mediaId:'missing-trim-a', name:'trim-a.mp4', in:0.5, out:8, src:8, speed:2},
        {id:'trim-main-next', mediaId:'missing-trim-b', name:'trim-b.mp4', in:0, out:5, src:5, speed:1}
      ];
      S.audio = [{id:'trim-audio-start', mediaId:'missing-audio', name:'trim-audio.mp3', in:0, out:8, src:8, start:4}];
      S.overlays = [{id:'trim-overlay', type:'text', text:'T', name:'trim-overlay', start:2, dur:6, layer:1}];
      sel = {id:'trim-main-start', lane:'main'}; t = 1.25; undoStack = [snap()]; redoStack = []; drawTimeline(); refresh(t); updateChrome();
    }""")
    # Let queued browser scroll events run; refresh-driven scrolling must never move the playhead.
    page.wait_for_timeout(75)
    playhead_after_refresh = page.evaluate("t")
    assert abs(playhead_after_refresh - 1.25) < 0.001, playhead_after_refresh
    assert not page.locator('#trimStartBtn').is_disabled(), 'From here should enable when playhead is inside a selected clip'
    page.locator('#trimStartBtn').click()
    main_start = page.evaluate("({in:S.main[0].in, out:S.main[0].out, speed:S.main[0].speed, t, duration:dur(S.main[0])})")
    assert abs(main_start['in'] - 3.0) < 0.001, main_start
    assert abs(main_start['duration'] - 2.5) < 0.001, main_start
    assert abs(main_start['t']) < 0.001, main_start
    print('PASS: From here trims source time correctly for a speed-adjusted main clip')
    page.locator('#undoBtn').click()
    page.wait_for_function("S.main[0].in === 0.5")
    assert abs(page.evaluate("S.main[0].out") - 8) < 0.001, 'undo should restore the complete original trim range'
    page.locator('#redoBtn').click()
    page.wait_for_function("S.main[0].in === 3")
    assert abs(page.evaluate("dur(S.main[0])") - 2.5) < 0.001, 'redo should restore the trimmed source range'
    print('PASS: From here trim participates correctly in Undo and Redo')

    page.evaluate("""() => {
      S.main = [{id:'trim-main-end', mediaId:'missing-trim-c', name:'trim-c.mp4', in:0, out:8, src:8, speed:1}];

    assert abs(main_end['out'] - 3) < 0.001 and abs(main_end['duration'] - 3) < 0.001, main_end
    print('PASS: To here trims the main clip ending at the playhead')

    page.evaluate("""() => {
      S.main = [{id:'timeline-support-audio', mediaId:'missing-support-audio', name:'support.mp4', in:0, out:20, src:20, speed:1}]; sel = {id:'trim-audio-start', lane:'audio'}; t = 6; drawTimeline(); refresh(t); updateChrome();

    print('PASS: From here trims audio source and moves its timeline start together')

    page.evaluate("""() => {
      S.audio = [{id:'trim-audio-end', mediaId:'missing-audio-end', name:'end.mp3', in:0, out:8, src:8, start:1}];
      S.main = [{id:'timeline-support-audio-end', mediaId:'missing-support-audio-end', name:'support-end.mp4', in:0, out:20, src:20, speed:1}]; sel = {id:'trim-audio-end', lane:'audio'}; t = 4; drawTimeline(); refresh(t); updateChrome();

    page.locator('#trimEndBtn').click()
    audio_end = page.evaluate("({out:S.audio[0].out, start:S.audio[0].start})")
    assert abs(audio_end['out'] - 3) < 0.001 and abs(audio_end['start'] - 1) < 0.001, audio_end
    print('PASS: To here trims an audio clip without shifting its timeline start')

    page.evaluate("""() => {
      S.main = [{id:'timeline-support-overlay', mediaId:'missing-support-overlay', name:'support-overlay.mp4', in:0, out:20, src:20, speed:1}]; sel = {id:'trim-overlay', lane:'overlay'}; t = 4; drawTimeline(); refresh(t); updateChrome();
