    print('PASS: From here and To here trim overlay timing and duration correctly')

    page.evaluate("""() => {
      S.main = [{id:'timeline-support-video-overlay', mediaId:'missing-support-video-overlay', name:'support-video-overlay.mp4', in:0, out:20, src:20, speed:1}];
      S.overlays = [{
        id:'trim-overlay-video', type:'video', mediaId:'missing-video-overlay',
        name:'picture-in-picture.mp4', start:2, dur:6, sourceIn:1, srcDur:10, layer:1,
        el:{currentTime:0, paused:true, play:()=>Promise.resolve(), pause:()=>{}}
      }];
      canvas.clear(); canvas.add({pmId:'trim-overlay-video', layer:1, visible:true});
      sel = {id:'trim-overlay-video', lane:'overlay'}; t = 4; drawTimeline(); refresh(t); updateChrome();
    }""")
    page.wait_for_timeout(40)
    assert not page.locator('#trimStartBtn').is_disabled(), 'video overlay From here should enable inside its source range'
    page.locator('#trimStartBtn').click()
    video_overlay_start = page.evaluate("({start:S.overlays[0].start,dur:S.overlays[0].dur,sourceIn:S.overlays[0].sourceIn,previewTime:S.overlays[0].el.currentTime})")
    assert abs(video_overlay_start['start'] - 4) < 0.001 and abs(video_overlay_start['dur'] - 4) < 0.001, video_overlay_start
    assert abs(video_overlay_start['sourceIn'] - 3) < 0.001 and abs(video_overlay_start['previewTime'] - 3) < 0.001, video_overlay_start
    assert abs(page.evaluate("overlaySourceAt({sourceIn:3,start:4},6)") - 5) < 0.001, 'export video source mapping should account for prior source trim'
    assert abs(page.evaluate("overlaySourceAt({sourceIn:3,start:8},6)") - 3) < 0.001, 'export seek before overlay start should preserve its trimmed source-in'
    page.evaluate("t = 5; refresh(t); updateChrome();")
    page.wait_for_timeout(40)
    page.locator('#trimEndBtn').click()
    video_overlay_end = page.evaluate("({start:S.overlays[0].start,dur:S.overlays[0].dur,sourceIn:S.overlays[0].sourceIn})")
    assert abs(video_overlay_end['start'] - 4) < 0.001 and abs(video_overlay_end['dur'] - 1) < 0.001, video_overlay_end
    assert abs(video_overlay_end['sourceIn'] - 3) < 0.001, video_overlay_end
    print('PASS: video overlay trimming preserves source-in across preview, export seek, and audio mixing')

    page.evaluate("""() => {
      S.main = [{id:'trim-one-frame', mediaId:'missing-one-frame', name:'one-frame.mp4', in:0, out:1, src:1, speed:1}];
      sel = {id:'trim-one-frame', lane:'main'}; t = 1/30; drawTimeline(); refresh(t); updateChrome();
    }""")
    page.wait_for_timeout(75)
    assert not page.locator('#trimStartBtn').is_disabled(), 'trim should allow frame-accurate edits, not require a 0.2 second margin'
    page.locator('#trimStartBtn').click()
    one_frame = page.evaluate("({in:S.main[0].in,duration:dur(S.main[0])})")
    assert abs(one_frame['in'] - (1/30)) < 0.0001, one_frame
    assert abs(one_frame['duration'] - (29/30)) < 0.0001, one_frame
    print('PASS: trim precision supports a one-frame edit at 30 fps')

    page.evaluate("""() => {
      S.main = [{id:'trim-boundary', mediaId:'missing-boundary', name:'boundary.mp4', in:0, out:5, src:5}];
      sel = {id:'trim-boundary', lane:'main'}; t = 0; drawTimeline(); refresh(t); updateChrome();
    }""")
    assert page.locator('#trimStartBtn').is_disabled(), 'From here should disable at the clip boundary'
    assert page.locator('#trimEndBtn').is_disabled(), 'To here should disable at the clip boundary'
    page.evaluate("trimSelectionAtPlayhead('start')")
    boundary = page.evaluate("({in:S.main[0].in,out:S.main[0].out})")
    assert boundary == {'in':0,'out':5}, boundary
    print('PASS: trim buttons reject boundary positions that would create zero-length clips')

    # Fast draft is an explicit quality/speed trade-off and must update both export controls.
    draft_settings = page.evaluate("""() => {
      const old = {res:EXP.res, fps:EXP.fps};
      applyExportPreset('draft');
      const chosen = {
        res:EXP.res, fps:EXP.fps,
        resSelected:[...$('resList').children].some(b => +b.dataset.v === 480 && b.classList.contains('on')),
        fpsSelected:[...$('fpsList').children].some(b => +b.dataset.v === 24 && b.classList.contains('on'))
      };
      EXP.res = old.res; EXP.fps = old.fps;
      for (const b of $('resList').children) b.classList.toggle('on', +b.dataset.v === EXP.res);
      for (const b of $('fpsList').children) b.classList.toggle('on', +b.dataset.v === EXP.fps);
      return chosen;
    }""")
    assert draft_settings == {'res':480,'fps':24,'resSelected':True,'fpsSelected':True}, draft_settings
    print('PASS: Fast draft preset switches both resolution and frame rate together')

    caption_chunks = page.evaluate("""async () => {
      const oldAsr = asr, oldCancel = captionCancelRequested;
      const oldLang = $('capLang').value, oldTranslate = $('capTr').checked;
      const sizes = [], progress = [], timestampModes = [];
      asr = async (pcm, options) => {
        sizes.push(pcm.length);
        timestampModes.push(options.return_timestamps);
        return {chunks:[{text:'alpha beta gamma delta', timestamp:[0,1]}]};
      };
      captionCancelRequested = false;
      try {
        const drafts = await captionFrom(
          new Float32Array(CAPTION_SAMPLE_RATE * 61),
          {start:5, in:0, out:61, sp:2, label:'test media'},
          p => progress.push(p.percent)
        );
        return {
          sizes, count:drafts.length,
          starts:drafts.map(x => x.start),
          durations:drafts.map(x => x.dur),
          progress, timestampModes
        };
      } finally {
        asr = oldAsr; captionCancelRequested = oldCancel;
        $('capLang').value = oldLang; $('capTr').checked = oldTranslate;
      }
    }""")
    assert caption_chunks['sizes'] == [30*16000,30*16000,16000], caption_chunks
    assert caption_chunks['count'] == 3, caption_chunks
    # Captions lead their speech by 0.04 s and are held 0.14 s after it (mediay-core.js CAPTION), at 2x speed.
    assert all(abs(a-b) < 0.03 for a,b in zip(caption_chunks['starts'],[5,20,35])), caption_chunks
    assert all(0.45 < d < 0.65 for d in caption_chunks['durations']), caption_chunks
    assert caption_chunks['progress'] == [1/3,2/3,1], caption_chunks
    assert caption_chunks['timestampModes'] == [True,True,True], caption_chunks
    print('PASS: auto captions stream 30-second inference chunks, use native segment timestamps, and retain source-time offsets')

    word_aligned = page.evaluate("""async () => {
      const oldAsr = asr, oldCancel = captionCancelRequested;
      asr = async (_pcm, options) => {
        if (options.return_timestamps !== true) throw new Error('native segment timestamps were not requested');
        return {chunks:[
          {text:'Hello there, this works.', timestamp:[1.0,2.5]},
          {text:'Again', timestamp:[4.0,4.3]}
        ]};
      };
      captionCancelRequested = false;
      try {
        const drafts = await captionFrom(new Float32Array(CAPTION_SAMPLE_RATE*5),
          {start:10,in:3,out:8,sp:2,label:'word timing test'}, () => {});
        return drafts.map(x => ({text:x.text,start:x.start,dur:x.dur}));
      } finally { asr=oldAsr; captionCancelRequested=oldCancel; }
    }""")
    assert len(word_aligned) == 2, word_aligned
    assert word_aligned[0]['text'] == 'Hello there, this works.', word_aligned
    assert abs(word_aligned[0]['start'] - 10.5) < 0.03, word_aligned
    assert 0.7 < word_aligned[0]['dur'] < 0.95, word_aligned
    assert word_aligned[1]['text'] == 'Again', word_aligned
    assert abs(word_aligned[1]['start'] - 12) < 0.03 and 0.15 < word_aligned[1]['dur'] < 0.3, word_aligned
    print('PASS: segment-level subtitle timing preserves speech boundaries and clip-speed mapping')

    caption_persistence = page.evaluate("""async () => {
      const draft = {id:'persist-caption-check',layer:S.nextLayer,type:'text',text:'Saved caption',style:0,start:1.25,dur:.75,top:250};
      const count = await commitCaptionDrafts([draft]);
      const checkpoint = await db.projects.get('current');
      const persisted = checkpoint && normalizeState(checkpoint.state);
      const stored = persisted && persisted.overlays.find(x => x.id === draft.id);
      await restore(checkpoint.state);
      const restored = S.overlays.find(x => x.id === draft.id);
      return {count, stored:stored && {start:stored.start,dur:stored.dur}, restored:restored && {text:restored.text,start:restored.start,dur:restored.dur}};
    }""")
    assert caption_persistence['count'] == 1, caption_persistence
    assert caption_persistence['stored'] == {'start':1.25,'dur':.75}, caption_persistence
    assert caption_persistence['restored'] == {'text':'Saved caption','start':1.25,'dur':.75}, caption_persistence
    print('PASS: generated captions are saved, verified, and survive project-state restoration')

    caption_cancel = page.evaluate("""async () => {
      const oldAsr = asr, oldCancel = captionCancelRequested;
      const before = {overlays:S.overlays.length, nextLayer:S.nextLayer};
      asr = async () => {
        captionCancelRequested = true;
        return {chunks:[{text:'should never be committed',timestamp:[0,1]}]};
      };
      captionCancelRequested = false;
      let name = '';
      try {
        await captionFrom(new Float32Array(CAPTION_SAMPLE_RATE*30), {start:0,in:0,out:30,sp:1,label:'test'}, () => {});
      } catch (error) { name = error.name; }
      finally { asr = oldAsr; captionCancelRequested = oldCancel; }
      return {name, before, after:{overlays:S.overlays.length,nextLayer:S.nextLayer}};
    }""")
    assert caption_cancel['name'] == 'AbortError', caption_cancel
    assert caption_cancel['before'] == caption_cancel['after'], caption_cancel
    print('PASS: cancelling caption inference discards uncommitted caption drafts')

    signal_checks = page.evaluate("""() => {
      const silent = new Float32Array(16000 * 2);
      const speechLike = new Float32Array(16000);
      for (let i=0;i<speechLike.length;i++) speechLike[i]=0.08*Math.sin(i*0.1);
      return {silent:hasAudibleSignal(silent), speech:hasAudibleSignal(speechLike)};
    }""")
    assert signal_checks == {'silent':False,'speech':True}, signal_checks
    print('PASS: silent tracks are skipped before running speech inference')

    perf_contract = page.evaluate("""() => {
      const metric = createMediaYMetric('Test report', {resolution:480,fps:24});
      metric.stage('Audio decoding');
      metric.command('seg0.mp4',12,true);
      metric.stage('Inference');
      metric.command('seg1.mp4',8,false);
      const report = metric.finish('test');
      recentMediaYMetrics.export = report;

        outcome:report.outcome,

      };
    }""")
    assert perf_contract['kind'] == 'Test report' and perf_contract['outcome'] == 'test', perf_contract
    assert perf_contract['total'] >= 0, perf_contract
    assert 'Audio decoding' in perf_contract['stages'] and 'Inference' in perf_contract['stages'], perf_contract
    assert perf_contract['clipCommands']['count'] == 2 and perf_contract['clipCommands']['failures'] == 1, perf_contract
    assert 'Test report report' in perf_contract['visible'] and 'clip encoding' in perf_contract['visible'], perf_contract
    print('PASS: performance report aggregates stage duration and FFmpeg command timings')

    cancel_button_result = page.evaluate("""() => {
      captionRunning = true;
      captionCancelRequested = false;
      $('busyCancel').hidden = false;
      updateBusy('Transcribing speech…','test cancellation button',20,'Captions');
      return {visible:!$('busyCancel').hidden,disabled:$('busyCancel').disabled};
    }""")
    assert cancel_button_result == {'visible':True,'disabled':False}, cancel_button_result
    page.locator('#busyCancel').click()
    cancel_click_result = page.evaluate("""() => ({
      requested:captionCancelRequested,

    assert cancel_click_result['requested'] and cancel_click_result['disabled'] and 'Cancellation requested' in cancel_click_result['label'], cancel_click_result
    page.evaluate("""() => {
      captionRunning = false; captionCancelRequested = false;
      $('busyCancel').hidden = true; $('busyOverlay').hidden = true;
      document.body.removeAttribute('aria-busy');
    }""")
    print('PASS: caption cancellation remains clickable above the blocking progress overlay')
    # ---- background service bookkeeping: one start, one stop, never leaked ----
    service_calls = page.evaluate("""async () => {
      const calls = [];
      const old = window.Capacitor;
      window.Capacitor = { isNativePlatform: () => true, Plugins: { MediaYExportService: {
        start: async () => { calls.push('start'); }, stop: async () => { calls.push('stop'); } } } };
      try {
        const both = await Promise.all([Background.begin(), Background.begin()]);   // export + captions overlap
        await Background.end(); const afterFirstEnd = calls.slice();
        await Background.end(); const afterSecondEnd = calls.slice();
        await Background.end();                                                      // extra end must be harmless
        window.Capacitor.Plugins.MediaYExportService.start = async () => { throw new Error('denied'); };
        const failed = await Background.begin(); await Background.end();
        return { both, afterFirstEnd, afterSecondEnd, final: calls, failed, active: Background.active };
      } finally { window.Capacitor = old; }
    }""")
    assert service_calls['both'] == [True, True], service_calls
    assert service_calls['afterFirstEnd'] == ['start'], service_calls          # still held by the second job
    assert service_calls['afterSecondEnd'] == ['start', 'stop'], service_calls
    assert service_calls['final'] == ['start', 'stop'], service_calls          # unbalanced end() never stops anything twice
    assert service_calls['failed'] is False and service_calls['active'] is False, service_calls
    print('PASS: native background service is started once, stopped once, and survives overlapping jobs and start failures')

    # ---- in-place project refresh (replaces "close and reopen the app and the project") ----
    refresh_result = page.evaluate("""async () => {
      const cap = {id:'refresh-check',layer:S.nextLayer,type:'text',caption:true,text:'Refresh me',style:0,start:0.5,dur:1,top:250};
      await commitCaptionDrafts([cap]);

      const objects = canvas.getObjects().filter(o => o.text === 'Refresh me').length;

               ffReleased: !ff, overlays: S.overlays.filter(o => o.id === 'refresh-check').length };
    }""")
    assert refresh_result['ok'] and refresh_result['same'] and refresh_result['objects'] == 1, refresh_result
    assert refresh_result['asrReleased'] and refresh_result['disposed'] == 1 and refresh_result['ffReleased'], refresh_result
    assert refresh_result['overlays'] == 1, refresh_result

    guards = page.evaluate("""async () => {
      const out = {};
      captionRunning = true; exportInProgress = 0;

      out.untouched = S.overlays.length === before;

    }""")
    assert guards['exportBlockedDuringCaptions'] and guards['captionsBlockedDuringExport'] and guards['untouched'], guards
    print('PASS: exports and caption generation never run at the same time')

    # ---- speech engine loading: retry, corrupt-cache recovery, honest offline errors, cancellation ----
    speech = page.evaluate("""async () => {
      speechRetryDelayMs = 0;
      const out = {}; const events = [];
      const realUpdate = updateBusy;
      const seed = pipelineImpl => speechModules.set('cpu', { env: { backends: { onnx: { wasm: {} } } }, pipeline: pipelineImpl });
      // (a) two network failures, then success
      let calls = 0; seed(async () => { calls++; if (calls < 3) throw new Error('Failed to fetch'); return { fake: 'model' }; });
      captionCancelRequested = false;
      const model = await createSpeechPipeline(false);
      out.retry = { calls, ok: model.fake === 'model', backend: asrBackend };
      // (b) a damaged cached file is deleted once, then the download is retried
      const deleted = []; const realCaches = window.caches;
      Object.defineProperty(window, 'caches', { configurable: true, value: { delete: async name => { deleted.push(name); return true; } } });
      let corruptCalls = 0; seed(async () => { corruptCalls++; if (corruptCalls === 1) throw new Error('protobuf parsing failed'); return { fake: 'model2' }; });
      const model2 = await createSpeechPipeline(false);
      out.corrupt = { calls: corruptCalls, deleted, ok: model2.fake === 'model2' };
      Object.defineProperty(window, 'caches', { configurable: true, value: realCaches });
      // (c) never reachable: the message must say offline + how big + that it works offline afterwards
      const onlineDesc = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(Navigator.prototype, 'onLine');
      Object.defineProperty(navigator, 'onLine', { configurable: true, get: () => false });
      seed(async () => { throw new Error('Failed to fetch'); });
      try { await createSpeechPipeline(false); out.offline = 'no error'; } catch (e) { out.offline = e.message; }
      delete navigator.onLine;
      // (d) cancellation is honoured before any download is attempted
      let cancelCalls = 0; seed(async () => { cancelCalls++; return {}; });
      captionCancelRequested = true;
      try { await createSpeechPipeline(false); out.cancel = 'no error'; } catch (e) { out.cancel = e.name; }
      out.cancelCalls = cancelCalls; captionCancelRequested = false;
      // (e) progress across several files is one overall percentage that never goes backwards
      const percents = [];
      updateBusy = (title, detail, pct) => { if (typeof pct === 'number') percents.push(pct); };
      seed(async (task, id, options) => {
        const cb = options.progress_callback;
        cb({ status: 'progress', file: 'a.onnx', loaded: 10, total: 100 }); cb({ status: 'progress', file: 'b.onnx', loaded: 0, total: 100 });
        cb({ status: 'progress', file: 'a.onnx', loaded: 100, total: 100 }); cb({ status: 'progress', file: 'b.onnx', loaded: 100, total: 100 });
        return {};
      });
      await createSpeechPipeline(false);
      updateBusy = realUpdate;
      out.percents = percents.filter(x => x !== 0);
      speechModules.clear();
      hideBusy();
      return out;
    }""")
    assert speech['retry'] == {'calls': 3, 'ok': True, 'backend': 'wasm'}, speech
    assert speech['corrupt']['calls'] == 2 and speech['corrupt']['deleted'] == ['transformers-cache'] and speech['corrupt']['ok'], speech
    assert 'offline' in speech['offline'] and '40 MB' in speech['offline'], speech
    assert speech['cancel'] == 'AbortError' and speech['cancelCalls'] == 0, speech
    assert speech['percents'] == sorted(speech['percents']) and speech['percents'][-1] == 100, speech
    print('PASS: speech engine loading retries, repairs a damaged cache, reports offline honestly, honours cancel and reports one overall percentage')

    def handle_dialog(d): d.accept('Smoke test project')
    page.on('dialog',handle_dialog)
    page.locator('#saveBtn').click(); page.wait_for_timeout(100)
    page.locator('#homeBtn').click(); page.wait_for_selector('#homeScreen:not([hidden])'); page.wait_for_timeout(100)
    saved = page.locator('#hmProjectTitle').inner_text()
    assert 'Smoke test project' in saved, saved
    print('PASS: Save creates a named project visible as the recent project')
    page.locator('#hmNew').click(); page.wait_for_selector('#setup:not([hidden])')
    page.locator('#suStart').click(); page.locator('#homeBtn').click(); page.wait_for_selector('#homeScreen:not([hidden])')
    library = page.locator('#hmProjectList').inner_text()
    assert 'Smoke test project' in library, library
    print('PASS: Starting a new project preserves the previously saved project')
    print('Page errors:', errors)
    assert not errors, errors
    browser.close()

'use strict';
const test = require('node:test');
const assert = require('node:assert/strict');
const fs = require('node:fs');
const path = require('node:path');

const app = fs.readFileSync(path.join(__dirname, '..', 'app.js'), 'utf8');

test('large caption sources use bounded 10-minute file-backed windows', () => {
  assert.match(app, /CAPTION_STREAM_WINDOW_SECONDS = 10 \* 60/);
  assert.match(app, /CAPTION_STREAM_MAX_SECONDS = 4 \* 60 \* 60/);
  assert.match(app, /shouldStreamCaptionSource\(sourceFile, source\)/);
  assert.match(app, /sourceDuration = Math\.max\(0, Number\(src && src\.src\) \|\| 0\)/);
  assert.match(app, /out: item\.out, src: item\.src/);
  assert.match(app, /out: clip\.out, src: clip\.src/);
  assert.match(app, /for await \(const windowData of streamCaptionPcmWindows\(/);
  assert.match(app, /engine\.mount\(workerFs, \{ blobs: \[\{ name: inputName, data: file \}\] \}, mountDir\)/);
  assert.match(app, /CAPTION_STREAM_OVERLAP_SECONDS = 1\.5/);
  assert.match(app, /Core\.planCaptionStreamWindows\(clipLength, CAPTION_STREAM_WINDOW_SECONDS, CAPTION_STREAM_OVERLAP_SECONDS\)/);
  assert.match(app, /yield \{ pcm: normalizedPcm, \.\.\.planWindow \}/);
});

test('windowed decode does not use invalid compressed-byte slicing or retain all PCM windows', () => {
  const start = app.indexOf('async function* streamCaptionPcmWindows');
  const end = app.indexOf('async function transcribeCaptionPcmWithFallback', start);
  assert.ok(start >= 0 && end > start, 'windowed decoder must be present');
  const decoder = app.slice(start, end);
  assert.doesNotMatch(decoder, /file\.slice\s*\(/);
  assert.doesNotMatch(decoder, /windowChunks|totalSamples\s*\+?=/);
  assert.match(decoder, /await engine\.deleteFile\(outputName\)/);
  assert.match(decoder, /await engine\.unmount\(mountDir\)/);
  assert.match(decoder, /WORKERFS/);
});

test('each streamed caption window is transcribed before its PCM buffer is released', () => {
  assert.match(app, /const windowSource = \{ \.\.\.source, start: \(Number\(source\.start\) \|\| 0\) \+ windowData\.offsetSeconds \/ speed \}/);
  assert.match(app, /await transcribeCaptionPcmWithFallback\(windowData\.pcm, windowSource/);
  assert.match(app, /windowData\.pcm = null/);
  assert.match(app, /decodeComplete = true/);
});

test('the global fallback helper does not reach into the capGo handler local scope', () => {
  const start = app.indexOf('async function transcribeCaptionPcmWithFallback');
  const end = app.indexOf('async function commitCaptionDrafts', start);
  assert.ok(start >= 0 && end > start);
  const helper = app.slice(start, end);
  assert.doesNotMatch(helper, /captionMetric/);
});

'use strict';

const end = app.indexOf('async function captionFrom', start);

test('Whisper uses its default transcription task unless translation is requested', () => {
  assert.match(source, /\.\.\.\(\$\('capTr'\)\.checked\s*\s*\?\s*\(lang\s*\?\s*\{\s*task:\s*'translate'\s*\}\s*:\s*\{\s*forced_decoder_ids:\s*\[\[1,\s*null\],\s*\[2,\s*50358\]\]\s*\}\)\s*:\s*\{\s*\}\)/);
  assert.doesNotMatch(source, /task:\s*\$\('capTr'\)\.checked\s*\?\s*'translate'\s*:\s*'transcribe'/);
});

test('selected language is still passed to Whisper when explicitly chosen', () => {
  assert.match(source, /\.\.\.\(lang\s*\?\s*\{\s*language:\s*lang\s*\}\s*:\s*\{\s*\}\)/);
});


test('Whisper word-level DTW is disabled for the bundled tiny model', () => {
  assert.match(app, /let asrWordTimestamps = false/);
});

test('WebGPU speech inference selects explicit encoder and decoder precision', () => {
  assert.match(app, /options\.dtype = \{ encoder_model: 'fp32', decoder_model_merged: 'q4' \}/);
});

  assert.match(app, /function normalizeQuietCaptionAudio\(pcm\)/);

test('an explicitly selected video clip does not silently switch to another lane', () => {

test('with no selection, separate audio tracks remain fallback sources after main video clips', () => {
  assert.match(app, /\[\.\.\.S\.main\.map\(videoCandidate\), \.\.\.S\.audio\.map\(audioCandidate\)\]/);
});

function lum(hex){const n=parseInt(hex.slice(1),16);const c=[(n>>16)&255,(n>>8)&255,n&255].map(v=>{v/=255;return v<=.04045?v/12.92:((v+.055)/1.055)**2.4;});return .2126*c[0]+.7152*c[1]+.0722*c[2];}
function ratio(a,b){const [hi,lo]=[lum(a),lum(b)].sort((x,y)=>y-x);return(hi+.05)/(lo+.05);}

test('core editor libraries are local, version-pinned and protected by Subresource Integrity',()=>{
  const files=[
    ['./vendor/media-libraries/fabric.min.js','sha256-uwRC1ppNRnMyD2ibxuFsBaDj8WzPv1UfOU2Qp+fPx3I='],
    ['./vendor/media-libraries/anime.min.js','sha256-tc4b48P1MPGS4PJXHRlChGCW1mEZy62jS/3JEsSHPzU='],
    ['./vendor/media-libraries/dexie.min.js','sha256-2JhqFVK0mn2MsIWpLGaYWuvzuzO2Stb3uu0kYdm2R/8=']
  ];
  for(const [src,integrity] of files){
    assert.ok(html.includes('src="'+src+'"'),'missing local library '+src);
    assert.ok(html.includes('integrity="'+integrity+'"'),'missing pinned SRI for '+src);
    assert.ok(sw.includes("'"+src+"'"),'offline shell does not precache '+src);
  }
  assert.doesNotMatch(html,/https:\/\/(?:cdnjs\.cloudflare\.com|unpkg\.com)\/(?:ajax\/libs\/(?:fabric\.js|animejs)|dexie)/);
  assert.match(sw,/pmy-v26/);
  assert.ok(workflow.includes('vendor/media-libraries/fabric.min.js'));
  assert.ok(workflow.includes('vendor/media-libraries/anime.min.js'));
  assert.ok(workflow.includes('vendor/media-libraries/dexie.min.js'));
  assert.ok(workflow.includes('vendor/media-libraries/LICENSES.md'));
});

  for(const color of ['#00DDEB','#168BFF','#946CF7']) assert.ok(ratio('#071321',color)>=4.5,'dark button text must meet AA on '+color);

FILE: tests/core.test.js
BLOB SHA: 657e5d1bc6216f6fd8e02006655b9f3e6b20a646

// Run with: node --test tests/
// Verifies the caption timing logic against synthetic speech whose true timing is known exactly.
const test = require('node:test');
const assert = require('node:assert/strict');
const Core = require('../mediay-core.js');

const SR = 16000;
// Deterministic "speech": noise bursts (words) with known start/end, separated by pauses, over a faint noise floor.
function makeSpeech(words, seconds, seed = 7) {
  let state = seed;
  const rand = () => (state = (state * 1664525 + 1013904223) % 4294967296) / 4294967296 - 0.5;
  const pcm = new Float32Array(Math.round(seconds * SR));
  for (let i = 0; i < pcm.length; i++) pcm[i] = rand() * 0.004;
  for (const w of words) {
    const a = Math.round(w.start * SR), b = Math.min(pcm.length, Math.round(w.end * SR));
    for (let i = a; i < b; i++) {
      const env = Math.sin(Math.PI * (i - a) / (b - a)) ** 0.5;
      pcm[i] += rand() * 1.2 * env * Math.sin(2 * Math.PI * 180 * i / SR + 1);
    }
  }
  return pcm;
}
// Three sentences, 4 words each, 0.9 s of silence between sentences.
function buildScript() {
  const words = []; let t = 0.7;
  const sentences = [['The', 'city', 'council', 'approved'], ['new', 'roads', 'and', 'schools'], ['Residents', 'say', 'thanks', 'today']];
  const segs = [];
  for (const s of sentences) {
    const start = t;
    for (const text of s) { const len = 0.2 + text.length * 0.05; words.push({ text, start: t, end: t + len }); t += len + 0.06; }
    segs.push({ text: s.join(' '), start, end: t - 0.06 });
    t += 0.9;
  }
  return { words, segs, seconds: t + 0.5 };
}
const median = list => [...list].sort((a, b) => a - b)[Math.floor(list.length / 2)];

test('speech activity finds each sentence', () => {
  const { words, seconds } = buildScript();
  const activity = Core.analyzeSpeechActivity(makeSpeech(words, seconds), SR);
  assert.equal(activity.reliable, true);
  assert.equal(activity.intervals.length, 3);
  assert.ok(Math.abs(activity.intervals[0][0] - words[0].start) < 0.08);
});

test('noise-only or constant audio is reported unreliable so the model timestamps are kept', () => {
  let s = 3; const pcm = new Float32Array(SR * 10);
  for (let i = 0; i < pcm.length; i++) pcm[i] = ((s = (s * 1664525 + 1013904223) % 4294967296) / 4294967296 - 0.5) * 0.5;
  assert.equal(Core.analyzeSpeechActivity(pcm, SR).reliable, false);
  assert.equal(Core.analyzeSpeechActivity(new Float32Array(SR * 5), SR).reliable, false);
  assert.equal(Core.analyzeSpeechActivity(new Float32Array(10), SR).reliable, false);
});

test('captions start where speech starts even when the model timestamps are 0.4 s early and 0.6 s late', () => {
  const { words, segs, seconds } = buildScript();
  const duration = seconds;
  const activity = Core.analyzeSpeechActivity(makeSpeech(words, seconds), SR);
  const wrong = segs.map(s => ({ text: s.text, start: Math.max(0, s.start - 0.4), end: s.end + 0.6 }));
  const lines = Core.buildCaptionLines(wrong, activity, duration);
  assert.equal(lines.length, 3);
  const errors = lines.map((line, i) => Math.abs(line.start - words[i * 4].start));
  assert.ok(median(errors) < 0.12, `median start error ${median(errors)}`);
  assert.ok(Math.max(...errors) < 0.3, `max start error ${Math.max(...errors)}`);
  // The old behaviour (trust the model) would have been off by the full 0.4 s:
  assert.ok(Math.abs(wrong[0].start - words[0].start) >= 0.39);
});

test('lines never overlap, stay inside the audio, and keep a minimum duration', () => {
  const { words, segs, seconds } = buildScript();
  const activity = Core.analyzeSpeechActivity(makeSpeech(words, seconds), SR);
  const lines = Core.buildCaptionLines(segs, activity, seconds);
  lines.forEach((line, i) => {
    assert.ok(line.start >= 0 && line.end <= seconds + 1e-9);
    assert.ok(line.end - line.start >= 0.03);
    if (lines[i + 1]) assert.ok(line.end <= lines[i + 1].start + 1e-9);
  });
});

test('model word times are blended with speech timing and still land on the right word', () => {
  const { words, seconds } = buildScript();
  const activity = Core.analyzeSpeechActivity(makeSpeech(words, seconds), SR);
  const biased = words.map(w => ({ text: w.text, start: w.start + 0.25, end: w.end + 0.25 }));
  const lines = Core.buildCaptionLines(Core.groupWordsIntoPhrases(biased), activity, seconds);
  const errors = lines.map((line, i) => Math.abs(line.start - words[i * 4].start));
  assert.ok(median(errors) < 0.12, `median ${median(errors)}`);
});

test('unreliable audio falls back to the model times and never crashes', () => {
  const lines = Core.buildCaptionLines([{ text: 'alpha beta gamma delta', start: 0, end: 1 }], { reliable: false, intervals: [] }, 5);
  assert.equal(lines.length, 1);
  assert.equal(lines[0].text, 'alpha beta gamma delta');
  assert.ok(lines[0].start >= 0 && lines[0].end > lines[0].start);
  assert.deepEqual(Core.buildCaptionLines([], null, 5), []);
  assert.deepEqual(Core.buildCaptionLines([{ text: '[MUSIC]', start: 0, end: 2 }], null, 5), []);
  assert.equal(Core.buildCaptionLines([{ text: 'late', start: 1, end: NaN }], null, 5).length, 1); // missing end time
});

test('chunk planning cuts in a pause, never mid-speech, and never exceeds 30 s', () => {
  // 70 s: speech everywhere except a pause at 26–27.5 s and 54–55.5 s.
  const words = [];
  for (let t = 0.5; t < 69; t += 0.8) if (!((t > 26 && t < 27.5) || (t > 54 && t < 55.5))) words.push({ start: t, end: t + 0.5 });
  const activity = Core.analyzeSpeechActivity(makeSpeech(words, 70), SR);
  const plan = Core.planCaptionChunks(70, activity);
  assert.ok(plan.every(([a, b]) => b - a <= 30.0001));
  assert.equal(plan[0][0], 0); assert.equal(plan[plan.length - 1][1], 70);
  for (let i = 1; i < plan.length; i++) assert.equal(plan[i][0], plan[i - 1][1]); // contiguous, nothing dropped
  assert.ok(plan[0][1] > 26 && plan[0][1] < 27.5, `first cut ${plan[0][1]} should be inside the pause`);
  // Without usable speech information the old fixed 30-second windows are kept.
  assert.deepEqual(Core.planCaptionChunks(61, { reliable: false }), [[0, 30], [30, 60], [60, 61]]);
});

test('silent windows are not sent to the speech model', () => {
  const { words, seconds } = buildScript();
  const activity = Core.analyzeSpeechActivity(makeSpeech(words, seconds), SR);
  assert.equal(Core.hasSpeech(activity, words[0].start, words[3].end), true);
  assert.equal(Core.hasSpeech(activity, words[3].end + 0.1, words[3].end + 0.7), false);
  assert.equal(Core.hasSpeech({ reliable: false }, 0, 1), true); // unknown: let the model decide
});

test('source time maps onto the timeline using clip start and speed', () => {
  assert.equal(Core.sourceToTimeline(2, 10, 1), 12);
  assert.equal(Core.sourceToTimeline(2, 10, 2), 11);
  assert.equal(Core.sourceToTimeline(2, 10, 0.5), 14);
  assert.equal(Core.sourceToTimeline(2, 10, 0), 12); // invalid speed behaves as 1x
});

test('word grouping joins stray punctuation and breaks on pauses and sentence ends', () => {
  const phrases = Core.groupWordsIntoPhrases([
    { text: 'Hello', start: 1, end: 1.1 }, { text: 'there', start: 1.15, end: 1.4 }, { text: ',', start: 1.4, end: 1.45 },
    { text: 'friend.', start: 1.5, end: 1.9 }, { text: 'Again', start: 2.0, end: 2.3 }, { text: 'later', start: 5, end: 5.3 }
  ]);
  assert.deepEqual(phrases.map(p => p.text), ['Hello there, friend.', 'Again', 'later']);
});


test('mixed word and phrase output keeps phrase segments between adjacent word runs', () => {
  const merged = Core.mergeCaptionSegments([
    { text: 'one', start: 1, end: 1.2, word: true },
    { text: 'two', start: 1.25, end: 1.45, word: true },
    { text: 'middle phrase here', start: 1.5, end: 2.2, word: false },
    { text: 'three', start: 2.25, end: 2.5, word: true },
    { text: 'four', start: 2.55, end: 2.8, word: true }
  ]);
  assert.deepEqual(merged.map(segment => segment.text), ['one two', 'middle phrase here', 'three four']);
  assert.ok(merged[0].end <= merged[1].start);
  assert.ok(merged[1].end <= merged[2].start);
});

test('caption lines break at real speech-interval boundaries and never bridge a long silence', () => {
  const activity = { reliable: true, intervals: [[1, 1.8], [5, 5.8]] };
  const lines = Core.buildCaptionLines([
    { text: 'first sentence', start: 1, end: 5.8 }
  ], activity, 7, { startSlackBefore: 0, endSlackAfter: 0, lead: 0, hold: 0, minDuration: 0.05, bridgeGap: 0 });
  assert.equal(lines.length, 2);
  assert.match(lines[0].text, /first/);
  assert.match(lines[1].text, /sentence/);
  assert.ok(lines[0].end <= 1.8 + 1e-9);
  assert.ok(lines[1].start >= 5 - 1e-9);
  assert.ok(lines[1].start - lines[0].end >= 3);
});


test('short continuous speech remains usable when voiced frames exceed 92 percent', () => {
  const words = [];
  for (let start = 0; start < 13; start += 4) {
    words.push({ text: 'continuous speech sample', start: start + 0.1, end: Math.min(13, start + 3.85) });
  }
  const activity = Core.analyzeSpeechActivity(makeSpeech(words, 13, 29), SR);
  assert.ok(activity.voicedRatio > 0.92, `expected mostly voiced audio, got ${activity.voicedRatio}`);
  assert.equal(activity.reliable, true, `dynamic range ${activity.dynamicRangeDb} dB, voiced ratio ${activity.voicedRatio}`);
});


test('speech resampling suppresses frequencies above the new Nyquist limit', () => {
  const inputRate = 48000, outputRate = 16000, seconds = 1;
  const input = new Float32Array(inputRate * seconds);
  for (let i = 0; i < input.length; i++) input[i] = Math.sin(2 * Math.PI * 12000 * i / inputRate);
  const output = Core.resamplePcm(input, inputRate, outputRate);
  const rms = Math.sqrt(output.reduce((sum, value) => sum + value * value, 0) / output.length);
  assert.ok(output.length === outputRate * seconds);
  assert.ok(rms < 0.08, `aliased 12 kHz tone RMS was ${rms}`);
});

test('speech resampling preserves a voice-band tone', () => {
  const inputRate = 48000, outputRate = 16000, seconds = 1;
  const input = new Float32Array(inputRate * seconds);
  for (let i = 0; i < input.length; i++) input[i] = 0.5 * Math.sin(2 * Math.PI * 1000 * i / inputRate);
  const output = Core.resamplePcm(input, inputRate, outputRate);
  const rms = Math.sqrt(output.reduce((sum, value) => sum + value * value, 0) / output.length);
  assert.ok(rms > 0.3 && rms < 0.4, `1 kHz tone RMS was ${rms}`);
});

test('repeating Whisper token loops are collapsed without deleting normal repetition', () => {
  assert.equal(Core.stripRepeatingLoops('hello hello hello hello hello hello hello hello'), 'hello');
  assert.equal(Core.stripRepeatingLoops('we go home we go home we go home we go home we go home'), 'we go home');
  assert.equal(Core.stripRepeatingLoops('we really really need this'), 'we really really need this');
});

test('caption builder safely ignores null segments and music-only output', () => {
  const lines = Core.buildCaptionLines([
    null,
    { text: '♪ ♫ ♪', start: 0, end: 1 },
    { text: 'Real words here', start: 1, end: 2 }
  ], null, 3);
  assert.equal(lines.length, 1);
  assert.equal(lines[0].text, 'Real words here');
});

test('caption lines break at Whisper segment boundaries even across short pauses', () => {
  const activity = { reliable: true, intervals: [[0.8, 1.8], [2.1, 3.1]] };
  const lines = Core.buildCaptionLines([
    { text: 'first sentence', start: 1, end: 1.8 },
    { text: 'second sentence', start: 2.1, end: 3.0 }
  ], activity, 4);
  assert.equal(lines.length, 2, JSON.stringify(lines));
  assert.match(lines[0].text, /first sentence/);
  assert.match(lines[1].text, /second sentence/);
});

test('widely spaced identical speech is preserved instead of being collapsed as a Whisper loop', () => {
  const realRepeatedSpeech = [
    { text: 'Thank you.', start: 0, end: 1 },
    { text: 'Thank you.', start: 10, end: 11 },
    { text: 'Thank you.', start: 20, end: 21 },
    { text: 'Thank you.', start: 30, end: 31 }
  ];
  const realLines = Core.buildCaptionLines(realRepeatedSpeech, { reliable: false, intervals: [] }, 40);
  assert.equal(realLines.length, 4);

  const loopThenNewSpeech = [
    ...Array.from({ length: 8 }, (_, i) => ({ text: 'Thank you.', start: i * 2, end: i * 2 + 1.8 })),
    { text: 'Welcome back.', start: 17, end: 18.5 }
  ];
  const afterLoop = Core.buildCaptionLines(loopThenNewSpeech, { reliable: false, intervals: [] }, 20);
  assert.equal(afterLoop.length, 2);
  assert.equal(afterLoop[1].text, 'Welcome back.');
  assert.ok(afterLoop[1].start >= 16.9); // The caption renderer intentionally applies a 40 ms visual lead-in.
});

test('large caption windows overlap for boundary context but own contiguous non-overlapping source ranges', () => {
  const windows = Core.planCaptionStreamWindows(1200, 600, 1.5);
  assert.equal(windows.length, 2);
  assert.equal(windows[0].offsetSeconds, 0);
  assert.equal(windows[0].durationSeconds, 600.75);
  assert.equal(windows[1].offsetSeconds, 599.25);
  assert.equal(windows[1].durationSeconds, 600.75);
  assert.equal(windows[0].ownershipStartSeconds, 0);
  assert.equal(windows[0].ownershipEndSeconds, 599.25);
  assert.equal(windows[1].ownershipStartSeconds, 599.25);
  assert.equal(windows[1].ownershipEndSeconds, 1200);
  for (let i = 0; i < windows.length; i++) {
    assert.ok(windows[i].ownershipEndSeconds > windows[i].ownershipStartSeconds);
    if (windows[i + 1]) {
      assert.equal(windows[i].ownershipEndSeconds, windows[i + 1].ownershipStartSeconds);
      assert.ok(windows[i].offsetSeconds + windows[i].durationSeconds > windows[i + 1].offsetSeconds);
    }
  }
  assert.deepEqual(Core.planCaptionStreamWindows(0, 600, 1.5), []);
  assert.equal(Core.planCaptionStreamWindows(601, 600, 1.5).length, 2);
});

const test = require('node:test');
const assert = require('node:assert/strict');
const fs = require('node:fs');
const path = require('node:path');

const app = fs.readFileSync(path.join(__dirname, '..', 'app.js'), 'utf8');
const workflow = fs.readFileSync(path.join(__dirname, '..', '.github', 'workflows', 'built.yml'), 'utf8');

test('Android export saves to Movies/MediaY before offering a share fallback', () => {
  const start = app.indexOf('async function saveVideo');
  const end = app.indexOf('// Native Android builds register this plugin', start);
  assert.ok(start >= 0 && end > start, 'saveVideo implementation must exist');
  const save = app.slice(start, end);
  const deviceSave = save.indexOf('MediaYMediaStore.saveVideo');
  const deviceResult = save.indexOf("method: 'device'");
  const fallbackShare = save.indexOf('Share.share');
  assert.ok(deviceSave >= 0, 'Android direct-save plugin call must exist');
  assert.ok(deviceResult > deviceSave, 'successful device save must be reported');
  assert.ok(fallbackShare > deviceResult, 'sharing may only be a fallback after direct saving');
  assert.match(save, /location:.*Movies\/MediaY/);
  assert.doesNotMatch(app, /pendingExportShare/, 'successful background exports must not queue an automatic share sheet');
});

test('failed Android cache appends remove the partially written MP4', () => {
  const start = app.indexOf('async function saveVideo');
  const end = app.indexOf('// Native Android builds register this plugin', start);
  const save = app.slice(start, end);
  const firstWrite = save.indexOf('await Filesystem.writeFile(options)');
  const markCreated = save.indexOf('cacheFileCreated = true', firstWrite);
  const append = save.indexOf('await Filesystem.appendFile(options)');
  const cleanup = save.lastIndexOf('if (cacheFileCreated) await Filesystem.deleteFile');
  assert.ok(firstWrite >= 0 && markCreated > firstWrite, 'cache creation must be recorded after the first successful write');
  assert.ok(append > markCreated, 'the partial cache must be marked before a later append can fail');
  assert.ok(cleanup > append, 'the catch path must delete a partial cache file');
});

test('Android build registers a MediaStore plugin that publishes MP4s to public Movies/MediaY', () => {
  assert.match(workflow, /registerPlugin\(MediaYMediaStorePlugin\.class\)/);
  assert.match(workflow, /MediaStore\.Video\.Media\.RELATIVE_PATH/);
  assert.match(workflow, /Environment\.DIRECTORY_MOVIES \+ "\/MediaY"/);
  assert.match(workflow, /MediaStore\.Video\.Media\.IS_PENDING/);
});


test('a completed render remains in FFmpeg storage after a delivery failure and can be retried', () => {
  assert.match(app, /let pendingExportSave = null/);
  assert.match(app, /pendingExportSave = \{ engine: ff, path: out \}/);
  assert.match(app, /if \(preserveOutputPath && name === preserveOutputPath\) continue/);
  assert.match(app, /async function retryPendingExportSave\(\)/);
  assert.match(app, /retry\.onclick = retryPendingExportSave/);
  assert.match(app, /pendingExportSave && pendingExportSave\.engine === ff/);
  assert.match(app, /error\.renderedData = data/);
  assert.match(app, /pendingExportSave = \{ engine: null, path: null, data: saveError\.renderedData \}/);
  assert.match(app, /pending\.data \? await saveVideo\(pending\.data\)/);
  assert.match(app, /if \(pending\.engine\) \{ try \{ await pending\.engine\.deleteFile\(pending\.path\)/);
  assert.match(app, /A completed render is waiting to be saved/);
});

'use strict';
const test = require('node:test');
const assert = require('node:assert/strict');
const fs = require('node:fs');
const path = require('node:path');

const app = fs.readFileSync(path.join(__dirname, '..', 'app.js'), 'utf8');

test('overlay PNG rasterization scales to the selected export width', () => {
  assert.match(app, /async function overlayPNG\(o, outputWidth = canvas\.getWidth\(\)\)/);
  assert.match(app, /const multiplier = Math\.max\(1, Number\(outputWidth\) \/ Math\.max\(1, canvas\.getWidth\(\)\)\)/);
  assert.match(app, /canvas\.toDataURL\(\{ format: 'png', multiplier \}\)/);
});

test('both FFmpeg and WebCodecs export paths request output-resolution overlay PNGs', () => {
  assert.match(app, /overlayPNG\(overlay, W\)/);
  assert.match(app, /overlayPNG\(o, W\), W, H/);
});

test('WebCodecs frame-count mismatch declines the fast path before muxing', () => {
  assert.match(app, /sink\.frameCount !== chunkFrames/);
  assert.match(app, /using the FFmpeg fallback/);
});

'use strict';
const test = require('node:test');
const assert = require('node:assert/strict');
const fs = require('node:fs');
const path = require('node:path');

const app = fs.readFileSync(path.join(__dirname, '..', 'app.js'), 'utf8');

test('muted picture-in-picture video audio is not unexpectedly mixed into exports', () => {
  assert.match(app, /el\.muted\s*=\s*true/);
  assert.match(app, /const ovA = \[\];/);
  assert.match(app, /Do not unexpectedly mix their hidden audio into the exported soundtrack/);
});

test('dropping media caches releases decoded audio buffers and thumbnail data', () => {
  const start = app.indexOf('function dropMediaCaches()');
  const end = app.indexOf('// Does by itself what closing', start);
  assert.ok(start >= 0 && end > start);
  const source = app.slice(start, end);
  assert.match(source, /for \(const id of Object\.keys\(decoded\)\) delete decoded\[id\]/);
  assert.match(source, /for \(const id of Object\.keys\(thumbs\)\) delete thumbs\[id\]/);
});

test('undo history remains bounded and Fabric overlay media is explicitly disposed', () => {
  assert.match(app, /undoStack\.length > 50/);
  assert.match(app, /function disposeOverlayObject\(object, overlay\)/);
  assert.match(app, /object\.dispose\(\)/);
  assert.match(app, /element\.pause\(\)/);
  assert.match(app, /element\.removeAttribute\('src'\)/);
  assert.match(app, /for \(const object of canvas\.getObjects\(\)\.slice\(\)/);
});

test('overlay rebuild releases Fabric-owned video elements and ignores stale asynchronous rebuilds', () => {
  assert.match(app, /object\.getElement\(\)/);
  assert.match(app, /let rebuildGeneration = 0/);
  assert.match(app, /generation !== rebuildGeneration/);
  assert.match(app, /element\.removeAttribute\('src'\)/);
});

test('OOM retry detection matches the oom token, not arbitrary words containing those letters', () => {
  assert.match(app, /\\boom\\b/);
  assert.doesNotMatch(app, /allocation failed\|out of memory\|oom\/i/);
});


test('FFmpeg cache is purged only after an integrity or WebAssembly instantiation failure', () => {
  assert.match(app, /function isFFmpegCacheIntegrityError\(error\)/);
  assert.match(app, /if \(isFFmpegCacheIntegrityError\(error\) && window\.caches\)/);
  const start = app.indexOf('async function loadFFmpegEngine()');
  const end = app.indexOf('// One shared load:', start);
  assert.ok(start >= 0 && end > start);
  const load = app.slice(start, end);
  assert.doesNotMatch(load, /if \(window\.caches\) caches\.delete\(ENGINE_CACHE\)/);
});


test('skip_loop_filter is restricted to the explicit Fast Draft preset', () => {
  assert.match(app, /fastDraft: false/);
  assert.match(app, /EXP\.fastDraft = false/);
  assert.match(app, /EXP\.fastDraft = name === 'draft'/);
  assert.match(app, /hiRate && EXP\.fastDraft \? \[true, false\] : \[false\]/);
});



================================================================================
FILE: tests/export_integration.py
BLOB SHA: 3be562b738444b3cb9ef4b24783aeddddd10c335

"""Real export test: real app + real FFmpeg.wasm + real H.264 encode, then inspects the MP4 that comes out.

Checks, for a single clip and for two clips with a fade transition and a timed text overlay:
  - the export completes and is a playable H.264 + AAC MP4 at the requested size,
  - its duration matches the timeline,
  - the transition occupies the right part of the timeline,
  - the overlay appears and disappears where the timeline says (within two frames).
Needs: ffmpeg + ffprobe on PATH, Playwright, and internet access (the app downloads Fabric, Dexie and the 31 MB FFmpeg
core from CDNs). Offline: set MEDIAY_LOCAL_LIBS to a folder containing node_modules with fabric, animejs, dexie, lucide and
@ffmpeg/core (npm packages) and those requests are served from disk instead.
Run: python tests/export_integration.py
"""
import functools, http.server, json, os, socketserver, subprocess, sys, tempfile, threading, time
from pathlib import Path
from playwright.sync_api import sync_playwright

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
LOCAL = os.environ.get("MEDIAY_LOCAL_LIBS")
FPS = 24

def sh(*cmd, **kw):
    return subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=kw.pop("text", True), check=True, **kw)

def make_clips(folder):
    a, b = Path(folder) / "a.webm", Path(folder) / "b.webm"
    common = ["-vf", "format=yuv420p", "-c:v", "libvpx", "-b:v", "300k", "-auto-alt-ref", "0", "-deadline", "realtime", "-cpu-used", "8", "-c:a", "libopus", "-shortest"]
    sh("ffmpeg", "-y", "-loglevel", "error", "-f", "lavfi", "-i", "testsrc2=size=640x360:rate=30:duration=3", "-f", "lavfi", "-i", "sine=frequency=440:sample_rate=44100:duration=3", *common, str(a))
    sh("ffmpeg", "-y", "-loglevel", "error", "-f", "lavfi", "-i", "rgbtestsrc=size=640x360:rate=30:duration=3", "-f", "lavfi", "-i", "sine=frequency=880:sample_rate=44100:duration=3", *common, str(b))
    return str(a), str(b)

def make_silent_clip(folder, name="silent.webm", duration=2, size="640x360"):
    out = Path(folder) / name
    sh("ffmpeg", "-y", "-loglevel", "error", "-f", "lavfi", "-i", f"color=c=navy:s={size}:r=30:d={duration}",
       "-vf", "format=yuv420p", "-c:v", "libvpx", "-b:v", "200k", "-deadline", "realtime", "-cpu-used", "8", "-an", str(out))
    return str(out)

def make_tone(folder, name, freq, duration=1.5):
    out = Path(folder) / name
    sh("ffmpeg", "-y", "-loglevel", "error", "-f", "lavfi", "-i", f"sine=frequency={freq}:sample_rate=44100:duration={duration}",
       "-c:a", "libopus", str(out))
    return str(out)

def make_voiced_clip(folder, name="pip_voiced.webm", duration=2, freq=880, size="320x180"):
    out = Path(folder) / name
    sh("ffmpeg", "-y", "-loglevel", "error", "-f", "lavfi", "-i", f"color=c=blue:s={size}:r=30:d={duration}",
       "-f", "lavfi", "-i", f"sine=frequency={freq}:sample_rate=44100:duration={duration}",
       "-vf", "format=yuv420p", "-c:v", "libvpx", "-b:v", "200k", "-deadline", "realtime", "-cpu-used", "8",
       "-c:a", "libopus", "-shortest", str(out))
    return str(out)

def probe(path):
    j = json.loads(sh("ffprobe", "-v", "error", "-show_entries", "format=duration:stream=codec_type,codec_name,width,height", "-of", "json", path).stdout)
    return float(j["format"]["duration"]), [(s["codec_type"], s["codec_name"], s.get("width"), s.get("height")) for s in j["streams"]]

def rms_db(path, start, length):
    output = sh("ffmpeg", "-v", "info", "-ss", str(start), "-t", str(length), "-i", path,
                "-af", "astats=metadata=1:reset=0", "-f", "null", "-").stderr
    values = []
    for line in output.splitlines():
        if "RMS level dB:" not in line:
            continue
        value = line.rsplit(":", 1)[-1].strip()
        if value.lower() != "-inf":
            try:
                values.append(float(value))
            except ValueError:
                pass
    return max(values) if values else -120.0

def overlay_window(path, quiet_time, start, length, w=120, h=214):
    """Seconds at which text appears/disappears in the bottom third, found from frame differences."""
    raw = sh("ffmpeg", "-v", "error", "-ss", str(start), "-t", str(length), "-i", path, "-vf", f"fps=30,scale={w}:{h},format=gray", "-f", "rawvideo", "-", text=False).stdout
    n = len(raw) // (w * h)
    frames = [raw[i * w * h:(i + 1) * w * h] for i in range(n)]
    crop = lambda f: [f[y * w + x] for y in range(int(h * .62), int(h * .82)) for x in range(w)]
    base = crop(frames[int((quiet_time - start) * 30)])
    diffs = [sum(abs(p - q) for p, q in zip(crop(f), base)) / len(base) for f in frames]
    on = [i for i, d in enumerate(diffs) if d > 4]
    return start + on[0] / 30, start + (on[-1] + 1) / 30

def run_export(base, files, setup_js, route_libs, assets=None):
    with sync_playwright() as p:
        browser = p.chromium.launch(headless=True, args=["--no-sandbox", "--disable-dev-shm-usage"])
        context = browser.new_context(viewport={"width": 390, "height": 844}, is_mobile=True, has_touch=True, service_workers="block")
        route_libs(context)
        for asset_name, asset_path in (assets or {}).items():
            context.route(f"**/{asset_name.lstrip('/')}", lambda route, _request, path=str(asset_path): route.fulfill(path=path))
        context.add_init_script("try{localStorage.setItem('pmy-consent-v1','yes')}catch(e){}")
        page = context.new_page(); errors = []; browser_logs = []
        page.on("pageerror", lambda e: errors.append(str(e)))
        page.on("console", lambda msg: browser_logs.append(f"{msg.type}: {msg.text}") if msg.type == "error" else None)
        page.goto(base + "/index.html", wait_until="load")
        page.wait_for_selector("#homeScreen:not([hidden])", timeout=30000)
        page.locator("#hmNew").click(); page.wait_for_selector("#setup:not([hidden])")
        page.locator("#suStart").click(); page.wait_for_function("document.querySelector('#homeScreen').hidden === true")
        page.set_input_files("#mediaInput", files)
        page.wait_for_function(f"S.main.length === {len(files)}", timeout=60000)
        page.wait_for_function("document.querySelector('#busyOverlay').hidden === true", timeout=60000)
        page.evaluate(setup_js)
        # Wait for the browser download itself, then save it before closing Chromium.
        # A page.on("download") callback can still be saving the artifact when browser.close()
        # runs, which cancels Download.save_as and makes a successful export look like a failure.
        try:
            with page.expect_download(timeout=180000) as download_info:
                page.evaluate("() => { $('goExport').onclick(null, 0); return true; }")
            download = download_info.value
        except Exception as error:
            diagnostics = page.evaluate("""() => ({
              status: document.getElementById('status')?.textContent || '',
              busy: document.getElementById('busyOverlay')?.innerText || '',
              progress: document.getElementById('busyProgress')?.getAttribute('aria-valuenow') || '',
              ffLogs: (window.ffLogs || []).slice(-30),
              native: !!(window.Capacitor && window.Capacitor.isNativePlatform && window.Capacitor.isNativePlatform())
            })""")
            browser.close()
            raise AssertionError(f"Export did not deliver a browser download: {error}; diagnostics={diagnostics}; pageErrors={errors[:10]}; consoleErrors={browser_logs[-15:]}") from error
        with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(suffix=".mp4", delete=False) as output:
            saved_path = output.name
        download.save_as(saved_path)
        status = page.evaluate("document.getElementById('status').textContent")
        total = page.evaluate("total()")
        browser.close()
    return saved_path, status, total, errors

def main():
    class Quiet(http.server.SimpleHTTPRequestHandler):
        def log_message(self, *a): pass
    socketserver.TCPServer.allow_reuse_address = True
    server = socketserver.ThreadingTCPServer(("127.0.0.1", 0), functools.partial(Quiet, directory=str(ROOT)))
    threading.Thread(target=server.serve_forever, daemon=True).start()
    base = f"http://127.0.0.1:{server.server_address[1]}"

    def route_libs(context):
        if not LOCAL: return
        nm = Path(LOCAL) / "node_modules"
        files = {"fabric.min.js": nm / "fabric/dist/fabric.min.js", "anime.min.js": nm / "animejs/lib/anime.min.js", "dexie.min.js": nm / "dexie/dist/dexie.min.js",
                 "lucide.min.js": nm / "lucide/dist/umd/lucide.min.js", "ffmpeg-core.js": nm / "@ffmpeg/core/dist/umd/ffmpeg-core.js", "ffmpeg-core.wasm": nm / "@ffmpeg/core/dist/umd/ffmpeg-core.wasm"}
        def handler(route):
            for name, path in files.items():
                if route.request.url.endswith(name):
                    return route.fulfill(body=path.read_bytes(), content_type="application/wasm" if name.endswith(".wasm") else "application/javascript")
            return route.abort() if "fonts.g" in route.request.url else route.continue_()
        for pattern in ("https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/**", "https://unpkg.com/**", "https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/**", "https://fonts.googleapis.com/**", "https://fonts.gstatic.com/**"):
            context.route(pattern, handler)

    folder = tempfile.mkdtemp()
    clip_a, clip_b = make_clips(folder)
    fast = f"EXP.res = 480; EXP.fps = {FPS};"

    # 1) single clip
    t0 = time.time()
    path, status, total, errors = run_export(base, [clip_a], fast + " 0", route_libs)
    assert path, f"no file was produced ({status}; errors {errors[:2]})"
    duration, streams = probe(path)
    assert "Export complete" in status, status
    assert abs(duration - total) < 0.15, (duration, total)
    assert ("video", "h264", 480, 854) in streams and any(s[0] == "audio" for s in streams), streams
    assert not errors, errors
    print(f"PASS: single-clip export is a valid H.264+AAC MP4 of {duration:.2f} s (timeline {total:.2f} s) in {time.time() - t0:.0f} s")

    # 2) two clips + fade transition + text overlay at 4.0-5.0 s
    t0 = time.time()
    setup = fast + """(async () => { S.main[1].tr = 'fade'; S.main[1].trDur = 0.5;
      await commitCaptionDrafts([{ id: 'marker1', layer: S.nextLayer, type: 'text', caption: true, text: 'MARKER', style: 0, start: 4.0, dur: 1.0, top: RATIOS[S.ratio][1] * 0.72 }]); })()"""
    path, status, total, errors = run_export(base, [clip_a, clip_b], setup, route_libs)
    assert path, f"no file was produced ({status}; errors {errors[:2]})"
    duration, streams = probe(path)
    assert "Export complete" in status and not errors, (status, errors)
    assert abs(duration - total) < 0.2, (duration, total)       # the transition must not shorten or lengthen the timeline
    on, off = overlay_window(path, quiet_time=3.8, start=3.6, length=2.8)
    frame = 1 / FPS
    assert abs(on - 4.0) <= 2.5 * frame and abs(off - 5.0) <= 2.5 * frame, (on, off)
    print(f"PASS: two-clip export keeps the timeline length ({duration:.2f} s vs {total:.2f} s) and shows the overlay at {on:.2f}-{off:.2f} s (timeline 4.00-5.00 s) in {time.time() - t0:.0f} s")

    # 3) Silent intro + voiced clip + fade transition + silent video PiP.
    # The main audio from clip_a must survive both the silent segment and silent PiP.
    t0 = time.time()
    silent_intro = make_silent_clip(folder, "intro_silent.webm", duration=2)
    pip_silent = make_silent_clip(folder, "pip_silent.webm", duration=1, size="320x180")
    setup = fast + """(async () => {
      S.main[1].tr = 'fade'; S.main[1].trDur = 0.5;
      const blob = await (await fetch('/pip_silent.webm')).blob();
      const file = new File([blob], 'pip_silent.webm', { type: 'video/webm' });
      const mediaId = await storeMedia(file);
      await addOverlay({ type: 'video', mediaId, name: file.name, start: 0.5, dur: 1.0, sourceIn: 0, srcDur: 1.0 });
    })()"""
    path, status, total, errors = run_export(base, [silent_intro, clip_a], setup, route_libs,
                                             assets={"pip_silent.webm": pip_silent})
    duration, streams = probe(path)
    assert "Export complete" in status and not errors, (status, errors)
    assert abs(duration - total) < 0.2, (duration, total)
    assert any(s[0] == "audio" for s in streams), f"missing AAC after silent intro: {streams}"
    assert rms_db(path, 2.5, 1.5) > -30.0, "voiced main clip audio was lost after silent intro / silent PiP"
    print(f"PASS: silent main segment and silent PiP preserve the voiced clip's audio ({duration:.2f} s) in {time.time() - t0:.0f} s")

    # 4) Two external voice-overs on a silent main clip. The later track must not be
    # truncated by the duration of the first (earlier) track.
    t0 = time.time()
    silent_main = make_silent_clip(folder, "silent_main.webm", duration=3)
    voice_early = make_tone(folder, "voice_early.webm", 440, duration=0.8)
    voice_late = make_tone(folder, "voice_late.webm", 880, duration=0.8)
    setup = fast + """(async () => {
      for (const [url, name, start] of [
        ['/voice_early.webm', 'voice_early.webm', 0.2],
        ['/voice_late.webm', 'voice_late.webm', 1.8]
      ]) {
        const blob = await (await fetch(url)).blob();
        const file = new File([blob], name, { type: 'audio/webm' });
        const mediaId = await storeMedia(file);
        S.audio.push({ id: uid(), mediaId, name, start, in: 0, out: 0.8, src: 0.8 });
      }
    })()"""
    path, status, total, errors = run_export(base, [silent_main], setup, route_libs,
        assets={"voice_early.webm": voice_early, "voice_late.webm": voice_late})
    duration, streams = probe(path)
    assert "Export complete" in status and not errors, (status, errors)
    assert abs(duration - total) < 0.15 and abs(duration - 3.0) < 0.15, (duration, total)
    assert any(s[0] == "audio" for s in streams), f"missing mixed audio stream: {streams}"
    assert rms_db(path, 0.3, 0.5) > -30.0, "first external voice-over was not audible in its window"
    assert rms_db(path, 1.15, 0.45) < -45.0, "unexpected audio bleed in silent gap between voice-overs"
    assert rms_db(path, 1.9, 0.6) > -30.0, "second external voice-over was truncated"
    print(f"PASS: both delayed voice-overs remain independently audible and output stays within {duration:.2f} s timeline in {time.time() - t0:.0f} s")

    # 5) PiP videos are intentionally muted in preview and export because there is no
    # overlay-volume control. A voiced PiP must not add a hidden audio track.
    t0 = time.time()
    silent_main = make_silent_clip(folder, "pip_main_silent.webm", duration=3)
    pip_silent = make_silent_clip(folder, "pip_only_silent.webm", duration=1, size="320x180")
    pip_voiced = make_voiced_clip(folder, duration=2)
    setup = fast + """(async () => {
      const silentBlob = await (await fetch('/pip_only_silent.webm')).blob();
      const silentFile = new File([silentBlob], 'pip_only_silent.webm', { type: 'video/webm' });
      const silentId = await storeMedia(silentFile);
      await addOverlay({ type: 'video', mediaId: silentId, name: silentFile.name, start: 0.2, dur: 0.8, sourceIn: 0, srcDur: 1.0 });
      const voicedBlob = await (await fetch('/pip_voiced.webm')).blob();
      const voicedFile = new File([voicedBlob], 'pip_voiced.webm', { type: 'video/webm' });
      const voicedId = await storeMedia(voicedFile);
      await addOverlay({ type: 'video', mediaId: voicedId, name: voicedFile.name, start: 1.0, dur: 0.8, sourceIn: 0.5, srcDur: 2.0 });
    })()"""
    path, status, total, errors = run_export(base, [silent_main], setup, route_libs,
        assets={"pip_only_silent.webm": pip_silent, "pip_voiced.webm": pip_voiced})
    duration, streams = probe(path)
    assert "Export complete" in status and not errors, (status, errors)
    assert abs(duration - total) < 0.15, (duration, total)
    assert not any(s[0] == "audio" for s in streams), f"muted PiP unexpectedly added an audio track: {streams}"
    print(f"PASS: silent and voiced PiP overlays remain muted; no hidden PiP audio is exported ({duration:.2f} s) in {time.time() - t0:.0f} s")

    # 6) Reversed external audio-track order & overlong music track.
    # Later music is pushed first and exceeds the 3-second project timeline.
    t0 = time.time()
    silent_main_rev = make_silent_clip(folder, "silent_main_rev.webm", duration=3)
    music_overlong = make_tone(folder, "music_overlong.webm", 660, duration=5.0)
    voice_first = make_tone(folder, "voice_first.webm", 440, duration=0.8)
    setup = fast + """(async () => {
      for (const [url, name, start, outDur, srcDur] of [
        ['/music_overlong.webm', 'music_overlong.webm', 1.6, 4.5, 5.0],
        ['/voice_first.webm', 'voice_first.webm', 0.2, 0.8, 0.8]
      ]) {
        const blob = await (await fetch(url)).blob();
        const file = new File([blob], name, { type: 'audio/webm' });
        const mediaId = await storeMedia(file);
        S.audio.push({ id: uid(), mediaId, name, start, in: 0, out: outDur, src: srcDur });
      }
    })()"""
    path, status, total, errors = run_export(base, [silent_main_rev], setup, route_libs,
        assets={"music_overlong.webm": music_overlong, "voice_first.webm": voice_first})
    duration, streams = probe(path)
    assert "Export complete" in status and not errors, (status, errors)
    assert abs(duration - total) < 0.15 and abs(duration - 3.0) < 0.15, (duration, total)
    assert any(s[0] == "audio" and s[1] == "aac" for s in streams), f"missing AAC audio stream: {streams}"
    assert rms_db(path, 0.3, 0.5) > -30.0, "earlier voice track (pushed second in reverse S.audio order) was lost"
    assert rms_db(path, 1.1, 0.35) < -45.0, "unexpected audio bleed in silent gap before overlong music track"
    assert rms_db(path, 1.8, 1.0) > -30.0, "overlong music track (pushed first in reverse S.audio order) was lost"
    print(f"PASS: reversed S.audio order and overlong music track trim cleanly to {duration:.2f} s while preserving both tracks in {time.time() - t0:.0f} s")

    # 7) Short overlapping fades & voiced -> silent -> voiced xfade transitions.
    t0 = time.time()
    short_voiced_1 = make_voiced_clip(folder, name="short_voiced_1.webm", duration=0.8, freq=440, size="640x360")
    short_silent_mid = make_silent_clip(folder, name="short_silent_mid.webm", duration=0.6, size="640x360")
    short_voiced_2 = make_voiced_clip(folder, name="short_voiced_2.webm", duration=0.8, freq=880, size="640x360")
    setup = fast + """(() => {
      S.main.forEach(c => { c.fade = true; });
      S.main[1].tr = 'fade'; S.main[1].trDur = 0.2;
      S.main[2].tr = 'fade'; S.main[2].trDur = 0.2;
    })()"""
    path, status, total, errors = run_export(base, [short_voiced_1, short_silent_mid, short_voiced_2], setup, route_libs)
    duration, streams = probe(path)
    assert "Export complete" in status and not errors, (status, errors)
    assert abs(duration - total) < 0.2 and abs(total - 2.2) < 0.1, (duration, total)
    assert ("video", "h264", 480, 854) in streams, f"invalid H.264 stream header: {streams}"
    assert any(s[0] == "audio" and s[1] == "aac" for s in streams), f"missing AAC stream header: {streams}"
    assert rms_db(path, 0.1, 0.5) > -35.0, "first sub-second voiced clip lost audio across fade/xfade"
    assert rms_db(path, 1.45, 0.5) > -35.0, "second sub-second voiced clip lost audio after silent middle clip and xfade"
    print(f"PASS: sub-second voiced -> silent -> voiced clips with overlapping fades and xfade transitions preserve both voiced segments ({duration:.2f} s) in {time.time() - t0:.0f} s")

    server.shutdown()

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

'use strict';
// Unit tests for mediay-fast.js that need no browser: MP4 parsing, Annex B conversion and the planning maths.
// The MP4 fixtures are generated with ffmpeg (skipped when ffmpeg is not installed).
const test = require('node:test');
const assert = require('node:assert/strict');
const { spawnSync } = require('node:child_process');
const fs = require('node:fs');
const os = require('node:os');
const path = require('node:path');
const Fast = require('../mediay-fast.js');

const haveFfmpeg = spawnSync('ffmpeg', ['-version']).status === 0 && spawnSync('ffprobe', ['-version']).status === 0;
const tmp = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'mediay-fast-'));
const sh = (cmd, args) => { const r = spawnSync(cmd, args, { encoding: 'utf8', maxBuffer: 1 << 28 }); if (r.status !== 0) throw new Error(cmd + ' failed: ' + r.stderr); return r.stdout; };
const blobOf = file => new Blob([fs.readFileSync(file)]);
const gen = (name, extra, vcodec = ['-c:v', 'libx264', '-preset', 'veryfast', '-bf', '2', '-g', '15', '-pix_fmt', 'yuv420p']) => {
  const out = path.join(tmp, name);
  sh('ffmpeg', ['-y', '-loglevel', 'error', '-f', 'lavfi', '-i', 'testsrc2=size=320x240:rate=30:duration=2', '-f', 'lavfi', '-i', 'sine=frequency=440:sample_rate=44100:duration=2',
    ...vcodec, '-c:a', 'aac', '-shortest', ...extra, out]);
  return out;
};
const packets = file => sh('ffprobe', ['-v', 'error', '-select_streams', 'v:0', '-show_entries', 'packet=pts_time,dts_time,size,pos,flags', '-of', 'json', file]);

test('timeline frame planning rounds cumulative boundaries without per-clip overshoot', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(Fast.planFrameCounts([1.01, 1.01], 30), [30, 31]);
  assert.equal(Fast.planFrameCounts([1.01, 1.01], 30).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0), Math.round(2.02 * 30));
  assert.deepEqual(Fast.planFrameCounts([1 / 3, 1 / 3, 1 / 3], 30), [10, 10, 10]);
  assert.deepEqual(Fast.planFrameCounts([0.001, 1.01], 30), [1, 29]);
  assert.deepEqual(Fast.planFrameCounts([], 30), []);
});

test('planning maths', () => {
  assert.equal(Fast.frameCount(1, 30), 30);
  assert.equal(Fast.frameCount(1.01, 30), 30);
  assert.equal(Fast.frameCount(0.001, 30), 1);
  assert.equal(Fast.avcLevelIdc(720, 1280, 30), 31);
  assert.equal(Fast.avcLevelIdc(1080, 1920, 30), 40);
  assert.equal(Fast.avcLevelIdc(2160, 3840, 30), 51);
  assert.deepEqual(Fast.fitRect(1920, 1080, 720, 1280), { x: 0, y: 436, w: 720, h: 406 });
  assert.deepEqual(Fast.fitRect(1080, 1920, 720, 1280), { x: 0, y: 0, w: 720, h: 1280 });
  const clip = { in: 2, speed: 2 };
  assert.ok(Math.abs(Fast.sourceLimit(clip, 0, 30) - (2 + (0.5 / 30) * 2)) < 1e-9);
  assert.ok(Fast.targetBitrate(720, 1280, 30) >= 1.5e6 && Fast.targetBitrate(3840, 2160, 60) <= 40e6);
});

test('eligibility reasons', () => {
  assert.equal(Fast.ineligibleReason({ clips: [{}], overlays: [{ type: 'text' }] }), null);
  assert.match(Fast.ineligibleReason({ clips: [{ reverse: true }] }), /reversed/);
  assert.match(Fast.ineligibleReason({ clips: [{}, { transition: true }] }), /clip 2 has a transition/);
  assert.match(Fast.ineligibleReason({ clips: [{}], overlays: [{ type: 'video' }] }), /video overlay/);
  assert.match(Fast.ineligibleReason({ clips: [{}], overlays: [{ type: 'image', blend: 'multiply' }] }), /blend/);
  assert.match(Fast.ineligibleReason({ clips: [] }), /no clips/);
});

test('HEVC codec string layout', () => {
  // hvcC for Main profile, level 4.0 (120), as written by common encoders
  const cfg = new Uint8Array(23);
  cfg[0] = 1; cfg[1] = 0x01; cfg[2] = 0x60; cfg[3] = 0; cfg[4] = 0; cfg[5] = 0; cfg[6] = 0x90; cfg[12] = 120;
  assert.equal(Fast.codecFromConfig('hvc1', cfg), 'hvc1.1.6.L120.90');
});

test('avcC / Annex B conversion on a synthetic sample', () => {
  const avcC = Uint8Array.from([1, 0x64, 0, 0x1f, 0xff, 0xe1, 0, 3, 0x67, 1, 2, 1, 0, 2, 0x68, 9]);
  const p = Fast.parseAvcC(avcC);
  assert.equal(p.nalLength, 4); assert.equal(p.sps.length, 1); assert.equal(p.pps.length, 1);
  const sample = Uint8Array.from([0, 0, 0, 3, 0x65, 0xaa, 0xbb, 0, 0, 0, 2, 0x41, 0xcc]);
  assert.deepEqual(Array.from(Fast.avccToAnnexB(sample, 4, null)), [0, 0, 0, 1, 0x65, 0xaa, 0xbb, 0, 0, 0, 1, 0x41, 0xcc]);
  assert.deepEqual(Array.from(Fast.avccToAnnexB(sample, 4, p)).slice(0, 12), [0, 0, 0, 1, 0x67, 1, 2, 0, 0, 0, 1, 0x68]);
});

test('Annex B sink enforces a bounded output buffer before copying oversized chunks', () => {
  let copied = false;
  const limited = new Fast.AnnexBSink(4);
  assert.throws(() => limited.onChunk({ byteLength: 5, copyTo() { copied = true; } }), Fast.Unsupported);
  assert.throws(() => limited.onChunk({ byteLength: 5, copyTo() { copied = true; } }), error => error.code === 'PIECE_BUDGET_EXCEEDED');
  assert.equal(copied, false, 'an over-budget chunk must be rejected before allocating its copy');
  assert.equal(limited.bytes, 0);

  const exact = new Fast.AnnexBSink(5);
  exact.onChunk({ byteLength: 5, copyTo(target) { target.fill(7); } });
  assert.deepEqual(Array.from(exact.finish()), [7, 7, 7, 7, 7]);
});

test('demux matches ffprobe: sample table, key frames, timing (B-frames, moov at end and at start)', { skip: !haveFfmpeg }, async () => {
  for (const [name, extra] of [['plain.mp4', []], ['faststart.mp4', ['-movflags', '+faststart']]]) {
    const file = gen(name, extra);
    const { video, hasAudio } = await Fast.demux(blobOf(file));
    const ref = JSON.parse(packets(file)).packets;
    assert.equal(hasAudio, true);
    assert.equal(video.count, ref.length, name);
    assert.equal(video.width, 320); assert.equal(video.height, 240);
    assert.match(video.codec, /^avc1\.[0-9a-f]{6}$/);
    assert.equal(video.rotation, 0);
    // packets from ffprobe are in file (decode) order, like our sample table
    for (let i = 0; i < ref.length; i++) {
      assert.equal(video.sizes[i], Number(ref[i].size), `size ${i}`);
      assert.equal(video.offsets[i], Number(ref[i].pos), `offset ${i}`);
      assert.equal(video.key[i], ref[i].flags.startsWith('K') ? 1 : 0, `key ${i}`);
      assert.ok(Math.abs(video.pts[i] - Number(ref[i].pts_time)) < 1e-4, `pts ${i}: ${video.pts[i]} vs ${ref[i].pts_time}`);
    }
  }
});

test('rotation metadata is read (90 and 270)', { skip: !haveFfmpeg }, async () => {
  for (const deg of [90, 270, 180]) {
    const base = gen('rot_src.mp4', []);
    const out = path.join(tmp, `rot${deg}.mp4`);
    sh('ffmpeg', ['-y', '-loglevel', 'error', '-display_rotation', String(deg), '-i', base, '-c', 'copy', out]);
    const { video } = await Fast.demux(blobOf(out));
    // ffmpeg's -display_rotation is counter-clockwise; the displayed picture is rotated that way,
    // so the clockwise rotation we must apply to the stored picture is the complement.
    assert.equal(video.rotation, (360 - deg) % 360 || 0, `deg ${deg}`);
  }
});

test('AnnexBSink counts every encoded output chunk as one frame', () => {
  const sink = new Fast.AnnexBSink(1024);
  const chunk = { byteLength: 3, copyTo(target) { target.set([1, 2, 3]); } };
  sink.onChunk(chunk, null);
  sink.onChunk(chunk, null);
  assert.equal(sink.frameCount, 2);
  assert.deepEqual(Array.from(sink.finish()), [1, 2, 3, 1, 2, 3]);
});

test('AVCC to Annex B: the converted stream decodes to the same frames as the original file', { skip: !haveFfmpeg }, async () => {
  // No B-frames here on purpose: that is what a WebCodecs encoder produces, and ffmpeg's raw .h264 reader
  // itself loses leading frames of B-frame streams (verified against ffmpeg's own h264_mp4toannexb).
  const file = gen('annexb.mp4', [], ['-c:v', 'libx264', '-preset', 'veryfast', '-bf', '0', '-g', '15', '-pix_fmt', 'yuv420p']);
  const { video } = await Fast.demux(blobOf(file));
  const params = Fast.parseAvcC(video.description);
  const bytes = fs.readFileSync(file);
  const parts = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < video.count; i++) {
    parts.push(Fast.avccToAnnexB(bytes.subarray(video.offsets[i], video.offsets[i] + video.sizes[i]), params.nalLength, video.key[i] ? params : null));
  }
  const es = path.join(tmp, 'annexb.h264');
  fs.writeFileSync(es, Buffer.concat(parts));
  const remuxed = path.join(tmp, 'annexb_out.mp4');
  sh('ffmpeg', ['-y', '-loglevel', 'error', '-f', 'h264', '-framerate', '30', '-i', es, '-c:v', 'copy', remuxed]);
  const md5 = f => sh('ffmpeg', ['-v', 'error', '-i', f, '-map', '0:v:0', '-fps_mode', 'passthrough', '-f', 'framemd5', '-']).split('\n').filter(l => l && !l.startsWith('#')).map(l => l.split(',').pop().trim());
  assert.deepEqual(md5(remuxed), md5(file));
});

test('unsupported inputs are rejected with Unsupported (so the caller can fall back)', { skip: !haveFfmpeg }, async () => {
  const webm = path.join(tmp, 'a.webm');
  sh('ffmpeg', ['-y', '-loglevel', 'error', '-f', 'lavfi', '-i', 'testsrc2=size=160x120:rate=10:duration=1', '-c:v', 'libvpx', webm]);
  await assert.rejects(() => Fast.demux(blobOf(webm)), Fast.Unsupported);
  const frag = path.join(tmp, 'frag.mp4');
  sh('ffmpeg', ['-y', '-loglevel', 'error', '-f', 'lavfi', '-i', 'testsrc2=size=160x120:rate=10:duration=1', '-c:v', 'libx264', '-movflags', 'frag_keyframe+empty_moov', frag]);
  await assert.rejects(() => Fast.demux(blobOf(frag)), Fast.Unsupported);
  const ten = path.join(tmp, 'ten.mp4');
  sh('ffmpeg', ['-y', '-loglevel', 'error', '-f', 'lavfi', '-i', 'testsrc2=size=160x120:rate=10:duration=1', '-c:v', 'libx264', '-pix_fmt', 'yuv420p10le', ten]);
  await assert.rejects(() => Fast.demux(blobOf(ten)), Fast.Unsupported);
});

test.after(() => { try { fs.rmSync(tmp, { recursive: true, force: true }); } catch (_) { /* ignore */ } });
test('encoder self-test times out and closes a stuck encoder', async () => {
  const saved = {
    VideoEncoder: globalThis.VideoEncoder,
    VideoFrame: globalThis.VideoFrame,
    OffscreenCanvas: globalThis.OffscreenCanvas,
  };
  class FakeCanvas {
    getContext() { return { fillRect() {}, set fillStyle(_) {} }; }
  }
  class FakeFrame { close() {} }
  try {
    for (const mode of ['queue', 'flush']) {
      let closed = false;
      globalThis.OffscreenCanvas = FakeCanvas;
      globalThis.VideoFrame = FakeFrame;
      globalThis.VideoEncoder = class {
        constructor() { this.encodeQueueSize = mode === 'queue' ? 5 : 0; }
        configure() {}
        encode() {}
        flush() { return mode === 'flush' ? new Promise(() => {}) : Promise.resolve(); }
        close() { closed = true; }
      };
      await assert.rejects(
        () => Fast.benchmarkEncoder({ config: { width: 16, height: 16, framerate: 30 } }, 1, 15),
        /Timed out/
      );
      assert.equal(closed, true, `${mode} timeout must close the encoder`);
    }
  } finally {
    for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(saved)) {
      if (value === undefined) delete globalThis[key];
      else globalThis[key] = value;
    }
  }
});

FILE: tests/fast_integration.py
