"""Integration test for the device-encoder export path as it is wired into app.js.

The real `tryDeviceEncoderExport` function is cut out of app.js and run in Chromium against stand-ins for the rest of the app
(project state, FFmpeg worker, overlay renderer). The FFmpeg commands it issues are then executed with a real ffmpeg, and the
resulting MP4 is inspected. It also checks that every reason to decline (and any mid-way failure) returns null and cleans up,
because that is what lets the export fall back to the FFmpeg-only path.

CODEC NOTE: The main integration path uses VP9 so it runs consistently across Chromium builds. The negative capability test
explicitly makes H.264 configurations unsupported; it must not depend on which codecs happen to ship with the CI browser.
H.264 bitstream specifics are covered by tests/fast.test.js; sound/assembly commands run with real FFmpeg.

Run: python tests/fast_integration.py   (needs ffmpeg, ffprobe, Playwright + Chromium)
"""
import base64, functools, http.server, json, re, shutil, socketserver, subprocess, sys, tempfile, threading
from pathlib import Path
from playwright.sync_api import sync_playwright

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
APP = (ROOT / "app.js").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
START = APP.index("const tryDeviceEncoderExport = async () => {")
END = APP.index("\n    };", START) + len("\n    };")
FN_SOURCE = APP[START:END].strip()
FN_SOURCE = FN_SOURCE[len("const tryDeviceEncoderExport = "):].rstrip(";")


def sh(*cmd, binary=False):
    r = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, check=True)
    return r.stdout if binary else r.stdout.decode()


def make_av(path, offset=0, audio=True):
    expr = "if(lt(Y,24),if(lt(X,288),if(bitand(floor((N+%d)/pow(2,floor(X/24))),1),235,16),16),16+X/8+Y/8)" % offset
    args = ["ffmpeg", "-y", "-loglevel", "error", "-f", "lavfi", "-i", "color=c=black:s=640x360:r=30:d=4"]
    if audio:
        args += ["-f", "lavfi", "-i", "sine=frequency=440:sample_rate=48000:duration=4"]
    args += ["-vf", f"format=gray,geq=lum='{expr}',format=yuv420p", "-c:v", "libvpx-vp9", "-b:v", "0", "-crf", "30", "-g", "15",
             "-deadline", "realtime", "-cpu-used", "8"]
    args += ["-c:a", "aac", "-shortest"] if audio else ["-an"]
    sh(*args, str(path))


HARNESS = r"""
async (a) => {
  const real = window.MediaYFast;
  const useVp9 = !a.realCodecs;
  let budgetInjected = false;
  const originalIsConfigSupported = window.VideoEncoder.isConfigSupported;
  if (a.noH264) {
    // Make this test deterministic: newer Chromium builds may include H.264 even when older CI images did not.
    window.VideoEncoder.isConfigSupported = async config => ({ supported: false, config });
  }
  const Fast = useVp9 ? Object.assign({}, real, {
    chooseEncoderConfig: o => real.chooseEncoderConfig(Object.assign({}, o, { codecs: ['vp09.00.10.08'], acceleration: ['no-preference'] })),
    AnnexBSink: function () { const sink = new real.IvfSink('VP90', 640, 360, 30); Object.defineProperty(sink, 'frameCount', { get: () => sink.frames.length }); return sink; },
    renderTimeline: async opts => {
      if (a.simulateBudgetOverrun && !budgetInjected) {
        budgetInjected = true;
        const error = new Error('simulated bounded-piece overrun'); error.code = 'PIECE_BUDGET_EXCEEDED'; throw error;
      }
      return real.renderTimeline(opts);
    },
    targetBitrate: (...args) => a.smallChunks ? 1.07e9 : real.targetBitrate(...args)
  }) : real;
  window.MediaYFast = Fast;
  const blobs = {};
  for (const [id, url] of Object.entries(a.media)) blobs[id] = await (await fetch(url)).blob();
  const png = async () => {
    const c = new OffscreenCanvas(640, 360), x = c.getContext('2d');
    x.fillStyle = '#fff'; x.fillRect(300, 200, 40, 40);
    return new Uint8Array(await (await c.convertToBlob({ type: 'image/png' })).arrayBuffer());
  };
  const log = { commands: [], written: {}, removed: [], notes: {}, stages: [], busy: [] };
  const ctx = {
    $: id => (id === 'useDeviceEncoder' ? { checked: a.toggle !== false } : null),
    S: a.S, FX: [{ f: '' }, { f: 'eq=saturation=1.5' }], TD: a.TD || a.S.main.map(() => 0), adjustmentFilter: c => (c.adjust ? 'eq=brightness=0.1' : ''),
    files: blobs, W: 640, H: 360, EXP: { fps: 30 }, dur: c => (c.out - c.in) / (c.speed || 1),
    exportMetric: { stage: n => log.stages.push(n), note: (k, v) => { log.notes[k] = v; } },
    exportCancelRequested: false,
    exportProgressHandler: 'SAVED-HANDLER',
    exportWorkspaceFiles: new Set(), exportSourceCache: new Map(), exportSourceCacheBytes: 0,
    ff: { writeFile: async (n, d) => {
      log.written[n] = (typeof d === 'string') ? d.length : d.length;
      if (n === 'alist.txt') log.alist = new TextDecoder().decode(d);
      if (n === 'vlist.txt') log.vlist = new TextDecoder().decode(d);
    } },
    run: async args => { if (a.failOn && args.join(' ').includes(a.failOn)) throw new Error('FFmpeg failed (code 1)'); log.commands.push(args); },
    ensureExportSource: async id => ({ name: a.names[id], size: 1, cached: false }),
    removeExportFile: async n => { log.removed.push(n); },
    overlayPNG: png, updateBusy: (t, d, p) => log.busy.push(p), say: () => {}
  };
  const factory = new Function('ctx', 'with (ctx) { return (' + a.fn + '); }');
  const fn = factory(ctx);
  let returned, error = null;
  try { returned = await fn(); } catch (e) { error = String(e && e.message || e); }
  window.MediaYFast = real;
  window.VideoEncoder.isConfigSupported = originalIsConfigSupported;
  return { returned, error, log, budgetInjected, handlerAfter: ctx.exportProgressHandler, workspace: [...ctx.exportWorkspaceFiles] };
}
"""


def ffprobe(path):
    j = json.loads(sh("ffprobe", "-v", "error", "-count_packets", "-show_entries", "format=duration:stream=codec_type,codec_name,nb_read_packets", "-of", "json", str(path)))
    return float(j["format"]["duration"]), {s["codec_type"]: (s["codec_name"], int(s.get("nb_read_packets", 0))) for s in j["streams"]}


def main():
    work = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="mediay-fastint-"))
    try:
        shutil.copy(ROOT / "mediay-fast.js", work / "mediay-fast.js")
        (work / "index.html").write_text("<!doctype html><title>t</title>")
        make_av(work / "av.mp4", 0, audio=True)
        make_av(work / "silent.mp4", 1000, audio=False)
        handler = functools.partial(http.server.SimpleHTTPRequestHandler, directory=str(work))
        handler.log_message = lambda *a, **k: None
        socketserver.TCPServer.allow_reuse_address = True
        server = socketserver.TCPServer(("127.0.0.1", 0), handler)
        threading.Thread(target=server.serve_forever, daemon=True).start()
        base = f"http://localhost:{server.server_address[1]}"
        failures = []

        def check(name, cond, detail=""):
            print(("PASS " if cond else "FAIL ") + name + ("" if cond else f"  [{detail}]"))
            if not cond:
                failures.append(name)

        def clip(mid, a, b, **kw):
            return dict({"mediaId": mid, "in": a, "out": b, "speed": 1, "fx": 0}, **kw)

        with sync_playwright() as p:
            browser = p.chromium.launch(args=["--no-sandbox", "--disable-dev-shm-usage"])
            page = browser.new_page()
            errors = []
            page.on("pageerror", lambda e: errors.append(str(e)))
            page.goto(base + "/index.html")
            page.add_script_tag(url=base + "/mediay-fast.js")

            def go(S, **kw):
                args = dict(fn=FN_SOURCE, S=S, media={"m1": f"{base}/av.mp4", "m2": f"{base}/silent.mp4"}, names={"m1": "av.mp4", "m2": "silent.mp4"})
                args.update(kw)
                return page.evaluate(HARNESS, args)

            overlay = {"id": "t1", "type": "text", "start": 0.2, "dur": 0.5, "layer": 1}

            # 1. a normal project: two clips (one sped up, one faded), one caption
            S = {"main": [clip("m1", 0, 1), clip("m1", 1, 3, speed=2, fade=True, vol=0.5)], "overlays": [overlay]}
            r = go(S)
            log = r["log"]
            check("success: returns the joined file", r["returned"] == "joined.mp4" and r["error"] is None, json.dumps(r)[:300])
            check("success: report says the device encoder was used", log["notes"].get("deviceEncoder", "").startswith("used"), str(log["notes"]))
            check("success: bounded picture pieces, per-clip sound and assembly all issued",
                  len([c for c in log["commands"] if "-f" in c and "h264" in c]) == 2
                  and log["written"].get("fastvideo_0.h264", 0) > 1000
                  and log["written"].get("fastvideo_1.h264", 0) > 1000
                  and "vlist.txt" in log["written"] and "alist.txt" in log["written"],
                  str(log["commands"])[:500])
            check("success: temporary files removed, workspace list keeps only joined.mp4",
                  {"fastvideo_0.h264", "fastvideo_1.h264", "fastsegment_0.mp4", "fastsegment_1.mp4",
                   "vlist.txt", "alist.txt", "aseg0.wav", "aseg1.wav"} <= set(log["removed"])
                  and "joined.mp4" in r["workspace"], f"{log['removed']} {r['workspace']}")
            check("success: progress only moves forward", all(a <= b for a, b in zip(log["busy"], log["busy"][1:])), str(log["busy"]))

            # run the recorded sound commands for real and check lengths
            audio_cmds = [c for c in log["commands"] if c[-1].endswith(".wav")]
            for cmd in audio_cmds:
                sh("ffmpeg", "-y", "-loglevel", "error", *[("av.mp4" if x == "av.mp4" else x) for x in cmd]) if False else subprocess.run(["ffmpeg", "-y", "-loglevel", "error", *cmd], cwd=work, check=True)
            lens = [float(sh("ffprobe", "-v", "error", "-show_entries", "format=duration", "-of", "csv=p=0", str(work / c[-1])).strip()) for c in audio_cmds]
            check("sound pieces have exactly the video length of their clip (1.0 s and 1.0 s)", len(lens) == 2 and all(abs(l - 1.0) < 0.003 for l in lens), str(lens))

            # Stand in for the two bounded WebCodecs chunks with real H.264 pieces.
            video_muxes = [c for c in log["commands"] if "-f" in c and "h264" in c]
            for cmd in video_muxes:
                raw = cmd[cmd.index("-i") + 1]
                sh("ffmpeg", "-y", "-loglevel", "error", "-f", "lavfi", "-i", "testsrc2=size=640x360:rate=30",
                   "-frames:v", "30", "-c:v", "libx264", "-bf", "0", "-pix_fmt", "yuv420p", "-f", "h264", str(work / raw))
                subprocess.run(["ffmpeg", "-y", "-loglevel", "error", *cmd], cwd=work, check=True)
            (work / "vlist.txt").write_text(log["vlist"])
            (work / "alist.txt").write_text(log["alist"])
            mux = log["commands"][-1]
            subprocess.run(["ffmpeg", "-y", "-loglevel", "error", *mux], cwd=work, check=True)
            dur, streams = ffprobe(work / "joined.mp4")
            check("assembled MP4 has H.264 picture (60 frames) and AAC sound, 2.0 s long", streams.get("video", ("", 0))[0] == "h264" and streams["video"][1] == 60 and streams.get("audio", ("",))[0] == "aac" and abs(dur - 2.0) < 0.05, f"{dur} {streams}")

            # Force a tiny per-piece frame budget to prove a single one-second clip
            # is rendered as multiple bounded segments instead of one large output buffer.
            r = go({"main": [clip("m1", 0, 1)], "overlays": []}, smallChunks=True)
            chunk_muxes = [c for c in r["log"]["commands"] if "-f" in c and "h264" in c]
            check("long-output path: one clip is split into six independently muxed bounded pieces",
                  r["returned"] == "joined.mp4" and len(chunk_muxes) == 6
                  and all(f"fastvideo_{i}.h264" in r["log"]["written"] for i in range(6))
                  and all(f"fastsegment_{i}.mp4" in r["log"]["removed"] for i in range(6)),
                  f"returned={r['returned']} chunks={len(chunk_muxes)} workspace={r['workspace']}")

            # An underestimated bitrate must shrink and retry the piece instead of abandoning WebCodecs.
            r = go({"main": [clip("m1", 0, 4)], "overlays": []}, simulateBudgetOverrun=True)
            check("budget overrun: retries at half-size and keeps the device encoder path",
                  r["budgetInjected"] and r["returned"] == "joined.mp4" and
                  r["log"]["notes"].get("deviceEncoder", "").startswith("used") and
                  r["log"]["notes"].get("deviceEncoderBudgetRetry") == "retrying with 60 frames per piece",
                  f"returned={r['returned']} notes={r['log']['notes']} error={r['error']}")

            # 2. project without any sound track -> picture only
            r = go({"main": [clip("m2", 0, 1)], "overlays": []})
            mux = r["log"]["commands"][-1] if r["log"]["commands"] else []
            check("silent project: one video piece and picture-only concat", r["returned"] == "joined.mp4"
                  and len(r["log"]["commands"]) == 2 and "-f" in mux and "concat" in mux
                  and "alist.txt" not in " ".join(mux), str(r["log"]["commands"]))

            # 2b. mixed voiced + silent clips: the fast path must remain eligible and
            # synthesize a silent WAV for the silent clip so concat has matching tracks.
            r = go({"main": [clip("m1", 0, 1), clip("m2", 0, 1)], "overlays": []})
            mixed_log = r["log"]
            mixed_audio_cmds = [c for c in mixed_log["commands"] if c[-1].endswith(".wav")]
            check("mixed-sound project: fast path stays enabled and creates one audio piece per clip",
                  r["returned"] == "joined.mp4" and r["error"] is None and len(mixed_audio_cmds) == 2
                  and any(any("anullsrc" in arg for arg in c) for c in mixed_audio_cmds)
                  and "vlist.txt" in mixed_log["written"] and "alist.txt" in mixed_log["written"],
                  f"returned={r['returned']} error={r['error']} commands={mixed_log['commands']}")
            for cmd in mixed_audio_cmds:
                subprocess.run(["ffmpeg", "-y", "-loglevel", "error", *cmd], cwd=work, check=True)
            mixed_lens = [float(sh("ffprobe", "-v", "error", "-show_entries", "format=duration", "-of", "csv=p=0",
                                   str(work / c[-1])).strip()) for c in mixed_audio_cmds]
            check("mixed-sound project: voiced and synthetic-silence WAVs have matching 1.0 s durations",
                  len(mixed_lens) == 2 and all(abs(x - 1.0) < 0.003 for x in mixed_lens), str(mixed_lens))
            mixed_video_muxes = [c for c in mixed_log["commands"] if "-f" in c and "h264" in c]
            for cmd in mixed_video_muxes:
                raw = cmd[cmd.index("-i") + 1]
                sh("ffmpeg", "-y", "-loglevel", "error", "-f", "lavfi", "-i", "testsrc2=size=640x360:rate=30",
                   "-frames:v", "30", "-c:v", "libx264", "-bf", "0", "-pix_fmt", "yuv420p", "-f", "h264", str(work / raw))
                subprocess.run(["ffmpeg", "-y", "-loglevel", "error", *cmd], cwd=work, check=True)
            (work / "vlist.txt").write_text(mixed_log["vlist"])
            (work / "alist.txt").write_text(mixed_log["alist"])
            subprocess.run(["ffmpeg", "-y", "-loglevel", "error", *mixed_log["commands"][-1]], cwd=work, check=True)
            mixed_dur, mixed_streams = ffprobe(work / "joined.mp4")
            check("mixed-sound project: final MP4 keeps AAC and full 2.0 s duration",
                  mixed_streams.get("video", ("", 0))[0] == "h264" and mixed_streams.get("audio", ("",))[0] == "aac"
                  and abs(mixed_dur - 2.0) < 0.05, f"{mixed_dur} {mixed_streams}")

            # 3. reasons to decline: all must return null, write nothing, keep the original progress handler
            def declines(name, S, expect, **kw):
                r = go(S, **kw)
                note = r["log"]["notes"].get("deviceEncoder", "")
                ok = r["returned"] is None and r["error"] is None and expect in note and not r["log"]["written"] and not r["log"]["commands"]
                check(f"declines: {name}", ok, f"returned={r['returned']} err={r['error']} note={note!r} written={list(r['log']['written'])}")

            declines("reversed clip", {"main": [clip("m1", 0, 1, rev=True)], "overlays": []}, "reversed")
            declines("filter on a clip", {"main": [clip("m1", 0, 1, fx=1)], "overlays": []}, "filter")
            declines("colour adjustments", {"main": [clip("m1", 0, 1, adjust=True)], "overlays": []}, "color adjustments")
            declines("transition", {"main": [clip("m1", 0, 1), clip("m1", 1, 2, tr="fade")], "overlays": []}, "transition", TD=[0, 0.5])
            declines("video overlay", {"main": [clip("m1", 0, 1)], "overlays": [{"id": "v", "type": "video", "start": 0, "dur": 1, "layer": 1}]}, "video overlay")
            declines("blend mode", {"main": [clip("m1", 0, 1)], "overlays": [{"id": "b", "type": "image", "start": 0, "dur": 1, "layer": 1, "blend": "multiply"}]}, "blend")
            declines("odd speed", {"main": [clip("m1", 0, 2, speed=4)], "overlays": []}, "speed")
            declines("switched off by the user", {"main": [clip("m1", 0, 1)], "overlays": []}, "turned off", toggle=False)
            declines("device has no H.264 encoder (capability explicitly unavailable)", {"main": [clip("m1", 0, 1)], "overlays": []}, "no usable video encoder", realCodecs=True, noH264=True)

            # 4. failure half way: must clean up everything it created and hand back the original handler
            r = go({"main": [clip("m1", 0, 1), clip("m1", 1, 2)], "overlays": [overlay]}, failOn="aseg1.wav")
            note = r["log"]["notes"].get("deviceEncoder", "")
            check("failure while preparing sound: returns null, says why, removes its files, restores the progress handler",
                  r["returned"] is None and "failed" in note
                  and {"fastvideo_0.h264", "fastvideo_1.h264", "fastsegment_0.mp4", "fastsegment_1.mp4",
                       "aseg0.wav", "aseg1.wav"} <= set(r["log"]["removed"])
                  and r["handlerAfter"] == "SAVED-HANDLER",
                  f"{r['returned']} {note} {r['log']['removed']} {r['handlerAfter']}")
            r = go({"main": [clip("m1", 0, 1)], "overlays": []}, failOn="joined.mp4")
            check("failure while assembling: all partial video/audio pieces and joined.mp4 are removed",
                  r["returned"] is None
                  and {"fastvideo_0.h264", "fastsegment_0.mp4", "aseg0.wav", "vlist.txt", "alist.txt", "joined.mp4"} <= set(r["log"]["removed"]),
                  f"{r['returned']} {r['log']['removed']}")

            check("no page errors", not errors, str(errors))
            browser.close()
        server.shutdown()
        if failures:
            print(f"\n{len(failures)} FAILED: {failures}")
            sys.exit(1)
        print("\nAll device-encoder integration checks passed.")
    finally:
        shutil.rmtree(work, ignore_errors=True)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()



================================================================================
FILE: tests/fast_pipeline.py
BLOB SHA: b7fdc7edeeadf42416b6d90977b27e7d628c194f

"""Pipeline test for the fast export engine (mediay-fast.js) in a real browser.

Real MP4 files -> real WebCodecs decode -> trim/speed/fade/overlay/rotation compositing -> real WebCodecs encode ->
the result is decoded by ffmpeg and checked frame by frame.

Source frames carry a 12-bit "barcode" of their frame number in the top-left corner, so the test can tell exactly
which source frame ended up in which output frame.

CODEC NOTE: The main frame-accuracy matrix uses VP9 for deterministic coverage. A separate test also attempts a real H.264
WebCodecs encode and validates the Annex B output with FFmpeg when the installed Chromium build exposes an H.264 encoder.
That desktop-browser check does not prove that Android WebView exposes a hardware encoder; physical-device validation remains
required. See docs/fast-export.md.

Needs: ffmpeg + ffprobe on PATH, Playwright with Chromium.
Run: python tests/fast_pipeline.py
"""
import base64, functools, http.server, json, shutil, socketserver, subprocess, sys, tempfile, threading
from pathlib import Path
from playwright.sync_api import sync_playwright

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
FPS = 30


def sh(*cmd, binary=False):
    r = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, check=True)
    return r.stdout if binary else r.stdout.decode()


def make_source(path, offset=0, rot=None, seconds=4):
    """640x360 VP9-in-MP4. Top-left: barcode of (frame number + offset). Background: dark gradient (asymmetric, for rotation checks)."""
    expr = ("if(lt(Y,24),if(lt(X,288),if(bitand(floor((N+%d)/pow(2,floor(X/24))),1),235,16),16),16+X/8+Y/8)" % offset)
    tmp = str(path) + ".tmp.mp4" if rot else str(path)
    sh("ffmpeg", "-y", "-loglevel", "error", "-f", "lavfi", "-i", f"color=c=black:s=640x360:r={FPS}:d={seconds}",
       "-vf", f"format=gray,geq=lum='{expr}',format=yuv420p", "-c:v", "libvpx-vp9", "-b:v", "0", "-crf", "22", "-g", "15",
       "-deadline", "realtime", "-cpu-used", "5", "-an", tmp)
    if rot:
        sh("ffmpeg", "-y", "-loglevel", "error", "-display_rotation", str(rot), "-i", tmp, "-c", "copy", str(path))


def decode_gray(path, w, h, input_format=None):
    args = ["ffmpeg", "-v", "error"]
    if input_format:
        args += ["-f", input_format]
    args += ["-i", str(path), "-vf", "format=gray", "-f", "rawvideo", "-"]
    raw = sh(*args, binary=True)
    n = len(raw) // (w * h)
    return [raw[i * w * h:(i + 1) * w * h] for i in range(n)]


def barcode(frame, w):
    bits = ["1" if frame[12 * w + 12 + 24 * i] > 128 else "0" for i in range(12)]
    return int("".join(reversed(bits)), 2)


def mean(frame, w, x0, y0, x1, y1):
    vals = [frame[y * w + x] for y in range(y0, y1) for x in range(x0, x1)]
    return sum(vals) / len(vals)


HARNESS = """
async (spec) => {
  const Fast = window.MediaYFast;
  const clips = [];
  for (const c of spec.clips) {
    const blob = await (await fetch(c.url)).blob();
    const { video } = await Fast.demux(blob);
    clips.push({ file: blob, track: video, in: c.in, out: c.out, speed: c.speed || 1, fade: !!c.fade });
  }
  const h264 = spec.codec === 'h264';
  const sink = h264 ? new Fast.AnnexBSink() : new Fast.IvfSink('VP90', spec.W, spec.H, spec.fps);
  let choice;
  const overlays = (spec.overlays || []).map(o => {
    const c = new OffscreenCanvas(o.w, o.h), x = c.getContext('2d');
    x.fillStyle = '#fff'; x.fillRect(0, 0, o.w, o.h);
    return { bitmap: c.transferToImageBitmap(), x: o.x, y: o.y, w: o.w, h: o.h, start: o.start, end: o.end };
  });
  let polled = 0;
  const progress = [];
  let result, error = null;
  try {
    choice = await Fast.chooseEncoderConfig({
      W: spec.W, H: spec.H, fps: spec.fps, bitrate: 6e6,
      ...(h264 ? {} : { codecs: ['vp09.00.10.08'], acceleration: ['no-preference'] })
    });
    result = await Fast.renderTimeline({ clips, W: spec.W, H: spec.H, fps: spec.fps, choice, sink, overlays,
      isCancelled: () => spec.cancelAfter != null && ++polled > spec.cancelAfter, onProgress: p => progress.push(p) });
  } catch (e) { error = String(e && e.message || e); }
  const bytes = sink.finish();
  let bin = '';
  for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i += 32768) bin += String.fromCharCode.apply(null, bytes.subarray(i, i + 32768));
  return { error, result, progress, format: h264 ? 'h264' : 'ivf', b64: btoa(bin) };
}
"""

BENCH = """
async () => {
  const Fast = window.MediaYFast;
  const choice = await Fast.chooseEncoderConfig({ W: 640, H: 360, fps: 30, codecs: ['vp09.00.10.08'], acceleration: ['no-preference'] });
  return await Fast.benchmarkEncoder(choice, 12);
}
"""


def main():
    work = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="mediay-fast-"))
    try:
        shutil.copy(ROOT / "mediay-fast.js", work / "mediay-fast.js")
        (work / "index.html").write_text("<!doctype html><title>t</title>")
        make_source(work / "a.mp4", 0)
        make_source(work / "b.mp4", 1000)
        make_source(work / "rot.mp4", 0, rot=90)

        handler = functools.partial(http.server.SimpleHTTPRequestHandler, directory=str(work))
        handler.log_message = lambda *a, **k: None
        socketserver.TCPServer.allow_reuse_address = True
        server = socketserver.TCPServer(("127.0.0.1", 0), handler)
        threading.Thread(target=server.serve_forever, daemon=True).start()
        base = f"http://localhost:{server.server_address[1]}"

        failures = []

        def check(name, cond, detail=""):
            print(("PASS " if cond else "FAIL ") + name + ("" if cond else f"  [{detail}]"))
            if not cond:
                failures.append(name)

        with sync_playwright() as p:
            browser = p.chromium.launch(args=["--no-sandbox", "--disable-dev-shm-usage"])
            page = browser.new_page()
            errors = []
            page.on("pageerror", lambda e: errors.append(str(e)))
            page.goto(base + "/index.html")
            page.add_script_tag(url=base + "/mediay-fast.js")

            def render(case_id, spec):
                out = page.evaluate(HARNESS, spec)
                output = work / f"{case_id}.h264" if spec.get("codec") == "h264" else work / f"{case_id}.ivf"
                output.write_bytes(base64.b64decode(out["b64"]))
                return out, output

            def ids(output, w, h):
                frames = decode_gray(output, w, h, "h264" if output.suffix == ".h264" else None)
                return [barcode(f, w) for f in frames], frames

            def clip(url, a, b, **kw):
                return dict(url=f"{base}/{url}", **{"in": a, "out": b}, **kw)

            # A. plain pass-through
            out, ivf = render("A", dict(W=640, H=360, fps=30, clips=[clip("a.mp4", 0, 2)]))
            got, _ = ids(ivf, 640, 360)
            check("A: 2 s clip -> 60 frames in order", out["error"] is None and got == list(range(60)), f"{out['error']} {got[:6]}...{got[-3:]} n={len(got)}")
            check("A: progress reaches 1", out["progress"] and out["progress"][-1] == 1, str(out["progress"][-2:]))

            # B. trim
            out, ivf = render("B", dict(W=640, H=360, fps=30, clips=[clip("a.mp4", 1.0, 2.0)]))
            got, _ = ids(ivf, 640, 360)
            check("B: trim 1.0-2.0 s -> source frames 30..59", got == list(range(30, 60)), f"{got}")

            # C. 2x speed
            out, ivf = render("C", dict(W=640, H=360, fps=30, clips=[clip("a.mp4", 0, 2, speed=2)]))
            got, _ = ids(ivf, 640, 360)
            check("C: 2x speed -> every 2nd frame, 30 frames", got == list(range(0, 60, 2)), f"{got}")

            # D. half speed
            out, ivf = render("D", dict(W=640, H=360, fps=30, clips=[clip("a.mp4", 0, 1, speed=0.5)]))
            got, _ = ids(ivf, 640, 360)
            check("D: 0.5x speed -> each frame twice, 60 frames", got == [k // 2 for k in range(60)], f"{got}")

            # E. two clips from two files, one continuous stream
            out, ivf = render("E", dict(W=640, H=360, fps=30, clips=[clip("a.mp4", 0, 1), clip("b.mp4", 1, 2)]))
            got, _ = ids(ivf, 640, 360)
            check("E: two clips join without gap or repeat", got == list(range(0, 30)) + list(range(1030, 1060)), f"{got}")
            frames_total = out["result"]["frames"] if out["result"] else None
            check("E: engine reports 60 frames", frames_total == 60, str(out["result"]))

            # F. lower output frame rate
            out, ivf = render("F", dict(W=640, H=360, fps=15, clips=[clip("a.mp4", 0, 2)]))
            got, _ = ids(ivf, 640, 360)
            check("F: 15 fps output from 30 fps source", got == list(range(0, 60, 2)), f"{got}")

            # G. overlay window (timeline seconds 0.5 .. 1.0 inclusive -> frames 15..30)
            out, ivf = render("G", dict(W=640, H=360, fps=30, clips=[clip("a.mp4", 0, 2)],
                                        overlays=[dict(x=300, y=200, w=40, h=40, start=0.5, end=1.0)]))
            _, frames = ids(ivf, 640, 360)
            lit = [i for i, f in enumerate(frames) if mean(f, 640, 310, 210, 330, 230) > 200]
            check("G: overlay visible exactly on frames 15..30", lit == list(range(15, 31)), f"{lit}")

            # G2. overlay on the second clip uses TIMELINE time, not clip time
            out, ivf = render("G2", dict(W=640, H=360, fps=30, clips=[clip("a.mp4", 0, 1), clip("b.mp4", 1, 2)],
                                         overlays=[dict(x=300, y=200, w=40, h=40, start=1.5, end=1.8)]))
            _, frames = ids(ivf, 640, 360)
            lit = [i for i, f in enumerate(frames) if mean(f, 640, 310, 210, 330, 230) > 200]
            check("G2: overlay timing continues across clips", lit == list(range(45, 55)), f"{lit}")

            # H. letterbox: 640x360 into a 360x640 portrait frame
            out, ivf = render("H", dict(W=360, H=640, fps=30, clips=[clip("a.mp4", 0, 1)]))
            _, frames = ivf and ids(ivf, 360, 640)
            f = frames[10]
            top = mean(f, 360, 0, 10, 360, 100); mid = mean(f, 360, 0, 300, 360, 340)
            check("H: letterbox bars are black, picture in the middle", top < 25 and mid > 25 and len(frames) == 30, f"top={top:.1f} mid={mid:.1f} n={len(frames)}")

            # I. rotation: compare with ffmpeg's own auto-rotated decode of the same file
            out, ivf = render("I", dict(W=360, H=640, fps=30, clips=[clip("rot.mp4", 0, 1)]))
            _, ours = ids(ivf, 360, 640)
            ref = decode_gray(work / "rot.mp4", 360, 640)
            def diff(a, b):
                return sum(abs(x - y) for x, y in zip(a[::7], b[::7])) / len(a[::7])
            wrong = bytes(reversed(ref[10]))  # 180 degrees off
            d_ok, d_bad = diff(ours[10][100 * 360:], ref[10][100 * 360:]), diff(ours[10][100 * 360:], wrong[100 * 360:])
            check("I: 90-degree metadata is honoured (matches ffmpeg's autorotate)", d_ok < 12 and d_ok * 2 < d_bad and len(ref) >= 30, f"d_ok={d_ok:.1f} d_bad={d_bad:.1f}")

            # J. fade in/out
            out, ivf = render("J", dict(W=640, H=360, fps=30, clips=[clip("a.mp4", 0, 2, fade=True)]))
            _, frames = ids(ivf, 640, 360)
            first, middle, last = mean(frames[0], 640, 0, 100, 640, 360), mean(frames[30], 640, 0, 100, 640, 360), mean(frames[-1], 640, 0, 100, 640, 360)
            check("J: fade starts and ends near black", first < middle * 0.4 and last < middle * 0.4, f"{first:.1f} {middle:.1f} {last:.1f}")

            # K. cancel stops cleanly and fast
            out, ivf = render("K", dict(W=640, H=360, fps=30, clips=[clip("a.mp4", 0, 3)], cancelAfter=10))
            check("K: cancel is reported and does not hang", out["error"] and "cancel" in out["error"].lower(), str(out["error"]))

            # L. self-test benchmark
            bench = page.evaluate(BENCH)
            check("L: encoder self-test reports a speed", bench["fps"] > 1, str(bench))
            print(f"   (info) software VP9 encoder self-test here: {bench['fps']:.0f} fps at 640x360")

            # M. Exercise the actual H.264 WebCodecs encoder and Annex B sink where Chromium supports it.
            h264_out, h264_file = render("M", dict(W=640, H=360, fps=30, codec="h264", clips=[clip("a.mp4", 0, 2)]))
            if h264_out["error"] and "no usable video encoder" in h264_out["error"]:
                print("SKIP M: this Chromium build does not expose an H.264 WebCodecs encoder")
            elif h264_out["error"] or not h264_out["result"]:
                check("M: H.264 WebCodecs output is valid Annex B and preserves all 60 frames", False, h264_out["error"])
            else:
                h264_ids, _ = ids(h264_file, 640, 360)
                check("M: H.264 WebCodecs output is valid Annex B and preserves all 60 frames",
                      h264_out["result"]["frames"] == 60 and h264_ids == list(range(60)),
                      f"frames={len(h264_ids)} ids={h264_ids[:8]}...{h264_ids[-4:]}")

            # N. fractional clip lengths share one cumulative frame grid instead of rounding each clip up.
            out, ivf = render("N", dict(W=640, H=360, fps=30,
                                        clips=[clip("a.mp4", 0, 1.01), clip("b.mp4", 0, 1.01)]))
            got, _ = ids(ivf, 640, 360)
            expected = list(range(0, 30)) + list(range(1000, 1031))
            check("N: fractional clips use cumulative frame boundaries (61 frames)",
                  out["error"] is None and out["result"] and out["result"]["frames"] == 61 and got == expected,
                  f"frames={len(got)} result={out['result']} ids={got[:5]}...{got[-5:]}")

            check("no page errors", not errors, str(errors))
            browser.close()
        server.shutdown()
        if failures:
            print(f"\n{len(failures)} FAILED: {failures}")
            sys.exit(1)
        print("\nAll fast-export pipeline checks passed.")
    finally:
        shutil.rmtree(work, ignore_errors=True)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

'use strict';
const test = require('node:test');
const assert = require('node:assert/strict');
const fs = require('node:fs');
const path = require('node:path');

const app = fs.readFileSync(path.join(__dirname, '..', 'app.js'), 'utf8');

test('history autosave is debounced and reports storage failures without losing editor state', () => {
  const start = app.indexOf('function scheduleProjectSave()');
  const end = app.indexOf('function commit()', start);
  assert.ok(start >= 0 && end > start, 'shared autosave helper must exist');
  const helper = app.slice(start, end);
  assert.match(helper, /clearTimeout\(saveTimer\)/);
  assert.match(helper, /setTimeout\(/);
  assert.match(helper, /\), 800\)/);
  assert.match(helper, /saveProject\(\)\.catch/);
  assert.match(helper, /Auto-save failed/);
});

test('normal edit commits schedule persistence and retain bounded undo history', () => {
  const start = app.indexOf('function commit()');
  const end = app.indexOf('function normalizeState', start);
  assert.ok(start >= 0 && end > start);
  const commit = app.slice(start, end);
  assert.match(commit, /undoStack\.push\(snap\(\)\)/);
  assert.match(commit, /undoStack\.length > 50/);
  assert.match(commit, /redoStack = \[\]/);
  assert.match(commit, /scheduleProjectSave\(\)/);
});

test('undo and redo schedule persistence only after the restored state is applied', () => {
  const undoStart = app.indexOf("$('undoBtn').onclick");
  const redoStart = app.indexOf("$('redoBtn').onclick", undoStart);
  const saveStart = app.indexOf('async function saveProject()', redoStart);
  assert.ok(undoStart >= 0 && redoStart > undoStart && saveStart > redoStart);
  const undo = app.slice(undoStart, redoStart);
  const redo = app.slice(redoStart, saveStart);
  assert.match(undo, /await restore\(undoStack\[undoStack\.length - 1\]\)/);
  assert.match(undo, /await restore[\s\S]*scheduleProjectSave\(\)/);
  assert.match(redo, /await restore\(s\)/);
  assert.match(redo, /await restore[\s\S]*scheduleProjectSave\(\)/);
  assert.match(undo, /if \(undoStack\.length < 2\) return/);
  assert.match(redo, /if \(!redoStack\.length\) return/);
});



================================================================================
FILE: tests/offline_speech_runtime.py
BLOB SHA: f3162249b562e2e8a886c6a5fbc20e0bb0609e9b
================================================================================
"""Proves the bundled speech engine (JavaScript + WASM runtime) runs with ALL external network blocked.

It loads vendor/transformers/transformers.min.js in headless Chromium, points the library at the bundled WASM
runtime exactly as app.js does (SPEECH_ENGINE.bundledRuntime), and runs a real ONNX inference on a tiny local model.
The Whisper model weights are NOT bundled, so this does not claim offline captions: only that the code and runtime
need no internet. Run: python tests/offline_speech_runtime.py
"""
import base64, functools, http.server, json, re, socketserver, subprocess, threading
from pathlib import Path
from playwright.sync_api import sync_playwright

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
FETCH = ROOT / "scripts" / "fetch_speech_engine.sh"
MODEL_B64 = ROOT / "tests" / "fixtures" / "offline" / "tiny_model.onnx.b64"   # a 343-byte ONNX graph, stored as text
PAGE = """<!doctype html><meta charset=utf-8><script type="module">
window.run = async () => {
  const { env, AutoModel, Tensor } = await import('/vendor/transformers/transformers.min.js');
  env.allowLocalModels = true; env.allowRemoteModels = false; env.useBrowserCache = false;
  env.localModelPath = '/tests/fixtures/offline/models/';
  env.backends.onnx.wasm.wasmPaths = new URL('/vendor/transformers/', location.href).href;   // same as app.js
  env.backends.onnx.wasm.numThreads = 1;
  const model = await AutoModel.from_pretrained('tiny', { quantized: false });
  const ids = a => new Tensor('int64', BigInt64Array.from(a.map(BigInt)), [1, a.length]);
  const out = await model({ input_ids: ids([1, 2, 3]), attention_mask: ids([1, 1, 1]), token_type_ids: ids([0, 0, 0]) });
  return Array.from(out.last_hidden_state.data);
};
window.ready = true;
</script>"""

class Quiet(http.server.SimpleHTTPRequestHandler):
    def log_message(self, *args): pass

def main():
    # The third-party speech engine files are not committed; they are downloaded and checksum-verified on demand.
    subprocess.run(["bash", str(FETCH)], check=True, cwd=ROOT)
    app_js = (ROOT / "app.js").read_text()
    assert re.search(r"wasmPaths\s*=\s*absoluteUrl\(SPEECH_ENGINE\.bundledRuntime\)", app_js) and "bundledRuntimeAvailable" in app_js, "app.js must point the library at the bundled WASM runtime"
    socketserver.TCPServer.allow_reuse_address = True
    server = socketserver.ThreadingTCPServer(("127.0.0.1", 0), functools.partial(Quiet, directory=str(ROOT)))
    threading.Thread(target=server.serve_forever, daemon=True).start()
    base = f"http://127.0.0.1:{server.server_address[1]}"
    external = []
    with sync_playwright() as p:
        browser = p.chromium.launch(headless=True, args=["--no-sandbox", "--disable-dev-shm-usage"])
        context = browser.new_context(service_workers="block")
        def block(route):
            external.append(route.request.url); route.abort()
        context.route(lambda url: not url.startswith(base), block)
        context.route(base + "/__offline_test__.html", lambda route: route.fulfill(body=PAGE, content_type="text/html"))
        model_bytes = base64.b64decode(MODEL_B64.read_text())
        context.route(base + "/tests/fixtures/offline/models/tiny/onnx/model.onnx", lambda route: route.fulfill(body=model_bytes, content_type="application/octet-stream"))
        page = context.new_page()
        page.goto(base + "/__offline_test__.html")
        page.wait_for_function("window.ready === true")
        values = page.evaluate("window.run()")
        browser.close()
    server.shutdown()
    assert values == [2, 3, 4], values
    assert not external, f"the speech engine tried to use the internet: {external}"
    print("PASS: bundled speech engine runs a real ONNX inference with all external network blocked", json.dumps(values))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

'use strict';
const test = require('node:test');
const assert = require('node:assert/strict');
const fs = require('node:fs');
const path = require('node:path');

const setup = fs.readFileSync(path.join(__dirname, '..', 'setup.js'), 'utf8');

test('project media GC saves current state and retains every media reference across all projects', () => {
  const start = setup.indexOf('async function collectUnreferencedMedia()');
  const end = setup.indexOf('async function deleteSavedProject', start);
  assert.ok(start >= 0 && end > start);
  const gc = setup.slice(start, end);
  assert.match(gc, /await saveProject\(\)/);
  assert.match(gc, /localStorage\.getItem\('pmy-templates'\)/);
  assert.match(gc, /template\.pre, template\.post, template\.overlays, template\.audio/);
  assert.match(gc, /template\.slotClip\.mediaId/);
  assert.match(gc, /saved templates could not be read/);
  assert.match(gc, /saved template has an unsupported shape/);
  assert.match(gc, /await db\.projects\.toArray\(\)/);
  assert.match(gc, /\[state\.main, state\.audio, state\.overlays\]/);
  assert.match(gc, /referenced\.add\(item\.mediaId\)/);
  assert.match(gc, /return 0; \/\/ Unknown references must never cause user media to be deleted/);
  assert.match(gc, /!Array\.isArray\(state\.main\)/);
  assert.match(gc, /!Array\.isArray\(state\.audio\)/);
  assert.match(gc, /!Array\.isArray\(state\.overlays\)/);
  assert.match(gc, /db\.media\.bulkDelete\(orphanIds\)/);
  assert.match(gc, /URL\.revokeObjectURL\(urls\[id\]\)/);
  assert.match(gc, /delete files\[id\]/);
  assert.match(gc, /delete decoded\[id\]/);
  assert.match(gc, /delete thumbs\[id\]/);
  assert.match(gc, /delete audioLoads\[id\]/);
  assert.doesNotMatch(gc, /dropMediaCaches\(\)/);
});

test('deleting a named project invokes best-effort cleanup without deleting shared media', () => {
  const start = setup.indexOf('async function deleteSavedProject');
  const end = setup.indexOf('async function renderProjectLibrary', start);
  assert.ok(start >= 0 && end > start);
  const deletion = setup.slice(start, end);
  assert.match(deletion, /await db\.projects\.delete\(id\)/);
  assert.match(deletion, /await collectUnreferencedMedia\(\)/);
  assert.match(deletion, /Media used by other projects will be kept/);
});

const test = require('node:test');
const assert = require('node:assert/strict');
const fs = require('node:fs');
const path = require('node:path');
const vm = require('node:vm');

const read = file => fs.readFileSync(path.join(__dirname, '..', file), 'utf8');
const app = read('app.js');
const wrapperPath = path.join(__dirname, '..', 'vendor/ffmpeg/ffmpeg.js');
const workerPath = path.join(__dirname, '..', 'vendor/ffmpeg/814.ffmpeg.js');
const ffmpegVendorMissing = !fs.existsSync(wrapperPath) || !fs.existsSync(workerPath);
const workflow = read('.github/workflows/built.yml');

test('native export reads FFmpeg output in bounded worker chunks', () => {
  assert.ok(app.includes('async function saveFFmpegOutput(engine, outputPath)'));
  assert.ok(app.includes('engine.readFileChunk(outputPath, offset, CHUNK_BYTES)'));
  assert.ok(app.includes('nativeStore.appendStreamingChunk({ data: btoa(binary) })'));
  const nativePath = app.slice(app.indexOf('async function saveFFmpegOutput'), app.indexOf('// Native Android builds register'))
    .split('if (!nativeStore')[0];
  assert.ok(!nativePath.includes('readFile(outputPath)'));
});

test('FFmpeg wrapper and worker expose the bounded range-read protocol', { skip: ffmpegVendorMissing ? 'Vendor FFmpeg bundle is omitted from this source snapshot' : false }, () => {
  const wrapper = fs.readFileSync(wrapperPath, 'utf8');
  const worker = fs.readFileSync(workerPath, 'utf8');
  assert.ok(wrapper.includes('readFileChunk='));
  assert.ok(worker.includes('case t.READ_FILE_CHUNK'));
});

test('Android MediaStore streaming export publishes only after all chunks are written', () => {
  const begin = workflow.indexOf('public synchronized void beginStreamingSave');
  const append = workflow.indexOf('public synchronized void appendStreamingChunk');
  const finish = workflow.indexOf('public synchronized void finishStreamingSave');
  assert.ok(begin >= 0 && append > begin && finish > append);
  const section = workflow.slice(begin, workflow.indexOf('public void saveVideo(PluginCall call)', finish));
  assert.ok(section.includes('MediaStore.Video.Media.IS_PENDING, 1'));
  assert.ok(section.includes('MediaStore.Video.Media.IS_PENDING, 0'));
  assert.ok(section.includes('abortStreamingSaveInternal()'));
  assert.ok(section.includes('4 * 1024 * 1024'));
});

test('build workflow uses read-only contents permission and isolates release write permission', () => {
  assert.ok(workflow.includes('permissions:\n  contents: read'));
  assert.ok(workflow.includes('  build:\n    permissions:\n      contents: read'));
  assert.ok(workflow.includes('  publish-release:\n'));
  assert.ok(workflow.includes('      contents: write'));
  assert.ok(workflow.includes('actions/download-artifact@v4'));
});

test('FFmpeg worker range reads return exact offsets and end-of-file metadata', { skip: !fs.existsSync(workerPath) ? 'Vendor FFmpeg worker is omitted from this source snapshot' : false }, async () => {
  const worker = fs.readFileSync(workerPath, 'utf8');
  const bytes = new TextEncoder().encode('abcdefghij');
  const messages = [];
  const fakeFS = {
    stat: () => ({ size: bytes.length }),
    open: filePath => ({ path: filePath }),
    read: (stream, target, targetOffset, length, position) => {
      const count = Math.max(0, Math.min(length, bytes.length - position));
      target.set(bytes.subarray(position, position + count), targetOffset);
      return count;
    },
    close: () => {}
  };
  const self = {
    postMessage: message => messages.push(message),
    createFFmpegCore: async () => ({
      FS: fakeFS,
      setLogger: () => {},
      setProgress: () => {}
    })
  };
  const context = {
    self,
    importScripts: () => {},
    btoa: value => Buffer.from(value, 'binary').toString('base64'),
    Uint8Array, Math, Number, Error, Promise, JSON
  };
  vm.runInNewContext(worker, context);
  await self.onmessage({ data: { id: 1, type: 'LOAD', data: { coreURL: 'core.js', wasmURL: 'core.wasm' } } });
  await self.onmessage({ data: { id: 2, type: 'READ_FILE_CHUNK', data: { path: 'final.mp4', offset: 5, length: 4 } } });
  await self.onmessage({ data: { id: 3, type: 'READ_FILE_CHUNK', data: { path: 'final.mp4', offset: 8, length: 4 } } });
  const first = messages.find(message => message.id === 2).data;
  const last = messages.find(message => message.id === 3).data;
  assert.equal(new TextDecoder().decode(first.data), 'fghi');
  assert.equal(first.bytesRead, 4);
  assert.equal(first.fileSize, 10);
  assert.equal(first.eof, false);
  assert.equal(new TextDecoder().decode(last.data), 'ij');
  assert.equal(last.bytesRead, 2);
  assert.equal(last.eof, true);
});

'use strict';
const test = require('node:test');
const assert = require('node:assert/strict');
const fs = require('node:fs');
const path = require('node:path');
const crypto = require('node:crypto');

const root = path.join(__dirname, '..');
const expected = {
  'fabric.min.js': 'uwRC1ppNRnMyD2ibxuFsBaDj8WzPv1UfOU2Qp+fPx3I=',
  'anime.min.js': 'tc4b48P1MPGS4PJXHRlChGCW1mEZy62jS/3JEsSHPzU=',
  'dexie.min.js': '2JhqFVK0mn2MsIWpLGaYWuvzuzO2Stb3uu0kYdm2R/8='
};

const missingVendorFiles = Object.keys(expected).filter(name => !fs.existsSync(path.join(root, 'vendor', 'media-libraries', name)));

test('vendored editor libraries match their pinned SHA-256 SRI digests', { skip: missingVendorFiles.length ? 'Vendor editor libraries are omitted from this source snapshot: ' + missingVendorFiles.join(', ') : false }, () => {
  for (const [name, digest] of Object.entries(expected)) {
    const file = path.join(root, 'vendor', 'media-libraries', name);
    const actual = crypto.createHash('sha256').update(fs.readFileSync(file)).digest('base64');
    assert.equal(actual, digest, name + ' integrity mismatch');
  }
});
